### [1] [Efficient LLM Serving for Agentic Workflows: A Data Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16104)
*Noppanat Wadlom,Junyi Shen,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Helium是一个面向工作流的LLM服务框架，将智能体工作流建模为查询计划，通过主动缓存和缓存感知调度优化跨调用依赖，相比现有系统实现最高1.56倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统（如vLLM）专注于优化单个推理调用，忽视了智能体工作流中跨调用依赖关系，导致大量冗余计算和效率低下。

Method: 从数据系统视角重新思考LLM服务，将智能体工作流建模为查询计划，将LLM调用视为一等操作符，集成主动缓存和缓存感知调度技术，最大化提示、KV状态和工作流间的重用。

Result: Helium在各种工作负载上相比最先进的智能体服务系统实现了最高1.56倍的加速，证明了跨工作流端到端优化对于可扩展高效LLM智能体的重要性。

Conclusion: 智能体工作流需要专门的端到端优化框架，Helium通过将经典查询优化原则与LLM服务相结合，为可扩展高效的LLM智能体系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are composed of sequences of interdependent Large Language Model (LLM) calls, and they have become a dominant workload in modern AI systems. These workflows exhibit extensive redundancy from overlapping prompts and intermediate results due to speculative and parallel exploration. Existing LLM serving systems, such as vLLM, focus on optimizing individual inference calls and overlook cross-call dependencies, leading to significant inefficiencies. This paper rethinks LLM and agent serving from a data systems perspective and introduces Helium, a workflow-aware serving framework that models agentic workloads as query plans and treats LLM invocations as first-class operators. Helium integrates proactive caching and cache-aware scheduling to maximize reuse across prompts, KV states, and workflows. Through these techniques, Helium bridges classic query optimization principles with LLM serving, achieving up to 1.56x speedup over state-of-the-art agent serving systems on various workloads. Our results demonstrate that end-to-end optimization across workflows is essential for scalable and efficient LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [2] [CoMAI: A Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Robust and Equitable Interview Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16215)
*Gengxin Sun,Ruihao Yu,Liangyi Yin,Yunqi Yang,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CoMAI是一个用于AI驱动面试评估的多智能体框架，通过模块化任务分解架构和集中式有限状态机协调，相比单智能体系统提供更鲁棒、公平和可解释的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI驱动面试评估中的鲁棒性和公平性挑战，克服基于大语言模型的单体单智能体系统在安全性、主观偏见和适应性方面的局限性。

Method: 采用模块化任务分解架构，通过集中式有限状态机协调四个专门智能体：问题生成、安全、评分和总结智能体，实现多层安全防御、多维评估和基于评分标准的结构化评分。

Result: 实验结果显示CoMAI达到90.47%准确率、83.33%召回率和84.41%候选人满意度，证明了其在AI驱动面试评估中的有效性。

Conclusion: CoMAI为AI驱动面试评估提供了一个鲁棒、公平和可解释的范式，通过多智能体协作架构显著提升了评估系统的性能和质量。

Abstract: Ensuring robust and fair interview assessment remains a key challenge in AI-driven evaluation. This paper presents CoMAI, a general-purpose multi-agent interview framework designed for diverse assessment scenarios. In contrast to monolithic single-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs), CoMAI employs a modular task-decomposition architecture coordinated through a centralized finite-state machine. The system comprises four agents specialized in question generation, security, scoring, and summarization. These agents work collaboratively to provide multi-layered security defenses against prompt injection, support multidimensional evaluation with adaptive difficulty adjustment, and enable rubric-based structured scoring that reduces subjective bias. Experimental results demonstrate that CoMAI achieved 90.47% accuracy, 83.33% recall, and 84.41% candidate satisfaction. These results highlight CoMAI as a robust, fair, and interpretable paradigm for AI-driven interview assessment.

</details>

### [3] [Simulation Distillation: Pretraining World Models in Simulation for Rapid Real-World Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15759)
*Jacob Levy,Tyler Westenbroek,Kevin Huang,Fernando Palafox,Patrick Yin,Shayegan Omidshafiei,Dong-Ki Kim,Abhishek Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimDist：一种从模拟器蒸馏结构先验到潜在世界模型的仿真到现实框架，通过在线规划和监督动力学微调实现快速现实世界适应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决仿真到现实转移的核心挑战，现有方法在真实机器人低数据环境下难以进行探索和长时域信用分配

Method: 从模拟器蒸馏结构先验到潜在世界模型，直接转移奖励和价值模型，通过在线规划和监督动力学微调实现快速适应

Result: 在精确操作和四足机器人运动任务中，SimDist在数据效率、稳定性和最终性能上显著优于现有方法

Conclusion: SimDist通过避免部署期间的价值学习和长时域信用分配，将现实适应简化为短时域系统辨识，实现了快速稳定的性能提升

Abstract: Simulation-to-real transfer remains a central challenge in robotics, as mismatches between simulated and real-world dynamics often lead to failures. While reinforcement learning offers a principled mechanism for adaptation, existing sim-to-real finetuning methods struggle with exploration and long-horizon credit assignment in the low-data regimes typical of real-world robotics. We introduce Simulation Distillation (SimDist), a sim-to-real framework that distills structural priors from a simulator into a latent world model and enables rapid real-world adaptation via online planning and supervised dynamics finetuning. By transferring reward and value models directly from simulation, SimDist provides dense planning signals from raw perception without requiring value learning during deployment. As a result, real-world adaptation reduces to short-horizon system identification, avoiding long-horizon credit assignment and enabling fast, stable improvement. Across precise manipulation and quadruped locomotion tasks, SimDist substantially outperforms prior methods in data efficiency, stability, and final performance. Project website and code: https://sim-dist.github.io/

</details>


### [4] [Early-Terminable Energy-Safe Iterative Coupling for Parallel Simulation of Port-Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16424)
*Qi Wei,Jianfeng Tao,Hongyu Nie,Wangtao Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Douglas-Rachford分裂的波域迭代耦合方法，确保大规模机器人系统并行仿真中的能量安全性，支持有限迭代预算下的离散无源性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分区时间步进方法在有限迭代耦合时可能违反功率一致性，注入虚假能量，即使各子系统本身是无源的。需要一种能保证能量安全、支持早期终止的迭代耦合方案。

Method: 在散射（波）坐标中嵌入Douglas-Rachford分裂方案，将无损互连约束表示为波域中的正交约束，每个子系统贡献由其一步积分器导出的离散时间散射端口映射。

Result: 在子系统时间步满足离散无源性条件和温和阻抗调谐条件下，证明了耦合宏步的离散无源性，剩余失配由显式残差捕获。随着内迭代预算增加，分区更新收敛于相同积分器导出的整体离散时间更新。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了原理性的自适应精度-计算权衡，支持变化计算预算下的能量一致实时并行仿真，耦合振荡器基准实验验证了无源性证书和RMS状态误差随迭代预算增加而单调衰减。

Abstract: Parallel simulation and control of large-scale robotic systems often rely on partitioned time stepping, yet finite-iteration coupling can inject spurious energy by violating power consistency--even when each subsystem is passive. This letter proposes a novel energy-safe, early-terminable iterative coupling for port-Hamiltonian subsystems by embedding a Douglas--Rachford (DR) splitting scheme in scattering (wave) coordinates. The lossless interconnection is enforced as an orthogonal constraint in the wave domain, while each subsystem contributes a discrete-time scattering port map induced by its one-step integrator. Under a discrete passivity condition on the subsystem time steps and a mild impedance-tuning condition, we prove an augmented-storage inequality certifying discrete passivity of the coupled macro-step for any finite inner-iteration budget, with the remaining mismatch captured by an explicit residual. As the inner budget increases, the partitioned update converges to the monolithic discrete-time update induced by the same integrators, yielding a principled, adaptive accuracy--compute trade-off, supporting energy-consistent real-time parallel simulation under varying computational budgets. Experiments on a coupled-oscillator benchmark validate the passivity certificates at numerical roundoff (on the order of 10e-14 in double precision) and show that the reported RMS state error decays monotonically with increasing inner-iteration budgets, consistent with the hard-coupling limit.

</details>


### [5] [CorrectionPlanner: Self-Correction Planner with Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15771)
*Yihong Guo,Dongqiangzi Ye,Sijia Chen,Anqi Liu,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CorrectionPlanner：一种具有自校正能力的自回归规划器，通过"提出-评估-校正"循环将规划建模为运动令牌生成，显著降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的自动驾驶规划器缺乏显式的自校正能力：一旦提出不安全动作，没有机制进行校正。这限制了规划的安全性。

Method: 提出CorrectionPlanner，采用自回归规划器，在"提出-评估-校正"循环中生成运动令牌。包含碰撞批评器预测短期碰撞风险，保留不安全运动令牌序列作为自校正轨迹，基于此生成下一个运动令牌，重复直到安全或满足安全标准。

Result: 在Waymax上碰撞率降低超过20%，在nuPlan上达到最先进的规划分数。自校正轨迹类似于语言模型中的推理轨迹，有效提升规划安全性。

Conclusion: CorrectionPlanner通过显式自校正机制显著提升自动驾驶规划安全性，将规划建模为运动令牌生成并引入"提出-评估-校正"循环，为学习型规划器提供了有效的安全增强框架。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires safe planning, but most learning-based planners lack explicit self-correction ability: once an unsafe action is proposed, there is no mechanism to correct it. Thus, we propose CorrectionPlanner, an autoregressive planner with self-correction that models planning as motion-token generation within a propose, evaluate, and correct loop. At each planning step, the policy proposes an action, namely a motion token, and a learned collision critic predicts whether it will induce a collision within a short horizon. If the critic predicts a collision, we retain the sequence of historical unsafe motion tokens as a self-correction trace, generate the next motion token conditioned on it, and repeat this process until a safe motion token is proposed or the safety criterion is met. This self-correction trace, consisting of all unsafe motion tokens, represents the planner's correction process in motion-token space, analogous to a reasoning trace in language models. We train the planner with imitation learning followed by model-based reinforcement learning using rollouts from a pretrained world model that realistically models agents' reactive behaviors. Closed-loop evaluations show that CorrectionPlanner reduces collision rate by over 20% on Waymax and achieves state-of-the-art planning scores on nuPlan.

</details>


### [6] [Emergent Dexterity via Diverse Resets and Large-Scale Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15789)
*Patrick Yin,Tyler Westenbroek,Zhengyu Zhang,Joshua Tran,Ignacio Dagnino,Eeshani Shilamkar,Numfor Mbiziwo-Tiapo,Simran Bagaria,Xinlei Liu,Galen Mullins,Andrey Kolobov,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为OmniReset的简单可扩展框架，通过程序化生成模拟器重置来系统性地暴露RL算法于多样化的机器人-物体交互，从而解决灵巧操作任务，无需针对每个任务进行大量工程调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大规模并行物理模拟的强化学习方法仍然脆弱且任务特定，需要大量针对每个任务的工程来设计奖励、课程和演示。即使如此，它们通常在长时程、接触丰富的操作任务上失败，且无法有效利用计算资源，因为性能在训练重复访问相同狭窄状态空间区域时迅速饱和。

Method: OmniReset框架通过程序化生成模拟器重置，系统性地将RL算法暴露于构成灵巧操作基础的多样化机器人-物体交互中。该方法使用单一奖励函数、固定算法超参数、无课程和无人类演示，将额外计算直接转化为更广泛的行为覆盖和持续的性能提升。

Result: OmniReset能够优雅地扩展到超出现有方法能力的长时程灵巧操作任务，并能学习到比基线方法在更广泛初始条件下更鲁棒的策略。此外，该方法蒸馏出的视觉运动策略在零样本转移到现实世界时显示出鲁棒的重试行为和显著更高的成功率。

Conclusion: OmniReset通过系统性地利用模拟器重置来简化长时程探索，为灵巧操作任务提供了一个简单、可扩展的强化学习框架，能够有效利用计算资源实现持续性能提升，并成功转移到现实世界。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in massively parallel physics simulations has driven major progress in sim-to-real robot learning. However, current approaches remain brittle and task-specific, relying on extensive per-task engineering to design rewards, curricula, and demonstrations. Even with this engineering, they often fail on long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation tasks and do not meaningfully scale with compute, as performance quickly saturates when training revisits the same narrow regions of state space. We introduce \Method, a simple and scalable framework that enables on-policy reinforcement learning to robustly solve a broad class of dexterous manipulation tasks using a single reward function, fixed algorithm hyperparameters, no curricula, and no human demonstrations. Our key insight is that long-horizon exploration can be dramatically simplified by using simulator resets to systematically expose the RL algorithm to the diverse set of robot-object interactions which underlie dexterous manipulation. \Method\ programmatically generates such resets with minimal human input, converting additional compute directly into broader behavioral coverage and continued performance gains. We show that \Method\ gracefully scales to long-horizon dexterous manipulation tasks beyond the capabilities of existing approaches and is able to learn robust policies over significantly wider ranges of initial conditions than baselines. Finally, we distill \Method \ into visuomotor policies which display robust retrying behavior and substantially higher success rates than baselines when transferred to the real world zero-shot. Project webpage: https://omnireset.github.io

</details>


### [7] [Robust Dynamic Object Detection in Cluttered Indoor Scenes via Learned Spatiotemporal Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15826)
*Juan Rached,Yixuan Jia,Kota Kondo,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用LiDAR的动态物体检测框架，通过融合时序占据网格的运动分割与学习的鸟瞰图动态先验，在杂乱环境中显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中可靠检测动态物体是自主导航的关键挑战。传统几何LiDAR方法在动态物体靠近静态结构或部分观测时会漏检；视觉增强方法受限于闭集检测器和相机视场约束，对新颖障碍物和视场外事件鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出纯LiDAR框架，融合时序占据网格的运动分割与学习的鸟瞰图动态先验。融合模块优先使用3D检测结果，同时利用学习的动态网格恢复因邻近性导致的假阴性检测。

Result: 使用运动捕捉地面真值实验表明，在高度杂乱环境中，该方法比最先进方法召回率提高28.67%，F1分数提高18.50%，同时保持相当的精度和位置误差。

Conclusion: 该LiDAR-only框架通过融合运动分割与学习动态先验，有效解决了动态物体在杂乱环境中的检测问题，显著提升了检测性能，为自主导航提供了更可靠的动态障碍物感知能力。

Abstract: Reliable dynamic object detection in cluttered environments remains a critical challenge for autonomous navigation. Purely geometric LiDAR pipelines that rely on clustering and heuristic filtering can miss dynamic obstacles when they move in close proximity to static structure or are only partially observed. Vision-augmented approaches can provide additional semantic cues, but are often limited by closed-set detectors and camera field-of-view constraints, reducing robustness to novel obstacles and out-of-frustum events. In this work, we present a LiDAR-only framework that fuses temporal occupancy-grid-based motion segmentation with a learned bird's-eye-view (BEV) dynamic prior. A fusion module prioritizes 3D detections when available, while using the learned dynamic grid to recover detections that would otherwise be lost due to proximity-induced false negatives. Experiments with motion-capture ground truth show our method achieves 28.67% higher recall and 18.50% higher F1 score than the state-of-the-art in substantially cluttered environments while maintaining comparable precision and position error.

</details>


### [8] [When Rolling Gets Weird: A Curved-Link Tensegrity Robot for Non-Intuitive Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16503)
*Lauren Ervin,Harish Bezawada,Vishesh Vikas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种采用半圆形弯曲连杆的张力结构机器人TeXploR2，通过内部移动质量块实现动态滚动，在滚动效率和稳定性之间取得平衡，适用于空间探索环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直线连杆张力结构机器人移动性有限且速度慢，球形机器人滚动效率高但稳定性不足，难以适应空间探索中的非结构化地形。需要一种既能高效滚动又具备可控稳定性的机器人设计。

Method: 基于现有几何静态建模框架，设计了改进型TeXploR2机器人，采用半圆形弯曲连杆，内部移动质量块沿连杆瞬时滚动，动态改变与地面的两个接触点。通过准静态、分段连续运动序列仿真分析，并制作了无缆原型机进行实验验证。

Result: 仿真揭示了惯性坐标系和机体坐标系之间的位置位移新见解，识别了非直观的滚动行为。实验验证了动态运动能力，初步冲击测试显示了张力结构固有的减震能力和适应性。

Conclusion: 弯曲连杆张力结构机器人在滚动效率和稳定性之间取得了良好平衡。未来工作将完善动态模型并进行真实环境验证，进一步改进原型机以增加弯曲连杆和地面接触点，提高可控性。

Abstract: Conventional mobile tensegrity robots constructed with straight links offer mobility at the cost of locomotion speed. While spherical robots provide highly effective rolling behavior, they often lack the stability required for navigating unstructured terrain common in many space exploration environments. This research presents a solution with a semi-circular, curved-link tensegrity robot that strikes a balance between efficient rolling locomotion and controlled stability, enabled by discontinuities present at the arc endpoints. Building upon an existing geometric static modeling framework [1], this work presents the system design of an improved Tensegrity eXploratory Robot 2 (TeXploR2). Internal shifting masses instantaneously roll along each curved-link, dynamically altering the two points of contact with the ground plane. Simulations of quasistatic, piecewise continuous locomotion sequences reveal new insights into the positional displacement between inertial and body frames. Non-intuitive rolling behaviors are identified and experimentally validated using a tetherless prototype, demonstrating successful dynamic locomotion. A preliminary impact test highlights the tensegrity structure's inherent shock absorption capabilities and conformability. Future work will focus on finalizing a dynamic model that is experimentally validated with extended testing in real-world environments as well as further refinement of the prototype to incorporate additional curved-links and subsequent ground contact points for increased controllability.

</details>


### [9] [Gaze-Aware Task Progression Detection Framework for Human-Robot Interaction Using RGB Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15951)
*Linlin Cheng,Koen Hindriks,Artem V. Belopolsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于RGB摄像头的无标定注视检测框架，用于人机交互中任务进度监测，通过注视模式识别任务完成信号


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视检测方法依赖专业眼动仪，限制了日常部署；基于外观的注视估计方法虽使用普通RGB摄像头，但在人机交互中的实用性尚未充分探索

Method: 使用机器人内置单目RGB摄像头（640x480分辨率）和先进注视估计技术，通过三个关注区域（平板、机器人面部、其他）监测用户自然行为模式，系统化参数优化平衡检测精度和交互延迟

Result: 任务完成检测准确率达77.6%；相比按钮交互，系统响应延迟略高但保留信息记忆，显著提升舒适度、社交存在感和感知自然性；多数参与者未意识到使用眼动引导交互

Conclusion: 证明了基于RGB摄像头的低成本、直观注视人机交互的可行性，能够实现自然且吸引人的交互体验

Abstract: In human-robot interaction (HRI), detecting a human's gaze helps robots interpret user attention and intent. However, most gaze detection approaches rely on specialized eye-tracking hardware, limiting deployment in everyday settings. Appearance-based gaze estimation methods remove this dependency by using standard RGB cameras, but their practicality in HRI remains underexplored. We present a calibration-free framework for detecting task progression when information is conveyed via integrated display interfaces. The framework uses only the robot's built-in monocular RGB camera (640x480 resolution) and state-of-the-art gaze estimation to monitor attention patterns. It leverages natural behavior, where users shift focus from task interfaces to the robot's face to signal task completion, formalized through three Areas of Interest (AOI): tablet, robot face, and elsewhere. Systematic parameter optimization identifies configurations that balance detection accuracy and interaction latency. We validate our framework in a "First Day at Work" scenario, comparing it to button-based interaction. Results show a task completion detection accuracy of 77.6%. Compared to button-based interaction, the proposed system exhibits slightly higher response latency but preserves information retention and significantly improves comfort, social presence, and perceived naturalness. Notably, most participants reported that they did not consciously use eye movements to guide the interaction, underscoring the intuitive role of gaze as a communicative cue. This work demonstrates the feasibility of intuitive, low-cost, RGB-only gaze-based HRI for natural and engaging interactions.

</details>


### [10] [ExpertGen: Scalable Sim-to-Real Expert Policy Learning from Imperfect Behavior Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15956)
*Zifan Xu,Ran Gong,Maria Vittoria Minniti,Ahmet Salih Gundogdu,Eric Rosen,Kausik Sivakumar,Riedana Yan,Zixing Wang,Di Deng,Peter Stone,Xiaohan Zhang,Karl Schmeckpeper*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExpertGen框架通过结合扩散策略和强化学习，在仿真中自动化学习专家策略，实现可扩展的仿真到现实迁移，无需奖励工程即可获得高质量策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取大规模高质量机器人数据成本高昂，人类演示（如遥操作）作为专家行为标准来源在现实世界中难以大规模获取，需要自动化方法在仿真中学习可泛化且鲁棒的策略。

Method: 1. 使用不完美演示（可由大语言模型合成或人类提供）训练扩散策略作为行为先验；2. 通过强化学习优化扩散模型的初始噪声，引导该先验实现高任务成功率，同时保持原始策略冻结；3. 冻结的扩散策略将探索限制在安全、类人行为流形内，仅需稀疏奖励即可有效学习。

Result: 在工业装配任务中达到90.5%总体成功率，在长时域操作任务中达到85%总体成功率，优于所有基线方法；策略展现出灵巧控制能力，在不同初始配置和失败状态下保持鲁棒；通过DAgger将状态策略蒸馏为视觉运动策略，成功部署到真实机器人硬件。

Conclusion: ExpertGen框架能够可靠地生成高质量专家策略，无需奖励工程，通过仿真到现实迁移实现可扩展的机器人策略学习，为大规模获取机器人数据提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Learning generalizable and robust behavior cloning policies requires large volumes of high-quality robotics data. While human demonstrations (e.g., through teleoperation) serve as the standard source for expert behaviors, acquiring such data at scale in the real world is prohibitively expensive. This paper introduces ExpertGen, a framework that automates expert policy learning in simulation to enable scalable sim-to-real transfer. ExpertGen first initializes a behavior prior using a diffusion policy trained on imperfect demonstrations, which may be synthesized by large language models or provided by humans. Reinforcement learning is then used to steer this prior toward high task success by optimizing the diffusion model's initial noise while keep original policy frozen. By keeping the pretrained diffusion policy frozen, ExpertGen regularizes exploration to remain within safe, human-like behavior manifolds, while also enabling effective learning with only sparse rewards. Empirical evaluations on challenging manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that ExpertGen reliably produces high-quality expert policies with no reward engineering. On industrial assembly tasks, ExpertGen achieves a 90.5% overall success rate, while on long-horizon manipulation tasks it attains 85% overall success, outperforming all baseline methods. The resulting policies exhibit dexterous control and remain robust across diverse initial configurations and failure states. To validate sim-to-real transfer, the learned state-based expert policies are further distilled into visuomotor policies via DAgger and successfully deployed on real robotic hardware.

</details>


### [11] [Routing and Control for Marine Oil-Spill Cleanup with a Boom-Towing Vessel Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16626)
*Snir Carmeli,Adir Morgan,Kiril Solovey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个多机器人框架，用于协调自主水面车辆（ASV）双机编队进行大规模溢油围控和清理，通过混合优化算法解决多溢油点的风险加权最小延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法主要针对孤立油膜和单个ASV编队，缺乏协调大规模机器人舰队应对多个溢油点的可扩展方法，而现实溢油事故往往涉及多个溢油点。

Method: 1) 将多溢油响应建模为风险加权最小延迟问题；2) 开发混合优化方法，结合混合整数线性规划和定制启发式算法；3) 设计两种拖曳围油栏ASV编队的跟踪控制器（反馈线性化控制器和PID控制器）。

Result: 混合优化方法能在商用硬件上数分钟内为数十个溢油点生成接近最优的路径规划；两种控制器在耦合船舶-围油栏动力学仿真中均表现出精确的路径跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模溢油灾害提供了可扩展、全面的快速风险感知多机器人响应方案，实现了从算法规划到物理执行的完整系统。

Abstract: Marine oil spills damage ecosystems, contaminate coastlines, and disrupt food webs, while imposing substantial economic losses on fisheries and coastal communities. Prior work has demonstrated the feasibility of containing and cleaning individual spills using a duo of autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs) equipped with a towed boom and skimmers. However, existing algorithmic approaches primarily address isolated slicks and individual ASV duos, lacking scalable methods for coordinating large robotic fleets across multiple spills representative of realistic oil-spill incidents. In this work, we propose an integrated multi-robot framework for coordinated oil-spill confinement and cleanup using autonomous ASV duos. We formulate multi-spill response as a risk-weighted minimum-latency problem, where spill-specific risk factors and service times jointly determine cumulative environmental damage. To solve this problem, we develop a hybrid optimization approach combining mixed-integer linear programming, and a tailored warm-start heuristic, enabling near-optimal routing plans for scenarios with tens of spills within minutes on commodity hardware. For physical execution, we design and analyze two tracking controllers for boom-towing ASV duos: a feedback-linearization controller with proven asymptotic stability, and a baseline PID controller. Simulation results under coupled vessel-boom dynamics demonstrate accurate path tracking for both controllers. Together, these components provide a scalable, holistic framework for rapid, risk-aware multi-robot response to large-scale oil spill disasters.

</details>


### [12] [Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16013)
*Gerhard Yu,Fuyuki Ishikawa,Oluwafemi Odu,Alvine Boaye Belle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAISE安全案例设计方法，针对VLA自动驾驶系统的新安全挑战，通过扩展HARA和定制化模式构建证据驱动的安全论证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA自动驾驶系统结合视觉、语言和动作，虽能实现更灵活、适应性的驾驶行为，但引入自然语言输入可能产生新型危险行为，需要专门的安全保障方法来建立信任

Method: 提出RAISE安全案例设计方法，包括：为指令驱动系统定制新模式、扩展HARA详细描述安全场景及其结果、设计VLA系统安全案例构建技术

Result: 通过SimLingo案例研究展示了RAISE方法如何为这类新兴自动驾驶系统构建严谨、基于证据的安全声明

Conclusion: RAISE方法为VLA自动驾驶系统提供了专门的安全案例设计框架，有助于解决其独特的安全挑战并建立操作信任

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving while understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. Such as the addition of natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop, which may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [13] [Geometry-Aligned LLM Fine-Tuning for Sequential Narrow-Opening Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16028)
*Al Jaber Mahmud,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出几何对齐的LLM微调框架，用于生成通过多个连续狭窄开口的刚体运动规划，通过双层训练确保几何可行性和跨开口协调性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决通过多个连续狭窄开口的刚体运动规划问题，该问题需要长时域几何推理，因为通过早期开口的配置会约束后续开口的可达配置集

Method: 几何对齐的LLM微调框架：1) 基于人类演示的失败驱动LoRA监督微调，包含结构化失败反馈；2) 使用带几何验证的组相对策略优化(GRPO)精炼LoRA适配器，通过模型规划器稠密化路径点序列并用几何奖励评分

Result: 在分布内和分布外环境中均获得最高成功率，定性地展示了长时域几何推理能力，能够选择便于进入后续开口的出口姿态

Conclusion: 提出的几何对齐LLM微调框架有效解决了通过多个狭窄开口的刚体运动规划问题，实现了长时域几何推理和跨开口协调

Abstract: We study rigid-body motion planning through multiple sequential narrow openings, which requires long-horizon geometric reasoning because the configuration used to traverse an early opening constrains the set of reachable configurations for subsequent ones. To achieve this, we propose a geometry-aligned large language model (LLM) fine-tuning framework that generates fixed-length, machine-readable waypoint sequences that are both geometrically feasible and coordinated across openings. Our approach uses a bi-level training pipeline. First, we perform failure-driven LoRA supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on human demonstrations, which incorporates structured failure feedback to teach the model common failure modes and enforce the output format. Second, we refine the same LoRA adapters using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with geometric verification: each sampled waypoint sequence is densified by a model-based planner and scored with a deterministic geometry-derived reward to achieve continuous-motion feasibility. To validate the effectiveness of our proposed method, we provide both quantitative and qualitative results from simulations. Our method achieves the highest success rate in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution environments and qualitatively exhibits long-horizon geometric reasoning by selecting exit poses that facilitate entry into subsequent openings.

</details>


### [14] [Compact Optical Single-axis Joint Torque Sensor Using Redundant Photo-Reflectors and Quadratic-Programming Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16040)
*Hyun-Bin Kim,Byeong-Il Ham,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于光反射器的非接触式关节扭矩传感器，用于精确的关节级扭矩控制和安全的物理交互。该传感器通过光学测量弹性结构的微变形，采用四方向冗余光反射器阵列提高灵敏度和信噪比，并开发了基于二次规划的标定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于电流传感器的协作机器人扭矩估计存在低扭矩精度差的问题，主要由于齿轮箱静摩擦/摩擦以及电流-扭矩非线性，特别是在静态条件下。需要一种能够精确测量低扭矩的传感器来实现精确的关节级扭矩控制和安全的物理交互。

Method: 1. 设计非接触式光反射器扭矩传感器，通过光学测量弹性结构的微变形；2. 采用四方向冗余光反射器阵列提高灵敏度和信噪比；3. 开发基于二次规划的标定方法，利用冗余性抑制噪声并提高分辨率；4. 实现紧凑型传感器设计（96mm直径，12mm厚度）；5. 通过温度室表征和有理拟合补偿来减轻MCU自发热和电机热量引起的零漂。

Result: 1. 传感器最大误差为0.083%FS，z轴扭矩测量的RMS误差为0.0266 Nm；2. 提出的标定方法在1kHz无滤波情况下达到0.0224 Nm的3σ分辨率，比最小二乘基线提高2.14倍；3. 温度补偿有效减轻了零漂；4. 通过扭矩控制和导纳控制验证，相对于基于电流传感器的控制，在低扭矩跟踪和扰动鲁棒性方面有显著改进。

Conclusion: 提出的非接触式光反射器关节扭矩传感器能够精确测量低扭矩，解决了基于电流传感器的协作机器人在低扭矩精度方面的局限性。冗余光反射器阵列和二次规划标定方法显著提高了测量精度和分辨率，紧凑的设计使其适用于实际机器人应用，为实现精确的关节级扭矩控制和安全的物理交互提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This study proposes a non-contact photo-reflector-based joint torque sensor for precise joint-level torque control and safe physical interaction. Current-sensor-based torque estimation in many collaborative robots suffers from poor low-torque accuracy due to gearbox stiction/friction and current-torque nonlinearity, especially near static conditions. The proposed sensor optically measures micro-deformation of an elastic structure and employs a redundant array of photo-reflectors arranged in four directions to improve sensitivity and signal-to-noise ratio. We further present a quadratic-programming-based calibration method that exploits redundancy to suppress noise and enhance resolution compared to least-squares calibration. The sensor is implemented in a compact form factor (96 mm diameter, 12 mm thickness). Experiments demonstrate a maximum error of 0.083%FS and an RMS error of 0.0266 Nm for z-axis torque measurement. Calibration tests show that the proposed calibration achieves a 3 sigma resolution of 0.0224 Nm at 1 kHz without filtering, corresponding to a 2.14 times improvement over the least-squares baseline. Temperature chamber characterization and rational fitting based compensation mitigate zero drift induced by MCU self heating and motor heat. Motor-level validation via torque control and admittance control confirms improved low torque tracking and disturbance robustness relative to current-sensor-based control.

</details>


### [15] [The Era of End-to-End Autonomy: Transitioning from Rule-Based Driving to Large Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16050)
*Eduardo Nebot,Julie Stephany Berrio Perez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文分析了自动驾驶从模块化规则系统向端到端学习系统的演进，重点考察了特斯拉FSD V12/V14、Rivian统一智能平台、NVIDIA Cosmos等大型驾驶模型，探讨了监督式端到端驾驶作为新兴产品类别的发展趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶领域正在经历从基于规则的模块化架构向端到端学习系统的根本性转变，需要系统性地分析这一技术演进路径、关键系统架构、部署策略及其对行业的影响。

Method: 通过追踪从传统感知-规划-控制架构到大型驾驶模型的演进历程，分析特斯拉FSD V12/V14、Rivian统一智能平台、NVIDIA Cosmos等代表性系统的架构设计、部署策略和安全考量。

Result: 端到端学习系统能够处理真实驾驶场景的长尾分布，监督式端到端驾驶（FSD Supervised/L2++）成为2026年后多家制造商计划部署的主流商业策略，早期运营证据表明其在复杂环境中能完成大部分动态驾驶任务。

Conclusion: 端到端学习正在成为自动驾驶的主导范式，监督式端到端驾驶系统将驾驶员角色转变为安全监督者，类似的架构进展可能扩展到人形机器人等其他具身AI系统。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is undergoing a shift from modular rule based pipelines toward end to end (E2E) learning systems. This paper examines this transition by tracing the evolution from classical sense perceive plan control architectures to large driving models (LDMs) capable of mapping raw sensor input directly to driving actions. We analyze recent developments including Tesla's Full Self Driving (FSD) V12 V14, Rivian's Unified Intelligence platform, NVIDIA Cosmos, and emerging commercial robotaxi deployments, focusing on architectural design, deployment strategies, safety considerations and industry implications. A key emerging product category is supervised E2E driving, often referred to as FSD (Supervised) or L2 plus plus, which several manufacturers plan to deploy from 2026 onwards. These systems can perform most of the Dynamic Driving Task (DDT) in complex environments while requiring human supervision, shifting the driver's role to safety oversight. Early operational evidence suggests E2E learning handles the long tail distribution of real world driving scenarios and is becoming a dominant commercial strategy. We also discuss how similar architectural advances may extend beyond autonomous vehicles (AV) to other embodied AI systems, including humanoid robotics.

</details>


### [16] [Ultrafast Sampling-based Kinodynamic Planning via Differential Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16059)
*Thai Duong,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary Kingston,Wil Thomason,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AkinoPDF：一种快速并行化采样运动规划技术，利用微分平坦性为机械臂、地面/空中车辆等系统生成动力学可行轨迹，规划时间仅需微秒到毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于采样的运动规划器在处理动力学约束时面临计算瓶颈：需要求解困难的两点边界值问题或进行动力学积分，这显著增加了规划时间。虽然现有并行化方法能在微秒级生成几何路径，但无法处理动力学约束。

Method: 利用微分平坦性将运动规划问题从原始状态空间转换到平坦输出空间，在该空间中可获得BVP的解析时间参数化解和动力学积分。平坦输出空间的轨迹可转换回原始状态空间的闭式动力学可行轨迹，并通过SIMD并行化实现快速验证。

Result: 方法在模拟基准测试和真实实验中均验证有效，在杂乱和动态环境中仅需微秒到毫秒级的规划时间，且与任何基于采样的运动规划器兼容。

Conclusion: AkinoPDF为广泛类别的微分平坦机器人系统提供了一种快速、精确的并行化动力学运动规划技术，解决了传统方法在处理动力学约束时的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Motion planning under dynamics constraints, i.e., kinodynamic planning, enables safe robot operation by generating dynamically feasible trajectories that the robot can accurately track. For high-\dof robots such as manipulators, sampling-based motion planners are commonly used, especially for complex tasks in cluttered environments. However, enforcing constraints on robot dynamics in such planners requires solving either challenging two-point boundary value problems (BVPs) or propagating robot dynamics over time, both of which are computational bottlenecks that drastically increase planning times. Meanwhile, recent efforts have shown that sampling-based motion planners can generate plans in microseconds using parallelization, but are limited to geometric paths. This paper develops AkinoPDF, a fast parallelized sampling-based kinodynamic motion planning technique for a broad class of differentially flat robot systems, including manipulators, ground and aerial vehicles, and more. Differential flatness allows us to transform the motion planning problem from the original state space to a flat output space, where an analytical time-parameterized solution of the BVP and dynamics integration can be obtained. A trajectory in the flat output space is then converted back to a closed-form dynamically feasible trajectory in the original state space, enabling fast validation via ``single instruction, multiple data" parallelism. Our method is fast, exact, and compatible with any sampling-based motion planner. We extensively verify the effectiveness of our approach in both simulated benchmarks and real experiments with cluttered and dynamic environments, requiring mere microseconds to milliseconds of planning time.

</details>


### [17] [Large Reward Models: Generalizable Online Robot Reward Generation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16065)
*Yanru Wu,Weiduo Yuan,Ang Qi,Vitor Guizilini,Jiageng Mao,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的在线奖励生成框架，用于机器人操作策略的在线精炼，无需手动设计奖励函数，显著提升模仿学习策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作策略精炼中具有潜力，但其效果受限于难以设计泛化性强的奖励函数。传统方法需要手动设计奖励函数，这限制了强化学习的应用效率和泛化能力。

Method: 1) 基于最先进的VLM构建鲁棒、可扩展的奖励模型，在大规模多源数据集上训练；2) 使用VLM根据当前视觉观察生成多维奖励信号，包括过程奖励、完成奖励和时间对比奖励；3) 从模仿学习训练的基础策略开始，利用VLM生成的奖励以闭环方式指导策略纠正次优行为；4) 奖励模型在测试环境中以零样本方式运行。

Result: 在需要顺序执行和精确控制的挑战性长时程操作基准测试中，该方法在仅30次强化学习迭代后，显著提高了初始模仿学习策略的成功率，表现出卓越的样本效率。VLM生成的信号能够提供可靠反馈来纠正执行错误。

Conclusion: VLM生成的奖励信号能够为机器人学习提供可靠反馈，有效消除了手动奖励工程的需求，促进了机器人策略的高效在线精炼。该方法展示了基础VLM在机器人强化学习中的实用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown great potential in refining robotic manipulation policies, yet its efficacy remains strongly bottlenecked by the difficulty of designing generalizable reward functions. In this paper, we propose a framework for online policy refinement by adapting foundation VLMs into online reward generators. We develop a robust, scalable reward model based on a state-of-the-art VLM, trained on a large-scale, multi-source dataset encompassing real-world robot trajectories, human-object interactions, and diverse simulated environments. Unlike prior approaches that evaluate entire trajectories post-hoc, our method leverages the VLM to formulate a multifaceted reward signal comprising process, completion, and temporal contrastive rewards based on current visual observations. Initializing with a base policy trained via Imitation Learning (IL), we employ these VLM rewards to guide the model to correct sub-optimal behaviors in a closed-loop manner. We evaluate our framework on challenging long-horizon manipulation benchmarks requiring sequential execution and precise control. Crucially, our reward model operates in a purely zero-shot manner within these test environments. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves the success rate of the initial IL policy within just 30 RL iterations, demonstrating remarkable sample efficiency. This empirical evidence highlights that VLM-generated signals can provide reliable feedback to resolve execution errors, effectively eliminating the need for manual reward engineering and facilitating efficient online refinement for robot learning.

</details>


### [18] [Towards the Vision-Sound-Language-Action Paradigm: The HEAR Framework for Sound-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16086)
*Chang Nie,Tianchen Deng,Guangming Wang,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HEAR框架，将声音作为连续控制范式的核心要素，解决现有VLA模型在实时声音感知与操作中的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常将声音视为静态预执行提示或仅关注人类语音，忽略了实时环境声学在任务执行中的关键状态验证作用，导致关键声音因低频更新或系统延迟而丢失

Method: 提出VSLA连续控制范式，并实例化为HEAR框架，包含四个组件：流式历史记录器、多感官推理器、音频世界模型预测器和流匹配实现器策略

Result: 构建了OpenX-Sound预训练数据集和HEAR-Bench首个声音中心操作基准测试，结果表明鲁棒的声音中心操作需要因果持久性和显式时间学习

Conclusion: HEAR框架为多感官基础模型在具身智能体中的应用提供了实践步骤，使机器人能够感知和交互动态环境

Abstract: While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate audio, they typically treat sound as static pre-execution prompts or focus exclusively on human speech. This leaves a significant gap in real-time, sound-centric manipulation where fleeting environmental acoustics provide critical state verification during task execution. Consequently, key sounds are easily missed due to low-frequency updates or system latency. This problem is exacerbated by action chunking with open-loop execution, which creates a Blind Execution Interval where acoustic events are lost between discrete audio observation windows. Recognizing the necessity of continuous auditory awareness, we formalize Vision-Sound-Language-Action (VSLA) as a continuous control paradigm conditioned on vision, streaming audio, language, and proprioception under delayed decision loops. As an instantiation, we introduce HEAR, a VSLA framework integrating four components: (i) a streaming Historizer to maintain a compact, causal audio context across execution gaps; (ii) an Envisioner adapted from omni foundation models to reason over multi-sensory inputs; (iii) an Advancer, formulated as an audio world model, to learn temporal dynamics by predicting near-future audio codes; and (iv) a flow-matching Realizer policy to generate smooth action chunks. To address the scarcity of pretraining data and evaluations for VSLA, we construct OpenX-Sound for pretraining, alongside HEAR-Bench, the first sound-centric manipulation benchmark with strict causal timing rules. Our results suggest that robust sound-centric manipulation necessitates causal persistence and explicit temporal learning. This framework provides a practical step toward multi-sensory foundation models for embodied agents, enabling robots to perceive and interact with dynamic environments. Code and videos are available at https://hear.irmv.top.

</details>


### [19] [SE(3)-LIO: Smooth IMU Propagation With Jointly Distributed Poses on SE(3) Manifold for Accurate and Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16118)
*Gunhee Shin,Seungjae Lee,Jei Kong,Youngwoo Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SE(3)-LIO：一种在SE(3)流形上表示和传播位姿的激光雷达-惯性里程计，通过考虑预测位姿间的相关性来精确表征相对变换的不确定性，并集成到运动补偿中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMU传播方法在运动预测和运动补偿方面存在局限：运动预测中通常将6自由度位姿的旋转和平移分离处理，导致旋转变化未有效融入平移传播；运动补偿中使用预测位姿的相对变换补偿运动畸变，但预测位姿的固有误差会引入相对变换的不确定性。

Method: 在SE(3)流形上表示和传播位姿，使传播的平移能适当考虑旋转变化；通过考虑预测位姿间的相关性精确表征相对变换的不确定性，并将该不确定性纳入运动补偿中的测量噪声；提出SE(3)-LIO系统，集成上述IMU传播和不确定性感知运动补偿方法。

Result: 在多样化数据集上验证了SE(3)-LIO的有效性，源代码和附加材料已公开。

Conclusion: 提出的SE(3)流形位姿表示和传播方法能更好地处理旋转变化对平移的影响，不确定性感知运动补偿能更精确地处理运动畸变，整体提升了激光雷达-惯性里程计的精度。

Abstract: In estimating odometry accurately, an inertial measurement unit (IMU) is widely used owing to its high-rate measurements, which can be utilized to obtain motion information through IMU propagation. In this paper, we address the limitations of existing IMU propagation methods in terms of motion prediction and motion compensation. In motion prediction, the existing methods typically represent a 6-DoF pose by separating rotation and translation and propagate them on their respective manifold, so that the rotational variation is not effectively incorporated into translation propagation. During motion compensation, the relative transformation between predicted poses is used to compensate motion-induced distortion in other measurements, while inherent errors in the predicted poses introduce uncertainty in the relative transformation. To tackle these challenges, we represent and propagate the pose on SE(3) manifold, where propagated translation properly accounts for rotational variation. Furthermore, we precisely characterize the relative transformation uncertainty by considering the correlation between predicted poses, and incorporate this uncertainty into the measurement noise during motion compensation. To this end, we propose a LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO), referred to as SE(3)-LIO, that integrates the proposed IMU propagation and uncertainty-aware motion compensation (UAMC). We validate the effectiveness of SE(3)-LIO on diverse datasets. Our source code and additional material are available at: https://se3-lio.github.io/.

</details>


### [20] [SignNav: Leveraging Signage for Semantic Visual Navigation in Large-Scale Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16166)
*Jian Sun,Yuming Huang,He Li,Shuqi Xiao,Shenyan Guo,Maani Ghaffari,Qingbiao Li,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SignNav任务，要求智能体在大型室内环境中通过解读标识牌语义提示进行导航，并构建LSI-Dataset数据集和Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer模型解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在大型室内环境中能够利用标识牌语义提示进行导航，但这一能力在具身导航领域尚未得到充分探索。现有研究缺乏对动态语义提示和稀疏标识牌布局的专门处理。

Method: 提出Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer模型，包含空间感知模块（将标识牌语义提示与现实世界对应）和时间感知模块（捕捉历史状态与当前观测的长程依赖）。采用两阶段训练策略和Dataset Aggregation方法。

Result: 在val-unseen分割上达到80%的成功率和0.74 NDTW，实现最先进性能。真实世界部署验证了方法在无预建地图物理环境中的实用性。

Conclusion: SignNav任务填补了具身导航中语义提示理解的空白，提出的START模型有效解决了动态语义提示和稀疏标识牌带来的挑战，为现实世界室内导航应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans routinely leverage semantic hints provided by signage to navigate to destinations within novel Large-Scale Indoor (LSI) environments, such as hospitals and airport terminals. However, this capability remains underexplored within the field of embodied navigation. This paper introduces a novel embodied navigation task, SignNav, which requires the agent to interpret semantic hint from signage and reason about the subsequent action based on current observation. To facilitate research in this domain, we construct the LSI-Dataset for the training and evaluation of various SignNav agents. Dynamically changing semantic hints and sparse placement of signage in LSI environments present significant challenges to the SignNav task. To address these challenges, we propose the Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer (START) model for end-to-end decision-making. The spatial-aware module grounds the semantic hint of signage into physical world, while the temporal-aware module captures long-range dependencies between historical states and current observation. Leveraging a two-stage training strategy with Dataset Aggregation (DAgger), our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, recording an 80% Success Rate (SR) and 0.74 NDTW on val-unseen split. Real-world deployment further demonstrates the practicality of our method in physical environment without pre-built map.

</details>


### [21] [Enforcing Task-Specified Compliance Bounds for Humanoids via Anisotropic Lipschitz-Constrained Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16180)
*Zewen He,Yoshihiko Nakamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出各向异性Lipschitz约束策略(ALCP)，将任务空间刚度上界映射为策略雅可比矩阵的状态相关约束，通过谱范数惩罚在RL训练中强制执行，实现方向相关柔顺控制，提升人形机器人运动稳定性和抗冲击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在人形双足运动控制中展现出潜力，但模型无关特性使其难以施加任务特定且可定量验证的柔顺目标。现有Lipschitz约束策略方法通常使用单一标量约束，缺乏与物理柔顺规格的明确联系，无法实现方向相关的柔顺控制。

Method: 提出各向异性Lipschitz约束策略(ALCP)，将任务空间刚度上界映射为策略雅可比矩阵的状态相关Lipschitz风格约束。通过铰链平方谱范数惩罚在强化学习训练中强制执行该约束，保持物理可解释性的同时实现方向相关柔顺控制。

Result: 在人形机器人上的实验表明，ALCP提高了运动稳定性和冲击鲁棒性，同时减少了振荡和能量消耗。该方法成功实现了物理可解释的方向相关柔顺控制。

Conclusion: ALCP通过将物理柔顺规格映射为各向异性Lipschitz约束，为强化学习提供了可定量验证的柔顺控制框架，有效提升了人形机器人运动的稳定性和鲁棒性，同时保持了物理可解释性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated substantial potential for humanoid bipedal locomotion and the control of complex motions. To cope with oscillations and impacts induced by environmental interactions, compliant control is widely regarded as an effective remedy. However, the model-free nature of RL makes it difficult to impose task-specified and quantitatively verifiable compliance objectives, and classical model-based stiffness designs are not directly applicable. Lipschitz-Constrained Policies (LCP), which regularize the local sensitivity of a policy via gradient penalties, have recently been used to smooth humanoid motions. Nevertheless, existing LCP-based methods typically employ a single scalar Lipschitz budget and lack an explicit connection to physically meaningful compliance specifications in real-world systems. In this study, we propose an anisotropic Lipschitz-constrained policy (ALCP) that maps a task-space stiffness upper bound to a state-dependent Lipschitz-style constraint on the policy Jacobian. The resulting constraint is enforced during RL training via a hinge-squared spectral-norm penalty, preserving physical interpretability while enabling direction-dependent compliance. Experiments on humanoid robots show that ALCP improves locomotion stability and impact robustness, while reducing oscillations and energy usage.

</details>


### [22] [PanguMotion: Continuous Driving Motion Forecasting with Pangu Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16196)
*Quanhao Ren,Yicheng Li,Nan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PanguMotion：一种用于连续驾驶场景的运动预测框架，通过将Pangu-1B大语言模型的Transformer块集成到自动驾驶运动预测架构中，利用历史上下文相关性提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法通常独立处理离散驾驶场景，忽略了真实驾驶环境中固有的时间连续性和历史上下文相关性，这限制了预测的准确性和对真实驾驶环境的适应性。

Method: 提出PanguMotion框架，将Pangu-1B大语言模型的Transformer块作为特征增强模块集成到自动驾驶运动预测架构中；使用RealMotion数据重组策略处理Argoverse 2数据集，将独立场景转换为连续序列以模拟真实驾驶场景。

Result: 在经RealMotion策略处理的Argoverse 2数据集上进行实验，验证了框架在连续驾驶场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: PanguMotion通过利用大语言模型的Transformer块和连续场景建模，能够更好地捕捉驾驶环境中的时间连续性和历史上下文相关性，有望提升自动驾驶系统的运动预测性能。

Abstract: Motion forecasting is a core task in autonomous driving systems, aiming to accurately predict the future trajectories of surrounding agents to ensure driving safety. Existing methods typically process discrete driving scenes independently, neglecting the temporal continuity and historical context correlations inherent in real-world driving environments. This paper proposes PanguMotion, a motion forecasting framework for continuous driving scenarios that integrates Transformer blocks from the Pangu-1B large language model as feature enhancement modules into autonomous driving motion prediction architectures. We conduct experiments on the Argoverse 2 datasets processed by the RealMotion data reorganization strategy, transforming each independent scene into a continuous sequence to mimic real-world driving scenarios.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Cybathlon: On-demand Quadrupedal Assistance for People with Limited Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16772)
*Carmen Scheidemann,Andrei Cramariuc,Changan Chen,Jia-Ruei Chiu,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种按需四足辅助机器人系统，通过共享自主性方法控制，结合半自主任务执行与人类遥操作，帮助行动受限者完成移动操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将机械臂安装在电动轮椅上的解决方案在增加有限移动能力的同时，却增加了尺寸并降低了轮椅的机动性。需要一种既能提供辅助又不限制用户移动性的系统。

Method: 开发了按需四足辅助机器人系统，采用共享自主性控制方法，结合半自主任务执行与人类遥操作。系统自动化了拾取放置任务和环境中的语义、碰撞感知导航。为遥操作设计了嘴部操纵杆接口，使行动受限者能精确控制机器人末端执行器。

Result: 在Cybathlon 2024辅助机器人竞赛中展示系统，并在家庭实验设置中验证，测量任务完成时间和用户满意度。系统能够协助完成包括需要灵巧操作在内的多种任务。用户研究证实增加机器人自主性减轻了操作者的心理负荷。

Conclusion: 提出了一种灵活系统，有潜力帮助轮椅使用者通过解决移动操作问题而无需外部支持来保持日常生活独立性。在主观指标上取得了与先前最先进技术相当的结果，同时允许操作者更多自主性和更大的操作灵活性。

Abstract: Background: Assistance robots have the potential to increase the independence of people who need daily care due to limited mobility or being wheelchair-bound. Current solutions of attaching robotic arms to motorized wheelchairs offer limited additional mobility at the cost of increased size and reduced wheelchair maneuverability.
  Methods: We present an on-demand quadrupedal assistance robot system controlled via a shared autonomy approach, which combines semi-autonomous task execution with human teleoperation. Due to the mobile nature of the system it can assist the operator whenever needed and perform autonomous tasks independently, without otherwise restricting their mobility. We automate pick-and-place tasks, as well as robot movement through the environment with semantic, collision-aware navigation. For teleoperation, we present a mouth-level joystick interface that enables an operator with reduced mobility to control the robot's end effector for precision manipulation.
  Results: We showcase our system in the \textit{Cybathlon 2024 Assistance Robot Race}, and validate it in an at-home experimental setup, where we measure task completion times and user satisfaction. We find our system capable of assisting in a broad variety of tasks, including those that require dexterous manipulation. The user study confirms the intuition that increased robot autonomy alleviates the operator's mental load.
  Conclusions: We present a flexible system that has the potential to help people in wheelchairs maintain independence in everyday life by enabling them to solve mobile manipulation problems without external support. We achieve results comparable to previous state-of-the-art on subjective metrics while allowing for more autonomy of the operator and greater agility for manipulation.

</details>


### [24] [Enabling Dynamic Tracking in Vision-Language-Action Models via Time-Discrete and Time-Continuous Velocity Feedforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16218)
*Johannes Hechtl,Philipp Schmitt,Georg von Wichert,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型在工业机器人部署中存在顺应性与响应性权衡，本文提出两种提取速度前馈项的方法：时间离散有限差分近似和连续三次B样条动作空间，以改善低刚度下的跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中前景广阔，但在刚性工业机器人部署中面临顺应性与响应性的固有权衡。标准行为克隆方法预测低频离散位姿，忽略了低层顺应控制器通常使用的速度和加速度前馈项，导致需要高刚度实现精确跟踪，牺牲了安全接触动力学。

Method: 提出两种从VLA模型中提取速度目标的方法：1) 时间离散有限差分近似，作为现有模型的有效桥梁；2) 连续三次B样条动作空间，原生产生C²连续轨迹用于高频控制。两种方法均严格模型无关，兼容任何标准动作分块架构，仅需修改遥操作、数据处理和低层控制器。

Result: 在要求严格的接触丰富立方体入孔任务上微调π₀.₅模型并评估两种方法。结果显示：通过有限差分加入速度前馈项显著提高任务执行速度；连续B样条方法保持高总体成功率，为更平滑的高阶导数提供基础而不损害顺应性。

Conclusion: 将速度前馈项整合到VLA策略中对于解决顺应性与响应性权衡至关重要。提出的两种方法都能改善低刚度下的跟踪性能，其中有限差分方法提升执行速度，B样条方法保持高成功率并提供平滑轨迹基础。

Abstract: While vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown great promise for robot manipulation, their deployment on rigid industrial robots remains challenging due to the inherent trade-off between compliance and responsiveness. Standard Behavior Cloning (BC) approaches predict discrete poses at low frequencies, omitting the velocity and acceleration feedforward terms typically used by low-level compliant controllers. This requires to rely on high stiffness for accurate tracking, thereby sacrificing safe contact dynamics. In this paper, we demonstrate the importance of integrating velocity feedforward terms into VLA policies to resolve this trade-off. We propose two methods for extracting velocity targets from VLAs: a time-discrete finite-difference approximation that serves as a highly effective bridge for existing models, and a continuous Cubic B-Spline action space that natively yields $C^2$ continuous trajectories for high-frequency control. Crucially, both approaches are strictly model-agnostic and compatible with any standard action-chunking architecture, requiring modifications only to teleoperation, data processing, and the low-level controller. We fine-tune the $π_{0.5}$ model and evaluate both of our approaches on a demanding, contact-rich cube-in-hole task. Our results indicate that incorporating the velocity feedforward term via finite differences significantly improves task execution speed, while the continuous B-Spline approach maintains high overall success rates and provides a foundation for smoother higher-order derivatives without compromising compliance.

</details>


### [25] [Real-Time Decoding of Movement Onset and Offset for Brain-Controlled Rehabilitation Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16825)
*Kanishka Mitra,Satyam Kumar,Frigyes Samuel Racz,Deland Liu,Ashish D. Deshpande,José del R. Millán*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究实现了基于脑电图的在线双状态运动想象控制，使上肢外骨骼能够通过非侵入性脑电信号直接启动和中止目标导向的伸展运动，提高了康复训练的神经可塑性针对性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人辅助疗法主要作用于肢体层面，只能间接激活受损神经回路，这成为实现真正偶联性、神经可塑性靶向康复的关键障碍。需要开发能够直接基于神经意图控制康复机器人的系统。

Method: 实现基于脑电图的在线双状态运动想象控制，参与者使用脑电信号启动外骨骼辅助，并在运动过程中自主中止机器人。引入不对称边界诊断揭示任务型重定心引起的系统性偏差，并提出基于注视点的类别无关重定心方法，在不采样指令类别的情况下跟踪漂移并保持类别几何结构。

Result: 八名参与者在两个在线会话中，组平均命中率分别为启动61.5%、中止64.5%。基于注视点的重定心方法显著提高了无阈值可分性（启动AUC增益+56%，p=0.0117；中止增益+34%，p=0.0251），减少了日内和日间偏差。

Conclusion: 该研究弥合了离线解码与实用的意图驱动启停控制之间的差距，实现了与神经可塑性目标一致的精确时间偶联辅助，为未来临床转化提供了支持。

Abstract: Robot-assisted therapy can deliver high-dose, task-specific training after neurologic injury, but most systems act primarily at the limb level-engaging the impaired neural circuits only indirectly-which remains a key barrier to truly contingent, neuroplasticity-targeted rehabilitation. We address this gap by implementing online, dual-state motor imagery control of an upper-limb exoskeleton, enabling goal-directed reaches to be both initiated and terminated directly from non-invasive EEG. Eight participants used EEG to initiate assistance and then volitionally halt the robot mid-trajectory. Across two online sessions, group-mean hit rates were 61.5% for onset and 64.5% for offset, demonstrating reliable start-stop command delivery despite instrumental noise and passive arm motion. Methodologically, we reveal a systematic, class-driven bias induced by common task-based recentering using an asymmetric margin diagnostic, and we introduce a class-agnostic fixation-based recentering method that tracks drift without sampling command classes while preserving class geometry. This substantially improves threshold-free separability (AUC gains: onset +56%, p = 0.0117; offset +34%, p = 0.0251) and reduces bias within and across days. Together, these results help bridge offline decoding and practical, intention-driven start-stop control of a rehabilitation exoskeleton, enabling precisely timed, contingent assistance aligned with neuroplasticity goals while supporting future clinical translation.

</details>


### [26] [PA-LVIO: Real-Time LiDAR-Visual-Inertial Odometry and Mapping with Pose-Only Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16228)
*Hailiang Tang,Tisheng Zhang,Liqiang Wang,Xin Ding,Man Yuan,Xiaoji Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PA-LVIO是一种实时LiDAR-视觉-惯性里程计与建图系统，采用仅位姿的束调整框架，实现高精度、高效率的多传感器融合定位与建图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统中需要实时、精确的LiDAR-视觉-惯性里程计与建图技术，用于导航和规划任务。现有方法在实时性、精度和漂移消除方面存在改进空间。

Method: 提出PA-LVIO系统：1) 采用仅位姿的束调整框架处理LiDAR和视觉测量；2) 集成无边缘化的帧到地图LiDAR测量模型消除里程计漂移；3) 使用IMU中心的在线时空标定实现像素级LiDAR-相机对齐；4) 基于精确位姿估计构建RGB渲染点云地图。

Result: 在轮式机器人、无人机和手持设备采集的28个序列、超过50公里轨迹的公开和私有数据集上测试。PA-LVIO在里程计精度和建图质量方面优于或媲美最先进的LVIO方法，且能在桌面PC和嵌入式ARM计算机上实时运行。

Conclusion: PA-LVIO系统实现了高精度、高效率的实时LiDAR-视觉-惯性里程计与建图，通过创新的仅位姿束调整框架和无边缘化测量模型，在多种平台上均表现出优越性能，满足智能交通系统的导航需求。

Abstract: Real-time LiDAR-visual-inertial odometry and mapping is crucial for navigation and planning tasks in intelligent transportation systems. This study presents a pose-only bundle adjustment (PA) LiDAR-visual-inertial odometry (LVIO), named PA-LVIO, to meet the urgent need for real-time navigation and mapping. The proposed PA framework for LiDAR and visual measurements is highly accurate and efficient, and it can derive reliable frame-to-frame constraints within multiple frames. A marginalization-free and frame-to-map (F2M) LiDAR measurement model is integrated into the state estimator to eliminate odometry drifts. Meanwhile, an IMU-centric online spatial-temporal calibration is employed to obtain a pixel-wise LiDAR-camera alignment. With accurate estimated odometry and extrinsics, a high-quality and RGB-rendered point-cloud map can be built. Comprehensive experiments are conducted on both public and private datasets collected by wheeled robot, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), and handheld devices with 28 sequences and more than 50 km trajectories. Sufficient results demonstrate that the proposed PA-LVIO yields superior or comparable performance to state-of-the-art LVIO methods, in terms of the odometry accuracy and mapping quality. Besides, PA-LVIO can run in real-time on both the desktop PC and the onboard ARM computer.

</details>


### [27] [Industrial cuVSLAM Benchmark & Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16240)
*Charbel Abi Hana,Kameel Amareen,Mohamad Mostafa,Dmitry Slepichev,Hesam Rabeti,Zheng Wang,Mihir Acharya,Anthony Rizk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究对移动机器人在真实物流环境中的视觉里程计和视觉SLAM系统进行了全面的基准评估，发现结合cuVSLAM前端与自定义SLAM后端的混合方案在1.7公里大规模生产设施中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视觉里程计和视觉SLAM系统在真实物流环境中的性能，为移动机器人导航选择最优的视觉定位方案，并为机器人系统集成提供依据。

Method: 在受控轨迹（平移、旋转、混合运动）和大规模生产设施数据集（约1.7公里）上比较多种视觉里程计方法，使用Vicon运动捕捉系统和基于LiDAR的SLAM参考作为地面真值，通过绝对位姿误差评估性能。

Result: 混合堆栈（cuVSLAM前端+自定义SLAM后端）在映射精度方面表现最强，在NVIDIA Jetson平台上成功部署并测试了基于cuVSLAM的VO堆栈。

Conclusion: cuVSLAM作为核心VO组件在机器人堆栈中具有显著优势，其深度集成能够提升移动机器人在物流环境中的导航性能，验证了该方案在实际部署中的可行性。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive benchmark evaluation of visual odometry (VO) and visual SLAM (VSLAM) systems for mobile robot navigation in real-world logistical environments. We compare multiple visual odometry approaches across controlled trajectories covering translational, rotational, and mixed motion patterns, as well as a large-scale production facility dataset spanning approximately 1.7 km. Performance is evaluated using Absolute Pose Error (APE) against ground truth from a Vicon motion capture system and a LiDAR-based SLAM reference. Our results show that a hybrid stack combining the cuVSLAM front-end with a custom SLAM back-end achieves the strongest mapping accuracy, motivating a deeper integration of cuVSLAM as the core VO component in our robotics stack. We further validate this integration by deploying and testing the cuVSLAM-based VO stack on an NVIDIA Jetson platform.

</details>


### [28] [MG-Grasp: Metric-Scale Geometric 6-DoF Grasping Framework with Sparse RGB Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16270)
*Kangxu Wang,Siang Chen,Chenxing Jiang,Shaojie Shen,Yixiang Dai,Guijin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MG-Grasp：一种无需深度传感器的6-DoF抓取框架，通过双视图3D基础模型从稀疏RGB图像重建度量尺度点云，实现高质量物体抓取


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图RGB-D抓取检测通常需要深度传感器，而纯RGB的6-DoF抓取方法由于几何表示不准确，难以实现物理可靠的机器人操作。现有方法在可靠抓取生成方面存在局限性。

Method: 提出MG-Grasp框架，利用双视图3D基础模型结合相机内外参，从稀疏RGB图像重建度量尺度和多视图一致性的密集点云，并生成稳定的6-DoF抓取姿态。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion数据集和真实世界实验中，MG-Grasp在基于RGB的6-DoF抓取方法中达到了最先进的抓取性能。

Conclusion: MG-Grasp通过深度感知的3D重建实现了无需深度传感器的高质量6-DoF抓取，为机器人操作提供了可靠的几何表示基础。

Abstract: Single-view RGB-D grasp detection remains a com- mon choice in 6-DoF robotic grasping systems, which typically requires a depth sensor. While RGB-only 6-DoF grasp methods has been studied recently, their inaccurate geometric repre- sentation is not directly suitable for physically reliable robotic manipulation, thereby hindering reliable grasp generation. To address these limitations, we propose MG-Grasp, a novel depth- free 6-DoF grasping framework that achieves high-quality object grasping. Leveraging two-view 3D foundation model with camera intrinsic/extrinsic, our method reconstructs metric- scale and multi-view consistent dense point clouds from sparse RGB images and generates stable 6-DoF grasp. Experiments on GraspNet-1Billion dataset and real world demonstrate that MG-Grasp achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) grasp performance among RGB-based 6-DoF grasping methods.

</details>


### [29] [GenZ-LIO: Generalizable LiDAR-Inertial Odometry Beyond Indoor--Outdoor Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16273)
*Daehan Lee,Hyungtae Lim,Seongjun Kim,Soonbin Rho,Changhyeon Lee,Sanghyun Park,Junwoo Hong,Eunseon Choi,Hyunyoung Jo,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenZ-LIO：一种适用于室内外环境的通用激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过自适应体素降采样、混合度量状态更新和体素剪枝对应搜索来解决空间尺度变化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激光雷达-惯性里程计（LIO）在空间尺度变化（如室内到室外过渡）时性能下降，因为点云密度变化会影响鲁棒性和计算效率。需要一种能适应不同场景尺度的通用LIO框架。

Method: 1. 基于PID控制器原理自适应调节降采样体素大小，使体素化点数趋近尺度感知设定点；2. 采用点-面和点-点残差的混合度量状态更新，缓解激光雷达退化问题；3. 引入体素剪枝对应搜索策略，减少不必要的计算负担。

Result: 实验表明GenZ-LIO在封闭室内、开放室外和过渡环境中均能实现鲁棒的里程计估计，并提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: GenZ-LIO通过自适应体素调节、混合度量更新和计算优化，成功解决了LIO在空间尺度变化时的性能问题，实现了跨室内外环境的通用性。

Abstract: Light detection and ranging (LiDAR)-inertial odometry (LIO) enables accurate localization and mapping for autonomous navigation in various scenes. However, its performance remains sensitive to variations in spatial scale, which refers to the spatial extent of the scene reflected in the distribution of point ranges in a LiDAR scan. Transitions between confined indoor and expansive outdoor spaces induce substantial variations in point density, which may reduce robustness and computational efficiency. To address this issue, we propose GenZ-LIO, a LIO framework generalizable across both indoor and outdoor environments. GenZ-LIO comprises three key components. First, inspired by the principle of the proportional-integral-derivative (PID) controller, it adaptively regulates the voxel size for downsampling via feedback control, driving the voxelized point count toward a scale-informed setpoint while enabling stable and efficient processing across varying scene scales. Second, we formulate a hybrid-metric state update that jointly leverages point-to-plane and point-to-point residuals to mitigate LiDAR degeneracy arising from directionally insufficient geometric constraints. Third, to alleviate the computational burden introduced by point-to-point matching, we introduce a voxel-pruned correspondence search strategy that discards non-promising voxel candidates and reduces unnecessary computations. Experimental results demonstrate that GenZ-LIO achieves robust odometry estimation and improved computational efficiency across confined indoor, open outdoor, and transitional environments. Our code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [30] [Agile Interception of a Flying Target using Competitive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16279)
*Timothée Gavin,Simon Lacroix,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用强化学习训练两个无人机（拦截者和目标）的策略，通过高保真仿真环境实现敏捷无人机拦截，并在真实场景中验证性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决敏捷无人机拦截问题，传统启发式方法难以应对敏捷目标的复杂动态，需要更智能的自主拦截方案

Method: 将拦截问题建模为竞争性强化学习问题，使用PPO算法分别训练拦截者和目标策略，构建基于JAX的高保真仿真环境，包含真实四旋翼动力学模型和底层控制架构，支持GPU并行计算

Result: 训练的策略在捕获率、捕获时间和碰撞率方面优于常见启发式基线方法，能够有效拦截敏捷目标，并在室内飞行场地的真实场景中验证了性能

Conclusion: 竞争性强化学习方法能够有效解决敏捷无人机拦截问题，训练的策略在仿真和真实环境中均表现出优越性能，为自主无人机拦截提供了可行方案

Abstract: This article presents a solution to intercept an agile drone by another agile drone carrying a catching net. We formulate the interception as a Competitive Reinforcement Learning problem, where the interceptor and the target drone are controlled by separate policies trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). We introduce a high-fidelity simulation environment that integrates a realistic quadrotor dynamics model and a low-level control architecture implemented in JAX, which allows for fast parallelized execution on GPUs. We train the agents using low-level control, collective thrust and body rates, to achieve agile flights both for the interceptor and the target. We compare the performance of the trained policies in terms of catch rate, time to catch, and crash rate, against common heuristic baselines and show that our solution outperforms these baselines for interception of agile targets. Finally, we demonstrate the performance of the trained policies in a scaled real-world scenario using agile drones inside an indoor flight arena.

</details>


### [31] [OGScene3D: Incremental Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussian Scene Graph Mapping for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16301)
*Siting Zhu,Ziyun Lu,Guangming Wang,Chenguang Huang,Yongbo Chen,I-Ming Chen,Wolfram Burgard,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OGScene3D：一种增量式开放词汇3D场景理解系统，通过置信度高斯语义表示、分层语义优化和渐进图构建，实现无需预建完整3D地图的实时场景理解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇场景理解方法需要预建完整3D语义地图来构建场景图，限制了在机器人增量探索环境中的适用性。机器人应用需要能够在环境逐步探索过程中进行实时场景理解的系统。

Method: 1) 置信度高斯语义表示：联合建模语义预测及其可靠性；2) 分层3D语义优化：通过局部对应建立和全局细化实现语义一致性；3) 长期全局优化：利用历史观测的时间记忆增强语义预测；4) 渐进图构建：动态创建和更新节点及语义关系

Result: 在广泛使用的数据集和真实世界场景上的大量实验证明了OGScene3D在开放词汇场景理解方面的有效性，能够实现准确的3D语义映射和场景图增量构建

Conclusion: OGScene3D成功解决了现有方法需要预建完整3D地图的限制，通过增量式方法实现了机器人场景中的开放词汇场景理解，为导航、操作等下游任务提供了支持

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is crucial for robotic applications, enabling robots to comprehend complex 3D environmental contexts and supporting various downstream tasks such as navigation and manipulation. However, existing methods require pre-built complete 3D semantic maps to construct scene graphs for scene understanding, which limits their applicability in robotic scenarios where environments are explored incrementally. To address this challenge, we propose OGScene3D, an open-vocabulary scene understanding system that achieves accurate 3D semantic mapping and scene graph construction incrementally. Our system employs a confidence-based Gaussian semantic representation that jointly models semantic predictions and their reliability, enabling robust scene modeling. Building on this representation, we introduce a hierarchical 3D semantic optimization strategy that achieves semantic consistency through local correspondence establishment and global refinement, thereby constructing globally consistent semantic maps. Moreover, we design a long-term global optimization method that leverages temporal memory of historical observations to enhance semantic predictions. By integrating 2D-3D semantic consistency with Gaussian rendering contribution, this method continuously refines the semantic understanding of the entire scene.Furthermore, we develop a progressive graph construction approach that dynamically creates and updates both nodes and semantic relationships, allowing continuous updating of the 3D scene graphs. Extensive experiments on widely used datasets and real-world scenes demonstrate the effectiveness of our OGScene3D on open-vocabulary scene understanding.

</details>


### [32] [ADAPT: Adaptive Dual-projection Architecture for Perceptive Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16328)
*Shuo Shao,Tianchen Huang,Wei Gao,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADAPT是一种用于人形机器人3D环境导航的自适应感知架构，通过水平高程图和垂直距离图表示环境，并将感知范围作为可学习动作，在快速运动时扩展感知范围，在复杂场景中收缩以获得更高局部分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖固定的感知配置，难以在复杂3D环境中平衡感知精度与计算效率。需要一种既能适应不同运动速度又能灵活调整感知范围的架构，以实现鲁棒的人形机器人导航。

Method: ADAPT采用双投影架构：水平高程图表示地形几何，垂直距离图表示可通行空间约束。关键创新是将空间感知范围作为可学习的动作，使策略能够根据运动状态自适应调整感知范围。相比体素基方法，大幅降低了观测维度和计算开销。

Result: ADAPT显著加速了训练过程，在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现了零样本迁移，并在多种3D环境挑战中显著优于固定感知范围的基线方法，表现出高度鲁棒的穿越能力。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过自适应感知范围和高效的环境表示，成功解决了复杂3D环境中人形机器人导航的感知-计算权衡问题，为实现敏捷、鲁棒的人形机器人运动提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Agile humanoid locomotion in complex 3D en- vironments requires balancing perceptual fidelity with com- putational efficiency, yet existing methods typically rely on rigid sensing configurations. We propose ADAPT (Adaptive dual-projection architecture for perceptive traversal), which represents the environment using a horizontal elevation map for terrain geometry and a vertical distance map for traversable- space constraints. ADAPT further treats its spatial sensing range as a learnable action, enabling the policy to expand its perceptual horizon during fast motion and contract it in cluttered scenes for finer local resolution. Compared with voxel-based baselines, ADAPT drastically reduces observation dimensionality and computational overhead while substantially accelerating training. Experimentally, it achieves successful zero-shot transfer to a Unitree G1 Humanoid and signifi- cantly outperforms fixed-range baselines, yielding highly robust traversal across diverse 3D environtmental challenges.

</details>


### [33] [Faulty Coffees: Barriers to Adoption of an In-the-wild Robo-Barista](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16336)
*Bruce W. Wilson,David A. Robb,Mei Yii Lim,Helen Hastie,Matthew Peter Aylett,Theodoros Georgiou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究任务型叙事能否影响服务机器人的长期参与度，通过在老年住宅区部署机器人咖啡师进行5周实验，发现尽管设计了持续参与机制，但实际重复互动率低，面临技术故障、无障碍障碍和社会动态等挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索任务型叙事对话是否能够促进用户与服务机器人的长期参与和持续互动，特别是在真实社区环境中

Method: 在英格兰斯托克顿的50岁以上住宅区部署Furhat机器人咖啡师5周，居民通过互动获得免费咖啡，机器人被分配到叙事或非叙事对话条件，进行纵向人机交互研究

Result: 尽管设计了持续参与机制，但重复互动率低，遇到了"好奇心试用但无保留"现象、技术故障、无障碍障碍以及住宅区社会动态等挑战，叙事条件并未显著提升长期参与度

Conclusion: 将实验中的挑战作为核心问题而非边缘问题进行分析，反思真实环境实验的现实情况，为纵向人机交互研究提供实践教训，特别是当研究在实践中"瓦解"时的应对策略

Abstract: We set out to study whether task-based narratives could influence long-term engagement with a service robot. To do so, we deployed a Robo-Barista for five weeks in an over-50's housing complex in Stockton, England. Residents received a free daily coffee by interacting with a Furhat robot assigned to either a narrative or non-narrative dialogue condition. Despite designing for sustained engagement, repeat interaction was low, and we encountered curiosity trials without retention, technical breakdowns, accessibility barriers, and the social dynamics of a housing complex setting. Rather than treating these as peripheral issues, we foreground them in this paper. We reflect on the in-the-wild realities of our experiment and offer lessons for conducting longitudinal Human-Robot Interaction research when studies unravel in practice.

</details>


### [34] [ManiTwin: Scaling Data-Generation-Ready Digital Object Dataset to 100K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16866)
*Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Jiawei Liu,Honghao Su,Shaolong Zhu,Minxuan Wang,Zixuan Li,Yue Chen,Huan-ang Gao,Yusen Qin,Jiawei Wang,Qixuan Zhang,Lan Xu,Jingyi Yu,Yao Mu,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiTwin是一个自动化管道，可将单张图像转换为仿真就绪的3D数字孪生资产，并构建了包含10万个高质量标注3D资产的ManiTwin-100K数据集，用于大规模机器人操作数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真学习为扩展机器人操作能力提供了基础，但缺乏数据生成就绪的数字资产（规模和多样性不足）。需要自动化流程来生成高质量的仿真就绪3D资产。

Method: 提出ManiTwin自动化管道：从单张图像生成仿真就绪且语义标注的3D资产。构建ManiTwin-100K数据集，每个资产包含物理属性、语言描述、功能标注和验证的操作建议。

Result: ManiTwin提供了高效的资产合成和标注工作流程，ManiTwin-100K提供了高质量、多样化的资产，适用于操作数据生成、随机场景合成和VQA数据生成，为可扩展的仿真数据合成和策略学习奠定了基础。

Conclusion: ManiTwin管道和ManiTwin-100K数据集解决了仿真学习中数字资产不足的问题，为大规模机器人操作数据生成提供了有效解决方案，建立了可扩展仿真数据合成的基础。

Abstract: Learning in simulation provides a useful foundation for scaling robotic manipulation capabilities. However, this paradigm often suffers from a lack of data-generation-ready digital assets, in both scale and diversity. In this work, we present ManiTwin, an automated and efficient pipeline for generating data-generation-ready digital object twins. Our pipeline transforms a single image into simulation-ready and semantically annotated 3D asset, enabling large-scale robotic manipulation data generation. Using this pipeline, we construct ManiTwin-100K, a dataset containing 100K high-quality annotated 3D assets. Each asset is equipped with physical properties, language descriptions, functional annotations, and verified manipulation proposals. Experiments demonstrate that ManiTwin provides an efficient asset synthesis and annotation workflow, and that ManiTwin-100K offers high-quality and diverse assets for manipulation data generation, random scene synthesis, and VQA data generation, establishing a strong foundation for scalable simulation data synthesis and policy learning. Our webpage is available at https://manitwin.github.io/.

</details>


### [35] [Encoding Predictability and Legibility for Style-Conditioned Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16368)
*Adrien Jacquet Crétides,Mouad Abrini,Hamed Rahimi,Mohamed Chetouani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCDP是一个模块化框架，通过风格条件扩散策略在机器人轨迹生成中平衡效率与透明性，仅在目标模糊时启用表达性运动，否则保持最优效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中需要在效率与透明运动之间取得平衡。高度表达性的运动通常带来不必要的时间和能量成本，而可读性行为在目标明显时会产生冗余的次优轨迹。需要一种方法能根据环境配置在可读性和效率之间自适应切换。

Method: 提出风格条件扩散策略(SCDP)，采用后训练流程：冻结基础扩散策略，训练轻量级场景编码器和条件预测器来调节扩散过程。推理时通过模糊检测模块激活相应条件，仅在目标模糊时优先表达性运动，否则恢复高效路径。

Result: 在操作和导航任务上的评估表明，SCDP在模糊环境中增强了可读性，同时在不需要可读性时保持了最优效率，且无需重新训练基础策略。

Conclusion: SCDP成功解决了人机协作中效率与透明性之间的权衡问题，通过模块化框架实现了根据环境配置自适应调整轨迹生成策略，提高了协作的安全性和信任度。

Abstract: Striking a balance between efficiency and transparent motion is a core challenge in human-robot collaboration, as highly expressive movements often incur unnecessary time and energy costs. In collaborative environments, legibility allows a human observer a better understanding of the robot's actions, increasing safety and trust. However, these behaviors result in sub-optimal and exaggerated trajectories that are redundant in low-ambiguity scenarios where the robot's goal is already obvious. To address this trade-off, we propose Style-Conditioned Diffusion Policy (SCDP), a modular framework that constrains the trajectory generation of a pre-trained diffusion model toward either legibility or efficiency based on the environment's configuration. Our method utilizes a post-training pipeline that freezes the base policy and trains a lightweight scene encoder and conditioning predictor to modulate the diffusion process. At inference time, an ambiguity detection module activates the appropriate conditioning, prioritizing expressive motion only for ambiguous goals and reverting to efficient paths otherwise. We evaluate SCDP on manipulation and navigation tasks, and results show that it enhances legibility in ambiguous settings while preserving optimal efficiency when legibility is unnecessary, all without retraining the base policy.

</details>


### [36] [Onboard MuJoCo-based Model Predictive Control for Shipboard Crane with Double-Pendulum Sway Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16407)
*Oscar Pang,Lisa Coiffard,Paul Templier,Luke Beddow,Kamil Dreczkowski,Antoine Cully*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于MuJoCo MPC框架的实时控制管道，利用交叉熵方法规划器在物理模拟器中评估候选动作序列，有效解决海上起重机双摆载荷的摇摆抑制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上环境中起重机运输重载时面临双摆载荷摇摆问题，受风浪等外部扰动加剧。现有控制方法依赖简化分析模型或深度强化学习泛化能力差，难以在计算受限的非线性物理系统上部署预测控制器。

Method: 采用MuJoCo MPC框架构建实时控制管道，利用交叉熵方法规划器在物理模拟器中直接评估候选动作序列。这种基于采样的方法通过模拟推演来协调动态目标跟踪和摇摆阻尼的冲突目标，无需复杂分析模型。

Result: 控制器能在资源受限的嵌入式硬件上有效运行，在抵抗外部基础扰动方面优于传统PID和强化学习基线。系统对未建模的物理差异（如引入第二个载荷）表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于物理模拟器的采样预测控制方法能有效解决海上起重机双摆载荷摇摆抑制问题，在计算受限硬件上实现实时控制，且对未建模扰动具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transferring heavy payloads in maritime settings relies on efficient crane operation, limited by hazardous double-pendulum payload sway. This sway motion is further exacerbated in offshore environments by external perturbations from wind and ocean waves. Manual suppression of these oscillations on an underactuated crane system by human operators is challenging. Existing control methods struggle in such settings, often relying on simplified analytical models, while deep reinforcement learning (RL) approaches tend to generalise poorly to unseen conditions. Deploying a predictive controller onto compute-constrained, highly non-linear physical systems without relying on extensive offline training or complex analytical models remains a significant challenge. Here we show a complete real-time control pipeline centered on the MuJoCo MPC framework that leverages a cross-entropy method planner to evaluate candidate action sequences directly within a physics simulator. By using simulated rollouts, this sampling-based approach successfully reconciles the conflicting objectives of dynamic target tracking and sway damping without relying on complex analytical models. We demonstrate that the controller can run effectively on a resource-constrained embedded hardware, while outperforming traditional PID and RL baselines in counteracting external base perturbations. Furthermore, our system demonstrates robustness even when subjected to unmodeled physical discrepancies like the introduction of a second payload.

</details>


### [37] [LIMBERO: A Limbed Climbing Exploration Robot Toward Traveling on Rocky Cliffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16531)
*Kentaro Uno,Masazumi Imai,Kazuki Takada,Teruhiro Kataonami,Yudai Matsuura,Antonin Ringeval-Meusnier,Keita Nagaoka,Mikio Eguchi,Ryo Nishibe,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMBERO是一个10公斤级四足攀爬机器人，采用脊柱式抓握器在崎岖陡峭地形上实现稳定移动和攀爬，首次在1G重力条件下实现了该尺度攀爬机器人的陡峭岩石表面攀爬能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轮式机器人在崎岖不平地形上移动困难，而月球和行星探测需要能够在高度不规则和陡峭倾斜表面上移动的机器人。配备抓握器的四肢攀爬机器人成为有前景的解决方案。

Method: 1. 设计新型抓握器，采用耦合的手指闭合和脊柱钩挂运动，由单个电机紧密驱动，实现轻量化设计下的卓越抓握性能；2. 开发高效算法，在连续粗糙地形上可视化基于几何的抓握性指数；3. 将这些组件集成到LIMBERO四足攀爬机器人中。

Result: 1. 抓握器重量仅525克，抓握力超过150N；2. 实现了在1G重力条件下攀爬陡峭岩石表面的能力，这是该尺度攀爬机器人首次达到的性能。

Conclusion: LIMBERO四足攀爬机器人通过创新的抓握器设计和抓握性可视化算法，成功实现了在崎岖陡峭地形上的稳定攀爬，为月球和行星探测中的地形穿越提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In lunar and planetary exploration, legged robots have attracted significant attention as an alternative to conventional wheeled robots, which struggle to traverse rough and uneven terrain. To enable locomotion over highly irregular and steeply inclined surfaces, limbed climbing robots equipped with grippers on their feet have emerged as a promising solution. In this paper, we present LIMBERO, a 10 kg-class quadrupedal climbing robot that employs spine-type grippers for stable locomotion and climbing on rugged and steep terrain. We first introduce a novel gripper design featuring coupled finger-closing and spine-hooking motions, tightly actuated by a single motor, which achieves exceptional grasping performance (>150 N) despite its lightweight design (525 g). Furthermore, we develop an efficient algorithm to visualize a geometry-based graspability index on continuous rough terrain. Finally, we integrate these components into LIMBERO and demonstrate its ability to ascend steep rocky surfaces under a 1 G gravity condition, a performance not previously achieved yet for limbed climbing robots of this scale.

</details>


### [38] [Kamino: GPU-based Massively Parallel Simulation of Multi-Body Systems with Challenging Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16536)
*Vassilios Tsounis,Guirec Maloisel,Christian Schumacher,Ruben Grandia,Agon Serifi,David Müller,Chris Amevor,Tobias Widmer,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kamino是一个基于GPU的物理求解器，用于大规模并行模拟具有高度耦合机械约束的异构系统，支持原生处理运动学闭环，实现高保真接触动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前复杂机器人系统（特别是具有运动学闭环的系统）的模拟通常需要简化系统拓扑为运动学树，并加入显式的闭环约束或模仿关节，这增加了实践负担。Kamino旨在通过原生支持这些耦合类型来减轻这一负担。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Warp在Python中实现，集成到Newton框架中。核心采用最先进的约束优化算法，通过将约束刚体多体前向动力学转化为非线性互补问题来计算约束力，支持异构世界的批量模拟。

Result: 在单GPU上实现了4096个并行环境的模拟，成功在具有6个嵌套运动学闭环的双足机器人DR Legs上训练了可行的行走策略，展示了高吞吐量并行化模拟能力。

Conclusion: Kamino通过原生支持运动学闭环和异构系统，实现了高保真、高吞吐量的物理模拟，为复杂机器人系统的数据驱动方法（如大规模强化学习）提供了有效的模拟平台。

Abstract: We present Kamino, a GPU-based physics solver for massively parallel simulations of heterogeneous highly-coupled mechanical systems.
