Implemented in Python using NVIDIA Warp and integrated into the Newton framework, it enables the application of data-driven methods, such as large-scale reinforcement learning, to complex robotic systems that exhibit strongly coupled kinematic and dynamic constraints such as kinematic loops. The latter are often circumvented by practitioners; approximating the system topology as a kinematic tree and incorporating explicit loop-closure constraints or so-called mimic joints. Kamino aims at alleviating this burden by natively supporting these types of coupling. This capability facilitates high-throughput parallelized simulations that capture the true nature of mechanical systems that exploit closed kinematic chains for mechanical advantage. Moreover, Kamino supports heterogeneous worlds, allowing for batched simulation of structurally diverse robots on a single GPU. At its core lies a state-of-the-art constrained optimization algorithm that computes constraint forces by solving the constrained rigid multi-body forward dynamics transcribed as a nonlinear complementarity problem. This leads to high-fidelity simulations that can resolve contact dynamics without resorting to approximate models that simplify and/or convexify the problem. We demonstrate RL policy training on DR Legs, a biped with six nested kinematic loops, generating a feasible walking policy while simulating 4096 parallel environments on a single GPU.

</details>


### [39] [Conservative Offline Robot Policy Learning via Posterior-Transition Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16542)
*Wanpeng Zhang,Hao Luo,Sipeng Zheng,Yicheng Feng,Haiweng Xu,Ziheng Xi,Chaoyi Xu,Haoqi Yuan,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PTR方法，通过后验转移重加权解决机器人异构数据中的冲突和低质量监督问题，实现更保守的离线后训练适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据集存在异构性（不同本体、相机设置、演示质量差异），包含恢复行为、操作者技能不一致或弱监督信息。均匀后训练对所有样本赋予相同权重，会平均化冲突或低归因数据，需要更智能的样本权重分配方法。

Method: 提出后验转移重加权（PTR）：1）将观察到的动作后结果编码为潜在目标；2）插入到不匹配目标的候选池中；3）使用独立的转移评分器估计目标索引的softmax识别后验；4）计算后验与均匀分布的比值作为PTR分数；5）转换为裁剪混合权重，通过自归一化加权回归应用于原始动作目标。该方法无需可处理的策略似然，兼容扩散和流匹配动作头。

Result: PTR根据每个样本在现有表示下的动作后结果归因性重新分配信用，而不是均匀信任所有记录监督，改进了对异构机器人数据的保守离线适应。

Conclusion: PTR是一种无奖励、保守的后训练方法，通过智能样本重加权处理机器人数据集的异构性，避免了对冲突或低质量数据的平均化，提高了离线策略适应的效果。

Abstract: Offline post-training adapts a pretrained robot policy to a target dataset by supervised regression on recorded actions. In practice, robot datasets are heterogeneous: they mix embodiments, camera setups, and demonstrations of varying quality, so many trajectories reflect recovery behavior, inconsistent operator skill, or weakly informative supervision. Uniform post-training gives equal credit to all samples and can therefore average over conflicting or low-attribution data. We propose Posterior-Transition Reweighting (PTR), a reward-free and conservative post-training method that decides how much each training sample should influence the supervised update. For each sample, PTR encodes the observed post-action consequence as a latent target, inserts it into a candidate pool of mismatched targets, and uses a separate transition scorer to estimate a softmax identification posterior over target indices. The posterior-to-uniform ratio defines the PTR score, which is converted into a clipped-and-mixed weight and applied to the original action objective through self-normalized weighted regression. This construction requires no tractable policy likelihood and is compatible with both diffusion and flow-matching action heads. Rather than uniformly trusting all recorded supervision, PTR reallocates credit according to how attributable each sample's post-action consequence is under the current representation, improving conservative offline adaptation to heterogeneous robot data.

</details>


### [40] [A Pin-Array Structured Climbing Robot for Stable Locomotion on Steep Rocky Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16543)
*Keita Nagaoka,Kentaro Uno,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种配备柔性针阵列结构夹持器的攀爬机器人，通过被动顺应表面不规则性实现稳定抓握，无需复杂传感或控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攀爬机器人在非结构化环境中面临重大挑战，特别是在不规则表面上实现可靠附着是关键问题。现有方法通常需要复杂的传感和控制，限制了机器人在真实环境中的实用性

Method: 设计了一种配备柔性针阵列结构夹持器的移动攀爬机器人。每个针采用垂直分割设计，结合弹性元件和金属脊柱，能够与微观表面特征实现机械互锁。夹持器被动顺应表面不规则性，无需复杂控制

Result: 统计建模和实验验证表明，个体针力和接触数量的变异性是抓握不确定性的主要来源。机器人在室内倾斜墙面（10-30度）和室外自然岩石地形测试中表现出鲁棒稳定的运动能力

Conclusion: 强调被动柔顺性和机械冗余的设计为真实世界攀爬机器人提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案，同时最小化了控制复杂性

Abstract: Climbing robots face significant challenges when navigating unstructured environments, where reliable attachment to irregular surfaces is critical. We present a novel mobile climbing robot equipped with compliant pin-array structured grippers that passively conform to surface irregularities, ensuring stable ground gripping without the need for complicated sensing or control. Each pin features a vertically split design, combining an elastic element with a metal spine to enable mechanical interlocking with microscale surface features. Statistical modeling and experimental validation indicate that variability in individual pin forces and contact numbers are the primary sources of grasping uncertainty. The robot demonstrated robust and stable locomotion in indoor tests on inclined walls (10-30 degrees) and in outdoor tests on natural rocky terrain. This work highlights that a design emphasizing passive compliance and mechanical redundancy provides a practical and robust solution for real-world climbing robots while minimizing control complexity.

</details>


### [41] [ASCENT: Transformer-Based Aircraft Trajectory Prediction in Non-Towered Terminal Airspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16550)
*Alexander Prutsch,David Schinagl,Horst Possegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASCENT：基于Transformer的轻量级多模态3D飞机轨迹预测模型，用于非塔台终端空域安全提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非塔台终端空域交通密度高、事故风险大，准确的轨迹预测能提升通用航空安全性

Method: 采用基于Transformer的运动编码器和基于查询的解码器，集成领域感知的3D坐标归一化和参数化预测，生成多样化机动假设

Result: 在TrajAir和TartanAviation数据集上优于现有基线，编码器有效捕捉运动动态，解码器与结构化飞机交通模式对齐

Conclusion: ASCENT是实时飞机轨迹预测的有效方法，消融研究证实了解码器设计、坐标系建模和参数化输出的贡献

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction can improve General Aviation safety in non-towered terminal airspace, where high traffic density increases accident risk. We present ASCENT, a lightweight transformer-based model for multi-modal 3D aircraft trajectory forecasting, which integrates domain-aware 3D coordinate normalization and parameterized predictions. ASCENT employs a transformer-based motion encoder and a query-based decoder, enabling the generation of diverse maneuver hypotheses with low latency. Experiments on the TrajAir and TartanAviation datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms prior baselines, as the encoder effectively captures motion dynamics and the decoder aligns with structured aircraft traffic patterns. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm the contributions of the decoder design, coordinate-frame modeling, and parameterized outputs. These results establish ASCENT as an effective approach for real-time aircraft trajectory prediction in non-towered terminal airspace.

</details>


### [42] [Dexterous grasp data augmentation based on grasp synthesis with fingertip workspace cloud and contact-aware sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16609)
*Liqi Wu,Haoyu Jia,Kento Kawaharazuka,Hirokazu Ishida,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于遥操作的抓取姿态采集框架，结合指尖接触感知的采样抓取生成器（FSG）进行数据增强，通过AutoWS自动生成结构化指尖工作空间云，实现任意结构机械手的实时抓取生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取是机器人应用的基础组件，但高效生成抓取数据集用于训练仍是瓶颈。机械手结构多样性使得通用抓取生成方法设计复杂，需要一种能适应任意结构机械手的高效数据增强方法。

Method: 1. 基于遥操作的框架采集少量抓取姿态演示；2. 使用FSG（指尖接触感知的采样抓取生成器）进行数据增强；3. 提出AutoWS自动生成结构化机器人指尖工作空间云，将手部结构信息直接嵌入点云中，避免逆运动学计算。

Result: 在YCB物体抓取实验中，该方法在速度和有效姿态生成率方面显著优于现有方法。框架支持任意结构机械手的实时抓取生成，结合演示可产生类人抓取。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据驱动的抓取训练提供了高效鲁棒的数据增强工具，能够适应不同机械手结构，实现实时抓取生成，并产生符合人类抓取习惯的抓取姿态。

Abstract: Robotic grasping is a fundamental yet crucial component of robotic applications, as effective grasping often serves as the starting point for various tasks. With the rapid advancement of neural networks, data-driven approaches for robotic grasping have become mainstream. However, efficiently generating grasp datasets for training remains a bottleneck. This is compounded by the diverse structures of robotic hands, making the design of generalizable grasp generation methods even more complex. In this work, we propose a teleoperation-based framework to collect a small set of grasp pose demonstrations, which are augmented using FSG--a Fingertip-contact-aware Sampling-based Grasp generator. Based on the demonstrated grasp poses, we propose AutoWS, which automatically generates structured workspace clouds of robotic fingertips, embedding the hand structure information directly into the clouds to eliminate the need for inverse kinematics calculations. Experiments on grasping the YCB objects show that our method significantly outperforms existing approaches in both speed and valid pose generation rate. Our framework enables real-time grasp generation for hands with arbitrary structures and produces human-like grasps when combined with demonstrations, providing an efficient and robust data augmentation tool for data-driven grasp training.

</details>


### [43] [Reconciling distributed compliance with high-performance control in continuum soft robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16630)
*Vito Daniele Perfetta,Daniel Feliu Talegon,Ebrahim Shahabi,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高度柔顺的连续体机械臂，通过协同设计建模、驱动和控制，实现了分布式柔顺性与高性能动态控制的共存，在完全柔顺的连续体上达到了软体机器人中最快的任务执行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高性能闭环控制主要依赖分段刚性架构，通过抑制复杂变形模式简化建模和控制，但这使得系统行为类似于传统关节机器人。软体机器人领域存在一个隐含结论：分布式柔顺性与动态精度是不兼容的。本文旨在证明这种权衡并非根本性的。

Method: 平台集成了直接驱动、能够实现弯曲和扭转耦合的肌腱路由方案，以及基于欠驱动系统降阶应变模型的结构化非线性控制架构。通过协同设计建模、驱动和控制，在保持必要机械复杂性的同时实现高带宽闭环控制。

Result: 实验证明系统能够准确、可重复地执行动态笛卡尔任务，包括快速定位和交互。在毫米级精度下，执行速度相比先前方法提高了近四倍，达到了软体机器人中最快的任务执行速度。

Conclusion: 分布式柔顺性与高性能动态控制可以共存，这为真正柔顺的机械臂开辟了道路，使其能够在不牺牲形态丰富性的情况下接近刚性机器人的操作能力。

Abstract: High-performance closed-loop control of truly soft continuum manipulators has remained elusive. Experimental demonstrations have largely relied on sufficiently stiff, piecewise architectures in which each actuated segment behaves as a distributed yet effectively rigid element, while deformation modes beyond simple bending are suppressed. This strategy simplifies modeling and control, but sidesteps the intrinsic complexity of a fully compliant body and makes the system behave as a serial kinematic chain, much like a conventional articulated robot. An implicit conclusion has consequently emerged within the community: distributed softness and dynamic precision are incompatible. Here we show this trade-off is not fundamental. We present a highly compliant, fully continuum robotic arm - without hardware discretization or stiffness-based mode suppression - that achieves fast, precise task-space convergence under dynamic conditions. The platform integrates direct-drive actuation, a tendon routing scheme enabling coupled bending and twisting, and a structured nonlinear control architecture grounded in reduced-order strain modeling of underactuated systems. Modeling, actuation, and control are co-designed to preserve essential mechanical complexity while enabling high-bandwidth loop closure. Experiments demonstrate accurate, repeatable execution of dynamic Cartesian tasks, including fast positioning and interaction. The proposed system achieves the fastest reported task-execution speed among soft robots. At millimetric precision, execution speed increases nearly fourfold compared with prior approaches, while operating on a fully compliant continuum body. These results show that distributed compliance and high-performance dynamic control can coexist, opening a path toward truly soft manipulators approaching the operational capabilities of rigid robots without sacrificing morphological richness.

</details>


### [44] [Kinema4D: Kinematic 4D World Modeling for Spatiotemporal Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16669)
*Mutian Xu,Tianbao Zhang,Tianqi Liu,Zhaoxi Chen,Xiaoguang Han,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kinema4D：首个动作条件化的4D生成式机器人模拟器，将机器人-世界交互解耦为精确的4D机器人控制轨迹和生成式的环境反应建模，实现物理合理、几何一致且具身无关的交互模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频生成的机器人模拟方法主要在2D空间或依赖静态环境线索，忽略了机器人-世界交互本质上是4D时空事件，需要精确的交互建模。传统模拟器受限于视觉/物理约束，无法模拟复杂的现实世界动态。

Method: 1) 精确4D机器人控制表示：通过运动学驱动基于URDF的3D机器人，生成精确的4D机器人控制轨迹；2) 生成式环境反应建模：将4D机器人轨迹投影为点云图作为时空视觉信号，控制生成模型合成复杂环境的反应动态为同步的RGB/点云序列。构建了包含201,426个交互片段的大规模数据集Robo4D-200k用于训练。

Result: 方法能有效模拟物理合理、几何一致且具身无关的交互，准确反映多样化的现实世界动态。首次展示了零样本迁移潜力，为下一代具身模拟提供了高保真基础。

Conclusion: Kinema4D通过恢复机器人-世界交互的4D本质，同时确保精确的机器人控制，超越了传统模拟器的限制，为具身AI提供了更真实、灵活的模拟环境，具有推动下一代具身模拟发展的潜力。

Abstract: Simulating robot-world interactions is a cornerstone of Embodied AI. Recently, a few works have shown promise in leveraging video generations to transcend the rigid visual/physical constraints of traditional simulators. However, they primarily operate in 2D space or are guided by static environmental cues, ignoring the fundamental reality that robot-world interactions are inherently 4D spatiotemporal events that require precise interactive modeling. To restore this 4D essence while ensuring the precise robot control, we introduce Kinema4D, a new action-conditioned 4D generative robotic simulator that disentangles the robot-world interaction into: i) Precise 4D representation of robot controls: we drive a URDF-based 3D robot via kinematics, producing a precise 4D robot control trajectory. ii) Generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions: we project the 4D robot trajectory into a pointmap as a spatiotemporal visual signal, controlling the generative model to synthesize complex environments' reactive dynamics into synchronized RGB/pointmap sequences. To facilitate training, we curated a large-scale dataset called Robo4D-200k, comprising 201,426 robot interaction episodes with high-quality 4D annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively simulates physically-plausible, geometry-consistent, and embodiment-agnostic interactions that faithfully mirror diverse real-world dynamics. For the first time, it shows potential zero-shot transfer capability, providing a high-fidelity foundation for advancing next-generation embodied simulation.

</details>


### [45] [When Should a Robot Think? Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Embodied Robotic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16673)
*Jun Liu,Pu Zhao,Zhenglun Kong,Xuan Shen,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Lin Cui,Hao Tang,Geng Yuan,Wei Niu,Wenbin Zhang,Xue Lin,Gaowen Liu,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RARRL：基于强化学习的资源感知推理框架，用于自适应协调具身智能体中的推理与执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人系统依赖LLM进行高级推理，但LLM推理引入显著计算延迟和资源开销，影响动作执行和系统可靠性。过度推理会延迟动作，推理不足则导致决策错误和任务失败，需要解决"何时推理、何时执行"的根本问题。

Method: 提出RARRL（基于强化学习的资源感知推理）分层框架，学习高层协调策略而非底层控制策略。该策略在智能体决策层运行，根据当前观察、执行历史和剩余资源，自适应决定是否调用推理、使用何种推理角色以及分配多少计算预算。

Result: 在ALFRED基准测试中，使用经验延迟配置文件进行广泛实验，RARRL相比固定或启发式推理策略，持续提高任务成功率，同时减少执行延迟并增强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应推理控制对于构建可靠高效的具身机器人智能体至关重要，RARRL框架有效解决了推理与执行之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Embodied robotic systems increasingly rely on large language model (LLM)-based agents to support high-level reasoning, planning, and decision-making during interactions with the environment. However, invoking LLM reasoning introduces substantial computational latency and resource overhead, which can interrupt action execution and reduce system reliability. Excessive reasoning may delay actions, while insufficient reasoning often leads to incorrect decisions and task failures. This raises a fundamental question for embodied agents: when should the agent reason, and when should it act? In this work, we propose RARRL (Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning), a hierarchical framework for resource-aware orchestration of embodied agents. Rather than learning low-level control policies, RARRL learns a high-level orchestration policy that operates at the agent's decision-making layer. This policy enables the agent to adaptively determine whether to invoke reasoning, which reasoning role to employ, and how much computational budget to allocate based on current observations, execution history, and remaining resources. Extensive experiments, including evaluations with empirical latency profiles derived from the ALFRED benchmark, show that RARRL consistently improves task success rates while reducing execution latency and enhancing robustness compared with fixed or heuristic reasoning strategies. These results demonstrate that adaptive reasoning control is essential for building reliable and efficient embodied robotic agents.

</details>


### [46] [Learning Whole-Body Control for a Salamander Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16683)
*Mengze Tian,Qiyuan Fu,Chuanfang Ning,Javier Jia Jie Pey,Auke Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用强化学习为仿蝾螈两栖机器人开发统一的关节级全身控制器，实现从仿真到真实硬件的稳定转移，并在陆地和水中实现协调运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蝾螈机器人大多依赖中枢模式发生器（CPG）和基于模型的协调策略，而基于学习的关节级全身控制器在仿真到真实硬件转移方面研究不足，且很少有腿式机器人尝试在复杂两栖环境中应用学习型控制器。

Method: 采用强化学习，将本体感觉观测和指令速度映射到关节级动作，使协调运动行为自然涌现；部署时采用系统级真实到仿真匹配和仿真到真实转移策略。

Result: 学习到的控制器在真实世界中实现了在平坦和不平坦地形上的稳定协调行走；在仿真中还实现了行走与游泳之间的过渡，展示了跨不同物理模式的运动能力。

Conclusion: 强化学习能够为高度关节化的仿蝾螈机器人开发统一的关节级控制器，实现从仿真到真实硬件的可靠转移，并为理解跨不同物理模式的运动现象提供了新视角。

Abstract: Amphibious legged robots inspired by salamanders are promising in applications in complex amphibious environments. However, despite the significant success of training controllers that achieve diverse locomotion behaviors in conventional quadrupedal robots, most salamander robots relied on central-pattern-generator (CPG)-based and model-based coordination strategies for locomotion control. Learning unified joint-level whole-body control that reliably transfers from simulation to highly articulated physical salamander robots remains relatively underexplored. In addition, few legged robots have tried learning-based controllers in amphibious environments. In this work, we employ Reinforcement Learning to map proprioceptive observations and commanded velocities to joint-level actions, allowing coordinated locomotor behaviors to emerge. To deploy these policies on hardware, we adopt a system-level real-to-sim matching and sim-to-real transfer strategy. The learned controller achieves stable and coordinated walking on both flat and uneven terrains in the real world. Beyond terrestrial locomotion, the framework enables transitions between walking and swimming in simulation, highlighting a phenomenon of interest for understanding locomotion across distinct physical modes.

</details>


### [47] [Development of Low-Cost and Bidirectional Syringe Pumps for Soft Robotics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16803)
*Krishamsu Subedi Chhetri,Aryan Mayor,Elise Corbin,Logan Walker,John Rieffel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发低成本模块化注射泵系统，解决传统气动驱动软机器人的局限性，支持抽真空和充气双重功能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统气动驱动方法（高压电磁阀、医用隔膜泵、微型压缩机、压缩流体）在效能、成本、复杂性和精度方面存在显著挑战，限制了硅基体素软机器人（Silibots）的发展和应用

Method: 设计并构建基于现成组件和3D打印部件的低成本模块化注射泵系统，该系统具备抽真空和充气双重功能，硬件模块化且软件可定制

Result: 开发出灵活可扩展的注射泵系统，能够根据研究需求定制或同时操作多个具有独特参数的泵，为软机器人技术的研究和教育提供更易获取的工具

Conclusion: 该注射泵系统克服了传统气动驱动方法的局限性，通过其低成本、模块化设计和双重驱动能力，为软机器人技术在研究和教育领域的广泛应用铺平了道路

Abstract: Soft robotics leverages deformable materials to develop robots capable of navigating unstructured and dynamic environments. Silicone Voxel-Based Soft Robots (Silibots) are a type of pneumatically actuated soft robots that rely on the inflation and deflation of their voxels for shape-shifting behaviors. However, traditional pneumatic actuation methods (high pressure solenoids, medical diaphragm pumps, micro compressors, compressed fluid) pose significant challenges due to their limited efficacy, cost, complexity, or lack of precision. This work introduces a low cost and modular syringe pump system, constructed with off the shelf and 3D printed parts, designed to overcome these limitations. The syringe pump system also enhances actuation with the unique ability to pull a vacuum as well pump air into the soft robot. Furthermore, the syringe pump features modular hardware and customizable software, allowing for researchers to tailor the syringe pump to their requirements or operate multiple pumps simultaneously with unique pump parameters. This flexibility makes the syringe pump an accessible and scalable tool that paves the way for broader adoption of soft robotic technologies in research and education.

</details>


### [48] [BrickSim: A Physics-Based Simulator for Manipulating Interlocking Brick Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16853)
*Haowei Wen,Ruixuan Liu,Weiyi Piao,Siyu Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrickSim是首个用于互锁砖块组装的实时物理模拟器，通过紧凑的力基力学模型和结构化凸二次规划解决卡扣连接问题，实现高保真度的组装、拆卸和结构坍塌模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁砖块组装为接触密集和长时程机器人操作提供了标准化测试平台，但现有刚体模拟器无法准确捕捉卡扣连接力学。需要开发专门模拟器来支持机器人操作研究。

Method: 提出紧凑的力基力学模型描述卡扣连接，使用结构化凸二次规划求解内力分布。采用混合架构：底层物理引擎处理刚体动力学，单独处理卡扣力学。基于Isaac Sim开发，支持多种机器人集成。

Result: 在150个真实世界组装体上，静态稳定性预测准确率达100%，平均求解时间5ms。动态跌落测试中准确再现结构坍塌，精确匹配断裂发生位置和具体断裂点。支持机器人组装演示。

Conclusion: BrickSim是首个实时高保真互锁砖块组装模拟器，为灵巧长时程机器人操作研究提供基础平台。开源代码已发布，支持机器人研究社区使用。

Abstract: Interlocking brick assemblies provide a standardized yet challenging testbed for contact-rich and long-horizon robotic manipulation, but existing rigid-body simulators do not faithfully capture snap-fit mechanics. We present BrickSim, the first real-time physics-based simulator for interlocking brick assemblies. BrickSim introduces a compact force-based mechanics model for snap-fit connections and solves the resulting internal force distribution using a structured convex quadratic program. Combined with a hybrid architecture that delegates rigid-body dynamics to the underlying physics engine while handling snap-fit mechanics separately, BrickSim enables real-time, high-fidelity simulation of assembly, disassembly, and structural collapse. On 150 real-world assemblies, BrickSim achieves 100% accuracy in static stability prediction with an average solve time of 5 ms. In dynamic drop tests, it also faithfully reproduces real-world structural collapse, precisely mirroring both the occurrence of breakage and the specific breakage locations. Built on Isaac Sim, BrickSim further supports seamless integration with a wide variety of robots and existing pipelines. We demonstrate robotic construction of brick assemblies using BrickSim, highlighting its potential as a foundation for research in dexterous, long-horizon robotic manipulation. BrickSim is open-source, and the code is available at https://github.com/intelligent-control-lab/BrickSim.

</details>


### [49] [MolmoB0T: Large-Scale Simulation Enables Zero-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16861)
*Abhay Deshpande,Maya Guru,Rose Hendrix,Snehal Jauhri,Ainaz Eftekhar,Rohun Tripathi,Max Argus,Jordi Salvador,Haoquan Fang,Matthew Wallingford,Wilbert Pumacay,Yejin Kim,Quinn Pfeifer,Ying-Chun Lee,Piper Wolters,Omar Rayyan,Mingtong Zhang,Jiafei Duan,Karen Farley,Winson Han,Eli Vanderbilt,Dieter Fox,Ali Farhadi,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Dhruv Shah,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了机器人学习中需要真实世界数据或任务特定微调才能实现sim-to-real迁移的传统观点，展示了通过大规模多样化仿真数据可以实现零样本迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战机器人学习领域的普遍观点，即仅靠仿真不足以实现有效的sim-to-real迁移，通常需要真实世界数据收集或任务特定微调。研究探索是否通过足够大规模和多样化的仿真训练数据，可以实现零样本迁移到真实世界。

Method: 开发了MolmoBot-Engine，一个完全开源的流程化数据生成管道，用于在MolmoSpaces中跨机器人、任务和多样化仿真环境生成程序化数据。创建了包含180万专家轨迹的MolmoBot-Data数据集。训练了三种策略类别：基于Molmo2的多帧视觉语言模型MolmoBot，用于直接比较的MolmoBot-Pi0，以及适合边缘部署和RL微调的轻量级策略MolmoBot-SPOC。

Result: 在Franka FR3桌面操作任务和Rainbow Robotics RB-Y1移动操作平台上进行真实世界评估，实现了零样本迁移到未见过的物体和环境。在桌面拾放任务中，MolmoBot在4种设置下达到79.2%的成功率，显著优于π0.5的39.2%。

Conclusion: 程序化环境生成结合多样化关节资产可以产生鲁棒的操作策略，能够广泛泛化到真实世界，证明了仅通过大规模多样化仿真数据实现零样本sim-to-real迁移的可行性。

Abstract: A prevailing view in robot learning is that simulation alone is not enough; effective sim-to-real transfer is widely believed to require at least some real-world data collection or task-specific fine-tuning to bridge the gap between simulated and physical environments. We challenge that assumption. With sufficiently large-scale and diverse simulated synthetic training data, we show that zero-shot transfer to the real world is not only possible, but effective for both static and mobile manipulation. We introduce MolmoBot-Engine, a fully open-source pipeline for procedural data generation across robots, tasks, and diverse simulated environments in MolmoSpaces. With it, we release MolmoBot-Data, a dataset of 1.8 million expert trajectories for articulated object manipulation and pick-and-place tasks. We train three policy classes: MolmoBot, a Molmo2-based multi-frame vision-language model with a flow-matching action head; MolmoBot-Pi0, which replicates the $π_0$ architecture to enable direct comparison; and MolmoBot-SPOC, a lightweight policy suitable for edge deployment and amenable to RL fine-tuning. We evaluate on two robotic platforms: the Franka FR3 for tabletop manipulation tasks and the Rainbow Robotics RB-Y1 mobile manipulator for door opening, drawer manipulation, cabinet interaction, and mobile pick-and-place. Without any real-world fine-tuning, our policies achieve zero-shot transfer to unseen objects and environments. On tabletop pick-and-place, MolmoBot achieves a success rate of 79.2% in real world evaluations across 4 settings, outperforming $π_{0.5}$ at 39.2%. Our results demonstrate that procedural environment generation combined with diverse articulated assets can produce robust manipulation policies that generalize broadly to the real world. Technical Blog: https://allenai.org/blog/molmobot-robot-manipulation

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [50] [Parameter-Efficient Deep Learning for Ultrasound-Based Human-Machine Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15625)
*Antonios Lykourinas,Chinmay Pendse,Francky Catthoor,Veronique Rochus,Xavier Rottenberg,Athanassios Skodras*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种深度学习模型在超声手部姿态估计任务上的性能，发现通过使用步进学习率调度器和射频信号包络作为输入模态，4层UDACNN模型以更少的参数超越了XceptionTime的性能，在Ultra-Pro数据集上达到了77.72%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声作为一种有前景的人机交互模态，可用于手部姿态估计，实现多达23个自由度的丰富交互。然而，现有研究缺乏对模型、输入模态和训练策略的系统比较，且只有一个公开数据集（Ultra-Pro）可用于可重复的基准测试。

Method: 在Ultra-Pro数据集上比较了六种基于不同标准选择的深度学习模型。重点研究了输入模态（射频信号包络）和训练策略（步进学习率调度器）的影响，并提出了4层UDACNN模型。

Result: 4层UDACNN模型在Ultra-Pro数据集上达到了77.72%的准确率，比XceptionTime性能提升2.28个百分点，同时参数减少了87.52%。这比之前报道的基线方法绝对提升了0.88%。

Conclusion: 优化人机交互性能需要模型、预处理和训练算法的适当组合。研究结果表明，通过精心选择输入模态和训练策略，可以用更简单的模型架构实现更好的性能。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) has emerged as a promising modality for Human-Machine Interfaces (HMIs), with recent research efforts exploring its potential for Hand Pose Estimation (HPE). A reliable solution to this problem could introduce interfaces with simultaneous support for up to 23 degrees of freedom encompassing all hand and wrist kinematics, thereby allowing far richer and more intuitive interaction strategies. Despite these promising results, a systematic comparison of models, input modalities and training strategies is missing from the literature. Moreover, there is only one publicly available dataset, namely the Ultrasound Adaptive Prosthetic Control (Ultra-Pro) dataset, enabling reproducible benchmarking and iterative model development. In this paper, we compare the performance of six different deep learning models, selected based on diverse criteria, on this benchmark. We demonstrate that, by using a step learning rate scheduler and the envelope of the RF signals as input modality, our 4-layer deep UDACNN surpasses XceptionTime's performance by $2.28$ percentage points while featuring $87.52\%$ fewer parameters. This result ($77.72\%$) constitutes an absolute improvement of $0.88\%$ from previously reported baselines. According to our findings, the appropriate combination of model, preprocessing and training algorithm is crucial for optimizing HMI performance.

</details>


### [51] [The Evolving Duet of Two Modalities: A Survey on Integrating Text and Visualization for Data Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15640)
*Xingyu Lan,Xi Li,Yixing Zhang,Mengqin Cheng,Jiazhe Wang,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文对数据可视化中文本作为叙事工具的作用进行了系统性综述，分析了文本在可视化中的功能、效果和设计方法，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本在数据可视化中作为叙事工具的作用被严重忽视。现有研究主要关注文本作为数据输入和交互方式，而文本在支持故事讲述和解释方面的功能研究零散且不系统。为了填补这一空白，需要对文本在可视化中的叙事作用进行系统性分析。

Method: 对98篇相关文献进行系统性综述，研究文本如何在可视化中被利用，分析其功能和效果，并探索如何设计文本来促进数据通信。

Result: 通过综合研究，识别了该领域的重要研究空白，并提出了未来发展方向，旨在推进文本与可视化的整合。

Conclusion: 文本作为叙事工具在数据可视化中具有重要作用，但相关研究尚不系统。本文的系统性综述为设计增强叙事清晰度和促进参与度的文本提供了指导，并为该领域的未来发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Text plays a fundamental yet understudied role as a narrative device in data visualization. While existing research has extensively explored text as data input and interaction modality, its function in supporting storytelling and interpretation remains fragmented. To address this gap, this work presents a systematic review of 98 publications that provide insights into using text as narrative. We investigate how text can be utilized in visualization, analyze its functions and effects, and explore how it can be designed to facilitate data communication. Our synthesis identifies significant research gaps in this domain and proposes future directions to advance the integration of text and visualization, ultimately aiming to provide guidance for designing text that enhances narrative clarity and fosters engagement.

</details>


### [52] [Lessons from Real-World Deployment of a Cognition-Preserving Writing Tool: Students Actively Engage with Critical Thinking and Planning Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15777)
*Yinuo Yang,Zheng Zhang,Ningzhi Tang,Xu Wang,Alex Ambrose,Nathaniel Myers,Patrick Clauss,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI写作工具VISAR在真实课堂环境中验证了认知保留脚手架的有效性，学生通过结构化支持获得写作学习收益


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明AI支持的认知脚手架（如观点探索和论点提炼）的益处，但这些功能在真实课堂环境中的运作机制仍未被充分探索。需要了解AI写作工具在真实教学场景中的整合效果和学生使用模式。

Method: 在本科写作课程中部署AI写作工具VISAR，跨越两个学期的三个班级（共49名学生），每班使用一周。采用混合方法：结合交互日志分析、写作成果分析、问卷调查和访谈，研究学生在真实写作任务中如何使用VISAR功能。

Result: 学生确实将AI支持的认知脚手架用于写作学习，并取得了可测量的学习收益。与先前研究相反，当系统提供结构化的规划和针对性生成支持时，学生自然地选择参与这些认知保留脚手架。这种学习导向的交互模式与议论文写作质量、概念理解提升以及新兴的批判性AI素养呈正相关。

Conclusion: 研究提供了AI支持写作脚手架在真实课堂环境中运作的实证证据，强调了认知保留功能在AI写作工具中的设计价值，为未来学习导向的AI写作工具提供了设计见解。

Abstract: AI-supported writing tools show strong potential for scaffolding students' learning of argumentative writing. Prior work has demonstrated the benefits of AI-supported cognitive scaffolds, such as idea exploration and argument refinement, but how these features function in authentic classroom settings remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate the classroom integration of an AI-supported writing tool, VISAR. We deployed VISAR in an undergraduate writing course across three sections for one week each over two semesters (49 students total). Using a mixed-methods approach that combines interaction logs, writing artifact analysis, surveys, and interviews, we examine how students used VISAR features in authentic writing tasks. Our findings confirm that students appropriated AI-supported cognitive scaffolds for writing learning and achieved measurable learning gains. While prior studies suggest that students may bypass important cognitive processes when using AI writing assistants, our classroom deployment shows that when systems provide structured supports for planning and targeted generation, students naturally choose to engage with these cognition-preserving scaffolds. These learning-oriented interaction patterns were positively associated with argumentative writing quality, improved conceptual understanding, and emerging critical AI literacy, highlighting the design value of cognition-preserving features in AI writing tools. Together, these findings provide empirical evidence of how AI-supported writing scaffolds operate in authentic classroom contexts and offer design insights for future learning-oriented AI writing tools.

</details>


### [53] [Lost in Transcription: Subtitle Errors in Automatic Speech Recognition Reduce Speaker and Content Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15807)
*Kowe Kadoma,Priyal Shrivastava,Mor Naaman*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 字幕错误会降低观众对演讲者及其内容的评价，而ASR系统在不同口音群体上的性能差异可能导致口音较重的演讲者受到双重惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究自动语音识别系统在不同人口群体中性能差异的实际影响，特别是字幕错误如何影响观众对演讲者及其内容的评价。

Method: 采用预先注册的在线实验设计，招募207名美国众包工作者作为参与者。实验让参与者观看不同口音的演讲者发表演讲，字幕分为准确和易出错两种条件，通过控制变量研究字幕质量对评价的影响。

Result: 结果显示易出错的字幕会一致降低对所有演讲者及其内容的评价。在控制字幕质量后，未发现不同口音群体之间存在差异影响。但结合ASR系统性能差异，口音较重的演讲者可能因字幕错误而受到双重惩罚。

Conclusion: 字幕错误会损害演讲者及其内容的评价，虽然实验未显示口音群体间的差异影响，但由于ASR系统在不同口音上的性能差异，口音较重的演讲者实际上可能面临更大的评价风险。

Abstract: Researchers have demonstrated that Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems perform differently across demographic groups. In this work, we examined how subtitle errors affect evaluations of speakers and their content using a preregistered online experiment (N=207, U.S.-based crowdworkers). Participants watched speakers with various accents deliver a talk in which the subtitles were accurate or error-prone. Our results indicate that error-prone subtitles consistently reduce both speaker and content evaluations for all speakers. We did not see disparate impact between the accent groups, controlling for subtitle quality. Taken together, though, the findings of this short paper imply that speakers with accents for which ASR systems perform poorly are likely to be further penalized by viewers with lower evaluations.

</details>


### [54] [Interpretative Interfaces: Designing for AI-Mediated Reading Practices and the Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15863)
*Gabrielle Benabdallah*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文主张从可解释AI转向解释性参与，通过交互界面让用户直接操作语言模型的中间表征，而非仅提供系统行为解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI接口仅提供解释但无法产生真正理解，科学家依赖LLMs进行文献处理时缺乏直接参与模型运作的手段，需要从被动解释转向主动参与。

Method: 借鉴文本学术、计算诗学、阅读写作技术史（如旁注、注释、索引、标注系统），设计解释性界面作为交互环境，让非专家用户干预语言模型的表征空间，特别是允许用户选择token并追踪其在模型中间层的轨迹。

Result: 提出解释性界面概念框架，使用户能够观察token语义位置随上下文处理的变化，标注有意义的转换，在模型内部表征中"刻写"自己的阅读路径，类似读者在书中通过标注和书签创建个人地图。

Conclusion: 将AI可解释性重新定义为交互设计项目而非纯技术问题，为支持解释性参与和科学知识批判性管理的AI中介阅读开辟路径。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) interfaces seek to make large language models more transparent, yet explanation alone does not produce understanding. Explaining a system's behavior is not the same as being able to engage with it, to probe and interpret its operations through direct manipulation. This distinction matters for scientific disciplines in particular: scientists who increasingly rely on LLMs for reading, citing, and producing literature reviews have little means of directly engaging with how these models process and transform the texts they generate. In this ongoing design research project, I argue for a shift from explainability to interpretative engagement. This shift moves away from accounts of system behavior to instead enable users to manipulate a model's intermediate representations. Drawing on textual scholarship, computational poetics, and the history of reading and writing technologies, including practices such as marginalia, glosses, indices, and annotation systems, I propose interpretative interfaces as interactive environments in which non-expert users can intervene in the representational space of a language model. More specifically, such interfaces will allow users to select a token and follow its trajectory through the model's intermediate layers. This way, they can observe how its semantic position shifts as context is processed, and possibly annotate the transformations they find useful or meaningful. The same way readers can create their own maps within a book through annotations and bookmarks, interpretative interfaces will allow users to inscribe their reading of a model's internal representations. The goal of this project is to reframe AI interpretability as an interaction design project rather than a purely technical one, and to open a path toward AI-mediated reading that supports interpretative engagement and critical stewardship of scientific knowledge.

</details>


### [55] [Thermopneumatic Pixels for Fast, Localized, Low-Voltage Touch Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16750)
*Max Linnander,Yon Visell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 热气压像素(TPP)是一种用于紧凑型可穿戴和表面触觉系统的触觉致动器，通过低压电脉冲产生瞬态压力变化，实现毫米级位移和超过1N的峰值力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种易于快速制造、简单集成到紧凑型可穿戴和表面触觉系统的触觉致动器，为实验性和消费级界面提供可访问的高性能触觉反馈解决方案。

Method: 采用热气压致动原理，将低压(~10V)电脉冲转换为密封腔内的瞬态压力增加，产生适合触觉刺激的平面外力和位移。使用廉价易得材料通过分层组装制造，支持快速原型制作和空间分布式致动。

Result: 机械特性显示峰值力超过1N，位移达毫米级。开发了用于同时操作多个TPP模块的驱动电子设备，感知研究结果证明了所得触觉反馈的有效性。

Conclusion: 低压热气压致动是一种可访问且高性能的方法，适用于将触觉反馈嵌入实验性和消费级界面，为触觉系统提供了快速制造和简单集成的解决方案。

Abstract: We present thermopneumatic pixels (TPPs), which are tactile actuators designed for rapid fabrication and straightforward integration into compact wearable and surface-based haptic systems. Each TPP converts low-voltage ($\sim$10 V) electrical pulses into transient pressure increases within a sealed cavity, producing out-of-plane forces and displacements suitable for tactile stimulation. The architecture enables scalable fabrication and spatially distributed actuation while maintaining simple electrical interfacing. The TPPs are constructed from inexpensive, readily available materials using straightforward layer-based assembly, facilitating rapid prototyping and integration into interactive devices. Mechanical characterization demonstrates peak forces exceeding 1 N and millimeter displacements. We further present driving electronics for operating multiple TPP modules concurrently and report perceptual study results demonstrating the effectiveness of the resulting tactile feedback. Together, these results establish low-voltage thermopneumatic actuation as an accessible and high-performance approach for embedding tactile feedback into experimental and consumer-facing interfaces.

</details>


### [56] [Why Avoid Generative Legal AI Systems? Hallucination, Overreliance, and their Impact on Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15937)
*Gizem Gültekin Varkonyi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文主张在法律职业中部署生成式AI系统需要严格限制，因为存在幻觉和过度依赖的关键风险。文章分析了生成式法律AI的定义、风险及其对司法独立和基本权利保护的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机是揭示生成式AI在法律领域应用中的潜在危险，特别是幻觉（hallucination）和过度依赖（overreliance）问题。作者认为这些风险可能削弱法律系统的可靠性和公正性，因此需要对此进行深入分析并提出相应的治理建议。

Method: 文章采用概念分析方法，首先定义了"生成式法律AI"这一术语，然后分析了其技术基础（基于统计token预测的架构而非法律推理）。接着将这些问题置于欧洲AI治理框架下，探讨幻觉和自动化偏见如何削弱可解释性原则。最后通过理论论证说明这些风险对司法独立的影响。

Result: 分析表明，生成式法律AI系统存在严重的幻觉风险，系统会优先考虑语言流畅性而非事实准确性，产生虚构内容。这些幻觉掩盖了推理过程，而输出的说服力和类人特性鼓励了专业人员的过度依赖。在欧盟AI治理框架下，这种虚假数据与自动化偏见的相互作用从根本上削弱了可解释性原则。

Conclusion: 文章得出结论：如果没有有效的人类审查机制，常规采用生成式法律AI将对司法独立和基本权利保护构成重大挑战。因此，在法律职业中部署这类系统需要强有力的限制措施，以确保法律系统的完整性和公正性。

Abstract: This article argues that the deployment of generative AI systems in legal profession requires strong restraint due to the critical risks of hallucination and overreliance. Central to this analysis is the definition of Generative Legal AI (GLAI), an umbrella term for systems specifically adapted for the legal domain which is ranging from document drafting to decision support in criminal justice. Unlike traditional AI, GLAI models are built on architectures designed for statistical token prediction rather than legal reasoning, often leading to confabulations where the system prioritizes linguistic fluency over factual accuracy. These hallucinations obscure the reasoning process, while the persuasive, human-like nature of the output encourages professional overreliance. The paper situates these dynamics within the framework of European AI governance, arguing that the interaction between fabricated data and automation bias fundamentally weakens the principle of explainability. The article concludes that without effective mechanisms for meaningful human scrutiny, the routine adoption of GLAI poses significant challenges to judicial independence and the protection of fundamental rights.

</details>


### [57] [Adaptive Captioning with Emotional Cues: Supporting DHH and Neurodivergent Learners in STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15977)
*Sunday David Ubur,Eugenia Ha Rim Rho,Denis Gracanin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了在实时字幕中嵌入情感和多模态线索（如表情、手势、关键词高亮和表情符号）的设计方案，以改善DHH和神经多样性学习者在STEM教育中的理解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实时字幕经常忽略情感和非语言线索，这对聋人和听力障碍者以及神经多样性学习者（如ADHD）的理解造成困难，特别是在认知要求高的STEM教育中，这种缺失会加剧不同能力用户面临的挑战。

Method: 采用设计导向的探索方法，开发了四种嵌入情感和多模态线索的字幕原型（包括面部表情、身体手势、关键词高亮和表情符号），通过初步研究和主要研究（共24名参与者）进行评估。

Result: 研究发现某些原型相比传统字幕降低了自我报告的认知负荷并提高了理解分数。定性反馈揭示了可定制字幕特征对适应神经多样性用户偏好（如ADHD或对表情符号的不同接受程度）的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为无障碍技术研究提供了重要见解，探讨了如何以既可用又有益于广泛学习者群体的方式将情感线索整合到字幕中，强调了可定制功能对满足神经多样性用户需求的重要性。

Abstract: Real-time captioning is vital for Deaf and Hard of Hearing (DHH) and neurodivergent learners (e.g., those with ADHD), yet it often omits emotional and non-verbal cues essential for comprehension. This omission is particularly consequential in STEM education, where cognitively demanding material can exacerbate the challenges faced by caption users across diverse ability profiles. In this paper, we present a design-oriented exploration of four captioning prototypes that embed emotional and multimodal cues, including facial expressions, body gestures, keyword highlighting, and emoji. Across a pilot and a main study with 24 participants, we found that certain prototypes reduced self-reported cognitive load and improved comprehension scores compared to traditional captions. Qualitative feedback reveals the importance of customizable caption features to accommodate neurodivergent users' preferences (e.g., ADHD or different levels of comfort with emojis). Our findings contribute to ongoing conversations in accessible technology research about how best to integrate emotional cues into captions in a way that is both usable and beneficial for a wide range of learners.

</details>


### [58] [The Midas Touch in Gaze vs. Hand Pointing: Modality-Specific Failure Modes and Implications for XR Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15991)
*Mohammad Dastgheib,Fatemeh Pourmahdian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: XR自适应模态平台研究：通过眼动去杂和手部目标宽度膨胀两种自适应干预，比较手部与眼动输入在静态与自适应模式下的性能差异，发现手部输入在吞吐量、错误率和认知负荷方面均优于眼动输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XR界面在输入方式上面临两难选择：手部输入易导致疲劳，而眼动输入存在"米达斯接触"问题和精度限制。研究旨在探索模态特定的自适应干预是否能改善XR指向性能并降低工作负荷。

Method: 开发了xr-adaptive-modality-2025平台，这是一个基于Web的开源框架，结合生理学启发的眼动模拟、ISO 9241-9多方向点击任务，以及两种模态特定的自适应干预：眼动去杂和手部目标宽度膨胀。采用2×2×2被试内设计，变量包括模态（手部vs眼动）、UI模式（静态vs自适应）和压力（有vs无）。

Result: 69名参与者的结果显示：手部输入吞吐量高于眼动（5.17 vs 4.73 bits/s），错误率更低（1.8% vs 19.1%），NASA-TLX工作负荷也更低。错误模式存在显著差异：眼动错误主要是滑动错误（99.2%），而手部错误主要是错过错误（95.7%）。眼动去杂干预适度减少了超时但未减少滑动错误，手部宽度膨胀因UI集成错误无法评估。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了模态特定的失败模式对自适应策略设计具有直接意义，建立的平台为未来研究提供了可重复的基础设施。手部输入在性能上优于眼动输入，但两种模态的错误模式完全不同，需要针对性的自适应干预策略。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) interfaces impose both ergonomic and cognitive demands, yet current systems often force a binary choice between hand-based input, which can produce fatigue, and gaze-based input, which is vulnerable to the Midas Touch problem and precision limitations. We introduce the xr-adaptive-modality-2025 platform, a web-based open-source framework for studying whether modality-specific adaptive interventions can improve XR-relevant pointing performance and reduce workload relative to static unimodal interaction. The platform combines physiologically informed gaze simulation, an ISO 9241-9 multidirectional tapping task, and two modality-specific adaptive interventions: gaze declutter and hand target-width inflation. We evaluated the system in a 2 x 2 x 2 within-subjects design manipulating Modality (Hand vs. Gaze), UI Mode (Static vs. Adaptive), and Pressure (Yes vs. No). Results from N=69 participants show that hand yielded higher throughput than gaze (5.17 vs. 4.73 bits/s), lower error (1.8% vs. 19.1%), and lower NASA-TLX workload. Crucially, error profiles differed sharply by modality: gaze errors were predominantly slips (99.2%), whereas hand errors were predominantly misses (95.7%), consistent with the Midas Touch account. Of the two adaptive interventions, only gaze declutter executed in this dataset; it modestly reduced timeouts but not slips. Hand width inflation was not evaluable due to a UI integration bug. These findings reveal modality-specific failure modes with direct implications for adaptive policy design, and establish the platform as a reproducible infrastructure for future studies.

</details>


### [59] [Change is Hard: Consistent Player Behavior Across Games with Conflicting Incentives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16136)
*Emily Chen,Alexander J. Bisberg,Dmitri Williams,Magy Seif El-Nasr,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究玩家在《英雄联盟》和《云顶之弈》中的灵活性表现，发现玩家行为在跨游戏环境中具有一致性，个体能动性比游戏结构更能预测跨平台行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索玩家灵活性（愿意参与广泛选项或专精）在不同游戏环境中的表现，研究跨游戏行为保留和一致性，减少自选择偏差，为长期关于能动性与结构的辩论提供实证证据。

Method: 分析4,830名在两个游戏中都至少玩过50场竞技比赛的玩家数据，采用新颖的跨游戏分析方法追踪相同玩家在不同环境中的行为，比较《英雄联盟》（鼓励专精）和《云顶之弈》（鼓励灵活）的游戏机制差异。

Result: 尽管两款游戏基于绩效的成功激励不同行为（《英雄联盟》鼓励专精，《云顶之弈》鼓励灵活），但玩家在跨平台中表现出行为一致性。个体能动性比游戏强加的结构更能预测跨平台行为。

Conclusion: 研究支持个体能动性在预测跨平台行为中比游戏结构更重要的观点，为游戏开发者、设计师和研究人员提供了关于构建促进行为改变系统的启示，表明在竞技环境中玩家的行为模式具有跨游戏一致性。

Abstract: This paper examines how player flexibility -- a player's willingness to engage in a breadth of options or specialize -- manifests across two gaming environments: League of Legends (League) and Teamfight Tactics (TFT). We analyze the gameplay decisions of 4,830 players who have played at least 50 competitive games in both titles and explore cross-game dynamics of behavior retention and consistency. Our work introduces a novel cross-game analysis that tracks the same players' behavior across two different environments, reducing self-selection bias. Our findings reveal that while games incentivize different behaviors (specialization in League versus flexibility in TFT) for performance-based success, players exhibit consistent behavior across platforms. This study contributes to long-standing debate about agency versus structure, showing individual agency may be more predictive of cross-platform behavior than game-imposed structure in competitive settings. These insights offer implications for game developers, designers and researchers interested in building systems to promote behavior change.

</details>


### [60] [One Kiss: Emojis as Agents of Genre Flux in Generative Comics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16359)
*Xiruo Wang,Xinyi Jiang,Ziqi Lyu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: One Kiss是一个漫画生成系统，通过表情符号输入实现"情感引导"，让用户从提示工程师转变为叙事导演，利用语义模糊性促进创意流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于提示的生成式AI交互存在精确控制与创意流动之间的权衡问题，用户需要在详细描述和创造性表达之间做出妥协。作者旨在探索一种更自然、更具情感导向的创作方式。

Method: 系统采用双流输入机制：用户通过绘制面板框架定义结构节奏，通过关键词与表情符号配对设置氛围基调。引入"情感引导"概念，利用表情符号的语义模糊性作为创意资源而非限制。

Result: 初步研究（N=6）表明，这种软引导方法可能将用户角色从提示工程师重新定义为叙事导演，模糊性成为创意惊喜的来源而非控制损失。系统支持"类型流动"，情感输入在面板间累积并逐渐改变故事类型。

Conclusion: One Kiss展示了通过情感导向输入而非精确文本提示进行创意协作的潜力，表情符号的模糊语义成为创意资源，为生成式AI的交互设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Generative AI has made visual storytelling widely accessible, yet current prompt-based interactions often force users into a trade-off between precise control and creative flow. We present One Kiss, a co-creative comic generation system that introduces "Affective Steering". Instead of writing text prompts, users guide the tone of their story through emoji inputs, whose semantic ambiguity becomes a resource rather than a limitation. Unlike traditional text-to-image tools that rely on explicit descriptions, One Kiss uses a dual-stream input in which users define structural pacing by sketching panel frames and set atmospheric tone by pairing keywords with emojis. This mechanism enables "Genre Flux," where emotional inputs accumulate across panels and gradually shift the genre of a story. A preliminary study (N = 6) suggests that this soft steering approach may reframe the user's role from prompt engineer to narrative director, with ambiguity serving as a source of creative surprise rather than a loss of control.

</details>


### [61] [CoEmpaTeam: Enhancing Cognitive Empathy using LLM-based Avatars and Dynamic Role Play in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16614)
*Dehui Kong,Martin Feick,Shi Liu,Alexander Maedche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CoEmpaTeam是一个基于VR的系统，使用LLM驱动的不同性格化身进行认知共情训练，通过动态角色扮演帮助用户提升理解他人视角的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知共情（理解他人视角的能力）在绩效驱动型社会中正在下降，而传统训练方法面临挑战，因为这是一种微妙且难以感知的软技能。

Method: 开发了CoEmpaTeam系统，使用VR技术和LLM驱动的不同性格化身，通过动态角色扮演让用户从他人视角体验情境。系统包含三个经过技术评估和在线实验验证的显著不同性格的化身。

Result: 实验室实验（32名参与者，三周训练）显示认知共情显著提升，日记研究表明这种提升能够转移到参与者的真实生活中。

Conclusion: CoEmpaTeam系统通过VR和LLM驱动的角色扮演有效提升了用户的认知共情能力，且这种训练效果能够迁移到现实生活情境中。

Abstract: Cognitive empathy, the ability to understand others' perspectives, is essential for effective communication, reducing biases, and constructive negotiation. However, this skill is declining in a performance-driven society, which prioritizes efficiency over perspective-taking. Here, the training of cognitive empathy is challenging because it is a subtle, hard-to-perceive soft skill. To address this, we developed CoEmpaTeam, a VR-based system that enables users to train their cognitive empathy by using LLM-driven avatars with different personalities. Through dynamic role play, users actively engage in perspective-taking, experiencing situations through another person's eyes. CoEmpaTeam deploys three avatars who significantly differ in their personality, validated by a technical evaluation and an online experiment (n=90). Next, we evaluated the system through a lab experiment with 32 participants who performed three sessions across two weeks, followed by a one-week diary study. Our results showed a significant increase in cognitive empathy, which, according to participants, transferred into their real lives.

</details>


### [62] [Why We Need to Destroy the Illusion of Speaking to A Human: Critical Reflections On Ethics at the Front-End for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16633)
*Sarah Diefenbach,Daniel Ullrich*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了基于大语言模型（如ChatGPT）的人机对话与真实人际对话的相似性与差异，并指出了这种表面相似性带来的伦理挑战，提出了更符合伦理的前端AI设计思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的聊天机器人（如ChatGPT）在日常生活中的广泛应用，人机对话变得越来越自然，类似于真实的人际对话。然而，这种表面相似性可能引发对AI的误解，带来伦理挑战。作者旨在分析人机对话与人际对话的异同，为更符合伦理的AI前端设计提供理论基础。

Method: 论文采用比较分析方法，系统性地对比了人机对话与人际对话在多个维度上的相似性和差异性。作者从对话结构、交互模式、认知机制等方面进行分析，识别出可能导致用户对AI产生误解的关键特征。

Result: 研究发现，虽然人机对话在表面形式上与人际对话相似，但在本质上有显著差异：AI缺乏真实的情感、意图和意识；对话是基于统计模式而非真实理解；AI没有持续的身份认同和记忆一致性。这些差异被表面的相似性所掩盖，可能导致用户对AI能力产生不切实际的期望和误解。

Conclusion: 论文强调需要更符合伦理的AI前端设计，通过明确揭示AI的局限性、避免过度拟人化、提供透明度等方式，减少用户对AI的误解。这种设计不仅有助于建立更健康的用户-AI关系，还能促进对AI技术的理性认知和负责任使用。

Abstract: Conversation with chatbots based on Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT has become one of the major forms of interaction with Artificial Intelligence (AI) in everyday life. What makes this interaction so convenient is that interacting with LLMs feels so natural, and resembles what we know from real, human conversations. At the same time, this seeming similarity is part of one of the ethical challenges of AI design, since it activates many misleading ideas about AI. We discuss similarities and differences between human-AI-conversations and interpersonal conversation and highlight starting points for more ethical design of AI at the front-end.

</details>


### [63] [Whose Knowledge Counts? Co-Designing Community-Centered AI Auditing Tools with Educators in Hawai`i](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16646)
*Dora Zhao,Hannah Cha,Michael J. Ryan,Angelina Wang,Rachel Baker-Ramos Evyn-Bree Helekahi-Kaiwi,Rebecca Diego,Josiah Hester,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨生成式AI在教育中的文化误代表现问题，特别是在夏威夷土著教育环境中，通过教师共同设计工作坊提出基于文化价值的AI审计工具设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI被引入课堂但存在文化误代表现和偏见问题，特别是在低资源语言和土著教育环境中AI系统表现不佳，夏威夷公立学校有整合夏威夷语言和文化的全州性要求，需要解决这些挑战

Method: 通过四个共同设计工作坊与22名公立学校教师合作，收集对生成式AI在教育中使用的担忧，特别是文化误代表现问题，并设计相应的审计工具

Result: 教师设想的工具基于特定的夏威夷文化价值观和实践，如追溯知识谱系；研究将AI审计概念化为社区导向过程而非孤立个体工作

Conclusion: AI审计应被视为社区导向过程，设计审计工具需要考虑特定文化价值观和实践，这对解决生成式AI在教育中的文化误代表现问题具有重要意义

Abstract: Although generative AI is being deployed into classrooms with promises of aiding teachers, educators caution that these tools can have unintended pedagogical repercussions, including cultural misrepresentation and bias. These concerns are heightened in low-resource language and Indigenous education settings, where AI systems frequently underperform. We investigate these challenges in Hawai`i, where public schools operate under a statewide mandate to integrate Hawaiian language and culture into education. Through four co-design workshops with 22 public school educators, we surfaced concerns about using generative AI in educational settings, particularly around cultural misrepresentation, and corresponding designs for auditing tools that address these issues. We find that educators envision tools grounded in specific Hawaiian cultural values and practices, such as tracing the genealogy of knowledge in source materials. Building on these insights, we conceptualize AI auditing as a community-oriented process rather than the work of isolated individuals, and discuss implications for designing auditing tools.

</details>

### [64] [Accelerating Approximate Analytical Join Queries over Unstructured Data with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16153)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Tengjun Jin,Chenghao Mo,Daniel Kang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BaS（阻塞增强采样）是一种结合嵌入阻塞和采样的混合方法，用于高效执行无结构化数据上的分析连接查询，同时提供统计保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无结构化数据分析连接查询日益普遍，但使用机器学习模型对表间所有数据对进行标记的成本过高。现有方法要么缺乏统计保证（导致误差高达79%），要么效率低下如均匀采样。

Method: BaS通过嵌入阻塞和采样的最优协调来缓解各自局限。它将数据元组分配到两个机制：假阴性机制使用采样估计结果，假阳性机制应用嵌入阻塞提高效率。采用两阶段算法自适应分配ML执行预算以最小化估计误差。

Result: 理论证明BaS渐近优于或匹配独立采样。在真实世界多模态数据集上，BaS提供有效置信区间，相比最先进基线将估计误差降低高达19倍。

Conclusion: BaS同时实现了统计保证和高效率，通过协调嵌入阻塞和采样解决了无结构化数据分析连接查询中的成本与精度权衡问题。

Abstract: Analytical join queries over unstructured data are increasingly prevalent in data analytics. Applying machine learning (ML) models to label every pair in the cross product of tables can achieve state-of-the-art accuracy, but the cost of pairwise execution of ML models is prohibitive. Existing algorithms, such as embedding-based blocking and sampling, aim to reduce this cost. However, they either fail to provide statistical guarantees (leading to errors up to 79% higher than expected) or become as inefficient as uniform sampling.
  We propose blocking-augmented sampling (BaS), which simultaneously achieves statistical guarantees and high efficiency. BaS optimally orchestrates embedding-based blocking and sampling to mitigate their respective limitations. Specifically, BaS allocates data tuples in the cross product into two regimes based on the failure modes of embeddings. In the regime of false negatives, BaS uses sampling to estimate the result. In the regime of false positives, BaS applies embedding-based blocking to improve efficiency. To minimize the estimation error given a budget for ML executions, we design a novel two-stage algorithm that adaptively allocates the budget between blocking and sampling. Theoretically, we prove that BaS asymptotically outperforms or matches standalone sampling. On real-world datasets across different modalities, we show that BaS provides valid confidence intervals and reduces estimation errors by up to 19$\times$, compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [65] [Dialect-Agnostic SQL Parsing via LLM-Based Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16155)
*Junwen An,Kabilan Mahathevan,Manuel Rigger*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SQLFlex是一个结合语法解析和LLM分割的SQL查询重写框架，能够鲁棒地解析多种SQL方言，在SQL代码检查和测试用例简化等任务中显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于SQL方言的多样性，现有的SQL分析和重写工具在处理特定方言语法时经常失败。虽然大型语言模型在理解SQL查询方面表现出潜力，但其处理层次结构的能力有限且存在幻觉风险，限制了在解析中的直接应用。

Method: 提出SQLFlex框架，将基于语法的解析与基于LLM的分割相结合。核心思想是将层次化解析分解为顺序分割任务，这更符合LLM的优势，并通过验证检查提高输出可靠性。具体使用子句级分割和表达式级分割两种策略，在不同层次上分解查询元素。

Result: 在SQL代码检查中，SQLFlex在ANSI模式下比SQLFluff的F1分数高出63.68%，同时与其方言特定模式性能相当。在测试用例简化中，SQLFlex比SQLess的简化率高出最多10倍。在独立评估中，它在八种不同方言上解析了91.55%到100%的查询，优于所有基线解析器。

Conclusion: SQLFlex能够鲁棒地解析多种SQL方言，可以作为许多查询分析和重写用例的基础框架，有效解决了现有工具在处理SQL方言多样性时的局限性。

Abstract: SQL is a widely adopted language for querying data, which has led to the development of various SQL analysis and rewriting tools. However, due to the diversity of SQL dialects, such tools often fail when encountering unrecognized dialect-specific syntax. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in understanding SQL queries, their inherent limitations in handling hierarchical structures and hallucination risks limit their direct applicability in parsing. To address these limitations, we propose SQLFlex, a novel query rewriting framework that integrates grammar-based parsing with LLM-based segmentation to parse diverse SQL dialects robustly. Our core idea is to decompose hierarchical parsing to sequential segmentation tasks, which better aligns with the strength of LLMs and improves output reliability through validation checks. Specifically, SQLFlex uses clause-level segmentation and expression-level segmentation as two strategies that decompose elements on different levels of a query. We extensively evaluated SQLFlex on both real-world use cases and in a standalone evaluation. In SQL linting, SQLFlex outperforms SQLFluff in ANSI mode by 63.68% in F1 score while matching its dialect-specific mode performance. In test-case reduction, SQLFlex outperforms SQLess by up to 10 times in simplification rate. In the standalone evaluation, it parses 91.55% to 100% of queries across eight distinct dialects, outperforming all baseline parsers. We believe SQLFlex can serve as a foundation for many query analysis and rewriting use cases.

</details>


### [66] [Work Sharing and Offloading for Efficient Approximate Threshold-based Vector Join](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16360)
*Kyoungmin Kim,Lennart Roth,Liang Liang,Anastasia Ailamaki*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出统一框架用于高效近似向量连接，通过软工作共享、合并索引和自适应混合搜索策略，显著提升效率-召回率权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量连接是现代向量和向量关系数据库系统的核心操作，用于多模态检索和语义分析。现有最先进方法虽然利用相似查询间的工作共享，但仍存在冗余索引遍历和过多距离计算的问题

Method: 提出统一框架包含三个关键技术：(1) 软工作共享：重用先前查询的遍历结果，而不仅仅是连接结果；(2) 合并索引：在查询向量和数据向量上构建统一索引，加速图探索；(3) 自适应混合搜索策略：提高对分布外查询的鲁棒性

Result: 在八个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在效率-召回率权衡方面相比现有最先进方法有显著改进

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架通过软工作共享、合并索引和自适应混合搜索策略，有效解决了向量连接中的冗余计算问题，显著提升了近似向量连接的效率和鲁棒性

Abstract: Vector joins - finding all vector pairs between a set of query and data vectors whose distances are below a given threshold - are fundamental to modern vector and vector-relational database systems that power multimodal retrieval and semantic analytics. Existing state-of-the-art approach exploits work sharing among similar queries but still suffers from redundant index traversals and excessive distance computations. We propose a unified framework for efficient approximate vector joins that (1) introduces soft work sharing to reuse traversal results beyond the join results of previous queries, (2) builds a merged index over both query and data vectors to further speedup graph explorations, and (3) improves robustness for out-of-distribution queries through an adaptive hybrid search strategy. Experiments on eight datasets demonstrate substantial improvements in efficiency-recall trade-off over the state of the art.

</details>


### [67] [Practical MCTS-based Query Optimization: A Reproducibility Study and new MCTS algorithm for complex queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16474)
*Vladimir Burlakov,Alena Rybakina,Sergey Kudashev,Konstantin Gilev,Alexander Demin,Denis Ponomaryov,Yuriy Dorn*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文对基于MCTS的查询优化方法进行可复现性研究，发现现有方法因依赖学习成本模型而存在泛化问题，提出使用数据库标准内部成本模型结合改进的Extreme UCT策略的新MCTS框架，在复杂连接场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MCTS的查询优化方法（如AlphaJoin和HyperQO）虽然声称优于传统方法，但依赖学习成本模型，存在泛化能力和部署准备度问题。论文旨在验证这些方法的可复现性，并解决其不稳定性问题。

Method: 1）对AlphaJoin和HyperQO进行全面的可复现性研究；2）通过消融研究诊断问题根源；3）提出新MCTS框架，使用数据库标准内部成本模型而非学习模型，并引入Extreme UCT选择策略增强搜索鲁棒性。

Result: 实验使用Join Order Benchmark（JOB）和新JOB-Complex基准，对比原始AlphaJoin、HyperQO、动态规划（DP）、遗传查询优化（GEQO）等方法。结果显示：1）现有学习MCTS方法在多样化工作负载下无法保持声称的性能优势；2）新方法优于学习MCTS方法，并在复杂连接场景中超越SOTA查询优化器。

Conclusion: 基于MCTS的查询优化方法的核心问题在于学习成本模型的泛化误差，而非搜索策略本身。使用数据库标准内部成本模型结合改进的Extreme UCT策略能提供更稳定、可部署的解决方案，在复杂连接场景中表现优异。论文开源了完整实现和实验材料。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) has been proposed as a transformative approach to join-order optimization in database query processing, with recent frameworks such as AlphaJoin and HyperQO claiming to outperform traditional methods. However, the fact that these frameworks rely on learned cost models raises concerns related to generalizability and deployment readiness. This paper presents a comprehensive reproducibility study of these methods, revealing that they often fail to support the claimed performance gains when subjected to diverse workloads.
  Through an ablation study, we diagnose the root cause of this instability: while the MCTS search strategy is effective, the accompanying learned cost models suffer from severe out-of-distribution generalization errors.
  Addressing this, we propose a novel MCTS framework. Unlike prior methods that rely on unstable learned components, our approach utilizes the database standard internal cost model, augmented by a new Extreme UCT (Upper Confidence Bound applied to Trees) selection policy to navigate the search space more robustly. We benchmark our method against the original AlphaJoin and HyperQO, as well as industry-standard baselines including Dynamic Programming (DP) and Genetic Query Optimization (GEQO), using the well-known Join Order Benchmark (JOB) and the new JOB-Complex benchmark. The results demonstrate that our approach outperforms learned MCTS methods and achieves superiority over a SOTA query optimizer in complex join scenarios on real-world data. We release the full implementation and experimental artifacts to support further research.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [Tokenization Tradeoffs in Structured EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15644)
*Lin Lawrence Guo,Santiago Eduardo Arciniegas,Joseph Jihyung Lee,Adam Paul Yan,George Tomlinson,Jason Fries,Lillian Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究评估了结构化电子健康记录（EHR）基础模型中分词策略对下游性能和计算效率的影响，发现联合事件编码和位置时间编码在74个临床预测任务中表现最优且计算更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EHR基础模型主要关注预训练架构和策略，但分词策略——如何将时间序列临床事件转换为离散模型输入——对信息保留、编码效率以及模型需要学习的关系有决定性影响，其设计选择对下游性能和计算效率的影响尚未充分探索。

Method: 采用因子设计方法，在儿科EHR数据上预训练Transformer模型，系统变化三个分词维度：事件编码（联合vs分离）、时间编码（位置vs绝对）、工作流标注（有vs无）。在74个临床预测任务上评估性能，并通过针对性消融实验分析优势来源。

Result: 联合事件编码在73/74任务中优于分离编码，且减少39.5%预训练浮点运算；位置时间编码在71/74任务中优于绝对编码，减少9.6%运算。消融实验表明联合编码优势源于局部绑定效率——将代码-属性对组合为单个token，而非需要模型在预训练中学习关联的分割token。外部验证显示该优势在成人ICU队列中具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 分词策略是改进EHR基础模型性能和效率的可控杠杆，联合事件编码和位置时间编码是最优选择。局部绑定效率是关键机制，而时间和工作流效应更具机构特异性。这为EHR模型设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Foundation models for structured electronic health records (EHRs) are pretrained on longitudinal sequences of timestamped clinical events to learn adaptable patient representations. Tokenization -- how these timelines are converted into discrete model inputs -- determines what information is preserved, how efficiently it is encoded, and which relationships must be learned versus precomputed. Yet the impact of tokenization design choices on downstream performance and computational efficiency remains largely unexplored. Here, we pretrained a transformer on pediatric EHR data under a factorial design, varying tokenization along event encoding, time encoding, and workflow annotation. We evaluated area-under-the-receiver-operating-characteristic curve across 74 clinical prediction tasks. Joint event encoding and positional time encoding outperformed their alternatives (73/74 and 71/74 tasks) while requiring 39.5% and 9.6% fewer pretraining floating-point operations, respectively. Targeted ablations traced the joint encoding advantage to local binding efficiency, that is, code-attribute pairs are combined into single tokens, rather than split across tokens that the model must learn to associate during pretraining. External evaluation on an adult intensive care unit cohort demonstrated that this advantage generalizes despite substantial vocabulary mismatch, while temporal and workflow effects remain institution-specific. These results establish tokenization as a tractable lever for improving both the performance and efficiency of EHR foundation models.

</details>


### [69] [Game-Theory-Assisted Reinforcement Learning for Border Defense: Early Termination based on Analytical Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15907)
*Goutam Das,Michael Dorothy,Kyle Volle,Daigo Shishika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合博弈论和强化学习的混合方法，通过阿波罗尼奥斯圆计算检测后阶段的均衡，实现早期终止RL训练，专注于学习搜索策略，在边境防御游戏中获得更高奖励和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博弈论在对抗性分析中提供最优性保证，但在信息不完全等假设被违反时变得脆弱；强化学习具有适应性但在复杂领域样本效率低。需要结合两者优势，在边境防御等有限感知范围的场景中提高训练效率。

Method: 使用阿波罗尼奥斯圆计算检测后阶段的均衡，实现RL训练的早期终止，避免学习追击动态。RL专注于学习搜索策略，检测后阶段保证最优继续。应用于单防御者和多防御者设置。

Result: 早期终止方法在单防御者和多防御者设置中均获得10-20%的更高奖励、更快收敛和更高效的搜索轨迹。大量实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的混合方法成功结合了博弈论的最优性保证和强化学习的适应性，在有限感知范围的边境防御游戏中显著提高了训练效率和性能，为类似问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Game theory provides the gold standard for analyzing adversarial engagements, offering strong optimality guarantees. However, these guarantees often become brittle when assumptions such as perfect information are violated. Reinforcement learning (RL), by contrast, is adaptive but can be sample-inefficient in large, complex domains. This paper introduces a hybrid approach that leverages game-theoretic insights to improve RL training efficiency. We study a border defense game with limited perceptual range, where defender performance depends on both search and pursuit strategies, making classical differential game solutions inapplicable. Our method employs the Apollonius Circle (AC) to compute equilibrium in the post-detection phase, enabling early termination of RL episodes without learning pursuit dynamics. This allows RL to concentrate on learning search strategies while guaranteeing optimal continuation after detection. Across single- and multi-defender settings, this early termination method yields 10-20% higher rewards, faster convergence, and more efficient search trajectories. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate the overall effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [70] [XLinear: Frequency-Enhanced MLP with CrossFilter for Robust Long-Range Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15645)
*Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XLinear是一种基于MLP的长序列预测模型，通过分解时间序列为趋势和季节性成分，分别使用增强频率注意力（EFA）和交叉滤波器块（CrossFilter Block）来捕获长期依赖并保持对噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLP-based预测器相比Transformer-based预测器对噪声更鲁棒，但难以捕获复杂特征和长期依赖关系。需要设计一种既能保持MLP轻量化和高鲁棒性优势，又能有效捕获长期依赖的预测模型。

Method: 1. 将时间序列分解为趋势成分和季节性成分；2. 针对包含长期特征的趋势成分，设计增强频率注意力（EFA）模块，利用频域操作捕获长期依赖；3. 针对季节性成分，提出交叉滤波器块（CrossFilter Block），避免注意力机制常导致的低鲁棒性问题，保持模型对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明XLinear在测试数据集上达到了最先进的性能。在保持MLP-based模型轻量级架构和高鲁棒性的同时，在捕获长期依赖方面优于其他MLP-based预测器。

Conclusion: XLinear成功解决了MLP-based预测器在捕获长期依赖方面的局限性，通过创新的频域注意力机制和交叉滤波器设计，实现了在保持鲁棒性的同时提升长期依赖建模能力，为时间序列预测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasters are widely used across various domains. Among them, MLP (multi-layer perceptron)-based forecasters have been proven to be more robust to noise compared to Transformer-based forecasters. However, MLP struggles to capture complex features, resulting in limitations on capturing long-range dependencies. To address this challenge, we propose XLinear, an MLP-based forecaster for long-range forecasting. Firstly, we decompose the time series into trend and seasonal components. For the trend component which contains long-range characteristics, we design Enhanced Frequency Attention (EFA) to capture long-term dependencies by leveraging frequency-domain operations. Additionally, a CrossFilter Block is proposed for the seasonal component to maintain the model's robustness to noise, avoiding the problems of low robustness often caused by attention mechanisms. Experimental results demonstrate that XLinear achieves state-of-the-art performance on test datasets. While keeping the lightweight architecture and high robustness of MLP-based models, our forecaster outperforms other MLP-based forecasters in capturing long-range dependencies.

</details>


### [71] [Alternating Reinforcement Learning with Contextual Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15646)
*Guangchen Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARL-RR提出了一种交替强化学习框架，通过逐个优化语义评分元类来避免固定标量化，解决了传统RLRR中线性压缩向量奖励的局限性，在健康领域数据集上表现出更好的性能和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLRR方法使用线性压缩将向量奖励转换为标量奖励，这种方法对人工评分设计敏感且无法捕捉奖励维度间的相关性，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出交替强化学习与评分奖励框架，消除固定标量化需求，逐个优化语义评分元类；引入轻量级搜索自适应过程动态选择下一个元类，使策略能强调关键目标。

Result: 在HealthBench数据集上的实验表明，ARL-RR在不同模型规模下均优于标量化方法，在模型性能和训练效率方面都有提升；理论分析显示奖励聚合具有方差收缩效应。

Conclusion: ARL-RR通过交替优化评分元类有效解决了传统RLRR的局限性，提供了一种更灵活、高效的强化学习框架，特别适用于需要多维度评估的复杂任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards (RLRR) is a framework that extends conventional reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and verifiable rewards (RLVR) by replacing scalar preference signals with structured, multi-dimensional, contextual rubric-based evaluations. However, existing approaches in RLRR are limited to linearly compressing vector rewards into a scalar reward with a fixed weightings, which is sensitive to artificial score design and fails to capture correlations among reward dimensions. To overcome the limitations of reward aggregation, this work proposes Alternating Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards (ARL-RR), a framework that eliminates the need for a fixed scalarization by optimizing one semantic rubric meta-class at a time. Theoretically, we show that reward aggregation induces a variance contraction effect, which helps explain the performance gains. We further introduce a lightweight, search-based adaptation procedure that selects the next meta-class dynamically based on task performance, enabling the policy to emphasize critical objectives and thereby improve the model performance. Empirically, our experiments on the HealthBench dataset with experts annotations demonstrate that ARL-RR uniformly outperforms scalarized methods in both model performance and training efficiency across different model scales (1.7B, 4B, 8B, and 14B).

</details>


### [72] [Steering Frozen LLMs: Adaptive Social Alignment via Online Prompt Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15647)
*Zeyu Zhang,Xiangxiang Dai,Ziyi Han,Xutong Liu,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CCLUB框架通过系统提示路由实现自适应社会对齐，使用共识聚类机制在效用和安全相似图交集内聚合数据，防止跨语义相近但风险不同情境的不安全泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练对齐（如RLHF或DPO）产生静态部署策略，但现实世界安全是全生命周期问题：静态防御无法应对不断演变的越狱行为，固定权重无法适应多元化、时变的安全规范，需要无需昂贵重训练即可在推理时引导行为的治理方法。

Method: 提出共识聚类LinUCB Bandit（CCLUB）框架，通过系统提示路由实现自适应社会对齐。采用保守共识聚类机制：仅在效用和安全相似图的交集内聚合数据，防止跨语义相近但风险不同情境的不安全泛化。理论分析提供次线性遗憾保证。

Result: 广泛实验验证CCLUB优于强基线，累积奖励提升10.98%，平均次优差距减少14.42%。理论分析证明CCLUB具有次线性遗憾保证，展示其接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: CCLUB为LLM推理时治理提供统一框架，通过系统提示路由实现自适应社会对齐，保守共识聚类机制有效防止不安全泛化，理论保证和实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically governed by post-training alignment (e.g., RLHF or DPO), which yields a largely static policy during deployment and inference. However, real-world safety is a full-lifecycle problem: static defenses degrade against evolving jailbreak behaviors, and fixed weights cannot adapt to pluralistic, time-varying safety norms. This motivates inference-time governance that steers behavior without costly retraining. To address this, we introduce the Consensus Clustering LinUCB Bandit (CCLUB), a unified framework for adaptive social alignment via system-prompt routing. CCLUB employs a conservative consensus clustering mechanism: it pools data only within the intersection of utility and safety similarity graphs, effectively preventing unsafe generalization across semantically proximal but risk-divergent contexts. Our theoretical analysis yields a sublinear regret guarantee, demonstrating near-optimal performance of CCLUB. Extensive experiments validate that CCLUB outperforms strong baselines, achieving a 10.98% improvement in cumulative reward and a 14.42% reduction in the average suboptimality gap.

</details>


### [73] [Optimal uncertainty bounds for multivariate kernel regression under bounded noise: A Gaussian process-based dual function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16481)
*Amon Lahr,Anna Scampicchio,Johannes Köhler,Melanie N. Zeilinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于多输出核方法估计的紧致、无分布不确定性边界，可轻松集成到下游优化任务中


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法不确定性边界存在以下问题：对噪声分布假设过强、过于保守、多输出情况下扩展性差、难以集成到下游任务中。这些限制阻碍了基于学习的控制系统的安全应用。

Method: 通过无约束的对偶公式推导出多输出核基估计的紧致无分布边界。该方法与经典高斯过程置信边界结构相同，可直接集成到下游优化流程中。

Result: 提出的边界推广了许多现有结果，并通过四旋翼动力学学习的实例展示了其应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种紧致、无分布且易于集成的多输出核方法不确定性边界，有助于提升基于学习的控制系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Non-conservative uncertainty bounds are essential for making reliable predictions about latent functions from noisy data--and thus, a key enabler for safe learning-based control. In this domain, kernel methods such as Gaussian process regression are established techniques, thanks to their inherent uncertainty quantification mechanism. Still, existing bounds either pose strong assumptions on the underlying noise distribution, are conservative, do not scale well in the multi-output case, or are difficult to integrate into downstream tasks. This paper addresses these limitations by presenting a tight, distribution-free bound for multi-output kernel-based estimates. It is obtained through an unconstrained, duality-based formulation, which shares the same structure of classic Gaussian process confidence bounds and can thus be straightforwardly integrated into downstream optimization pipelines. We show that the proposed bound generalizes many existing results and illustrate its application using an example inspired by quadrotor dynamics learning.

</details>


### [74] [How to Achieve Prototypical Birth and Death for OOD Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15650)
*Ningkang Peng,Qianfeng Yu,Xiaoqian Peng,Linjing Qian,Yafei Liu,Canran Xiao,Xinyu Lu,Tingyu Lu,Zhichao Zheng,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于原型生死机制的动态原型学习方法PID，根据数据复杂度自适应调整原型数量，显著提升OOD检测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原型的OOD检测方法通常使用固定数量的原型，无法适应不同类别固有的复杂度差异，缺乏根据数据复杂度自适应调整原型数量的机制

Method: 受生物学细胞生死过程启发，提出PID方法，包含两个动态机制：原型出生（在表示不足的数据区域实例化新原型）和原型死亡（通过评估可区分性修剪边界模糊的原型），动态调整原型数量

Result: 在CIFAR-100等基准测试中显著优于现有方法，达到SOTA性能，特别是在FPR95指标上表现突出

Conclusion: 通过动态原型生死机制自适应调整原型数量，能够学习更紧凑、更好分离的ID嵌入，显著增强OOD样本检测能力

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for the secure deployment of machine learning models, and prototype-based learning methods are among the mainstream strategies for achieving OOD detection. Existing prototype-based learning methods generally rely on a fixed number of prototypes. This static assumption fails to adapt to the inherent complexity differences across various categories. Currently, there is still a lack of a mechanism that can adaptively adjust the number of prototypes based on data complexity. Inspired by the processes of cell birth and death in biology, we propose a novel method named PID (Prototype bIrth and Death) to adaptively adjust the prototype count based on data complexity. This method relies on two dynamic mechanisms during the training process: prototype birth and prototype death. The birth mechanism instantiates new prototypes in data regions with insufficient representation by identifying the overload level of existing prototypes, thereby meticulously capturing intra-class substructures. Conversely, the death mechanism reinforces the decision boundary by pruning prototypes with ambiguous class boundaries through evaluating their discriminability. Through birth and death, the number of prototypes can be dynamically adjusted according to the data complexity, leading to the learning of more compact and better-separated In-Distribution (ID) embeddings, which significantly enhances the capability to detect OOD samples. Experiments demonstrate that our dynamic method, PID, significantly outperforms existing methods on benchmarks such as CIFAR-100, achieving State-of-the-Art (SOTA) performance, especially on the FPR95 metric.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Reward Suppression: Reshaping Steganographic Communication Protocols in MARL via Dynamic Representational Circuit Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15655)
*Liu Hung Ming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DRCB防御机制，通过VQ-VAE瓶颈将不可观测消息转换为可审计统计对象，检测隐写合谋，在优化层防止多智能体系统逃避监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化多智能体强化学习中存在隐写合谋威胁，现有防御机制仅关注行为或奖励层，无法检测潜在通信通道中的协调行为，需要底层优化层的防御方案。

Method: 基于AI母语框架，使用VQ-VAE瓶颈转换不可观测消息为可审计统计对象，监控Jensen-Shannon散度漂移、L2范数码本位移和随机观察者池准确率，计算EMA合谋分数，触发四级干预措施。

Result: 在MNIST标签的上下文囚徒困境实验中，DRCB将观察者平均准确率从0.858提升至0.938（+9.3%），波动性降低43%，同时保持平均联合奖励（p=0.854）。分析214,298个符号样本确认"语义退化"现象。

Conclusion: DRCB提供任务无关的方法论，为自主系统部署前审计提供技术路径，识别"透明度悖论"现象，反映古德哈特定律，实现MICA合规的多智能体内耦合审计。

Abstract: In decentralized Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), steganographic collusion -- where agents develop private protocols to evade monitoring -- presents a critical AI safety threat. Existing defenses, limited to behavioral or reward layers, fail to detect coordination in latent communication channels. We introduce the Dynamic Representational Circuit Breaker (DRCB), an architectural defense operating at the optimization substrate.
  Building on the AI Mother Tongue (AIM) framework, DRCB utilizes a Vector Quantized Variational Autoencoder (VQ-VAE) bottleneck to convert unobservable messages into auditable statistical objects. DRCB monitors signals including Jensen-Shannon Divergence drift, L2-norm codebook displacement, and Randomized Observer Pool accuracy to compute an EMA-based Collusion Score. Threshold breaches trigger four escalating interventions: dynamic adaptation, gradient-space penalty injection into the Advantage function A^pi, temporal reward suppression, and full substrate circuit breaking via codebook shuffling and optimizer state reset.
  Experiments on a Contextual Prisoner's Dilemma with MNIST labels show that while static monitoring fails (p = 0.3517), DRCB improves observer mean accuracy from 0.858 to 0.938 (+9.3 percent) and reduces volatility by 43 percent, while preserving mean joint reward (p = 0.854). Analysis of 214,298 symbol samples confirms "Semantic Degradation," where high-frequency sequences converge to zero entropy, foreclosing complex steganographic encodings. We identify a "Transparency Paradox" where agents achieve surface-level determinism while preserving residual capacity in long-tail distributions, reflecting Goodhart's Law. This task-agnostic methodology provides a technical path toward MICA-compliant (Multi-Agent Internal Coupling Audit) pre-deployment auditing for autonomous systems.

</details>


### [76] [A federated learning framework with knowledge graph and temporal transformer for early sepsis prediction in multi-center ICUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15651)
*Yue Chang,Guangsen Lin,Jyun Jie Chuang,Shunqi Liu,Xinkui Li,Yaozheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合联邦学习、医学知识图谱和时间Transformer的元学习框架，用于多中心ICU脓毒症早期预测，在保护隐私的同时显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU脓毒症早期预测对提高生存率至关重要，但面临数据分散、医疗数据复杂时序性以及严格隐私限制等挑战，需要开发既能保护隐私又能准确预测的多中心协作方案。

Method: 提出新颖框架，整合联邦学习（FL）实现多医院协作训练而不共享原始数据；利用医学知识图谱融入结构化医疗关系；采用时间Transformer捕捉临床时间序列数据的长期依赖；结合模型无关元学习（MAML）策略促进全局模型快速适应本地数据分布。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上评估，AUC达到0.956，相比传统集中式模型提升22.4%，相比标准联邦学习提升12.7%，展现了强大的脓毒症预测能力。
