*Andrea Moleri,Christian Internò,Ali Raza,Markus Olhofer,David Klindt,Fabio Stella,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FederatedFactory：一种将联邦学习单元从判别参数反转为生成先验的零依赖框架，通过单轮通信交换生成模块，支持从零合成全局类别平衡数据集，消除梯度冲突和外部先验偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在本地标签分布互斥的联邦学习场景中，标准的权重聚合方法因优化轨迹冲突而失效。现有方法通常依赖预训练基础模型，引入了不现实的假设。

Method: 提出FederatedFactory框架，将联邦单元从判别参数反转为生成先验。通过单轮通信交换生成模块，支持从零合成全局类别平衡数据集，完全消除梯度冲突和外部先验偏差。

Result: 在MedMNIST和ISIC2019等医学影像基准测试中，该方法恢复了集中式上限性能。在极端异构场景下，将CIFAR-10基线准确率从崩溃的11.36%提升至90.57%，并将ISIC2019 AUROC恢复至90.57%。

Conclusion: FederatedFactory通过生成先验的联邦交换，有效解决了标签分布互斥下的联邦学习问题，消除了梯度冲突和外部先验偏差，同时支持通过确定性删除特定生成模块实现精确的模块化遗忘。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed optimization without compromising data sovereignty. Yet, where local label distributions are mutually exclusive, standard weight aggregation fails due to conflicting optimization trajectories. Often, FL methods rely on pretrained foundation models, introducing unrealistic assumptions. We introduce FederatedFactory, a zero-dependency framework that inverts the unit of federation from discriminative parameters to generative priors. By exchanging generative modules in a single communication round, our architecture supports ex nihilo synthesis of universally class balanced datasets, eliminating gradient conflict and external prior bias entirely. Evaluations across diverse medical imagery benchmarks, including MedMNIST and ISIC2019, demonstrate that our approach recovers centralized upper-bound performance. Under pathological heterogeneity, it lifts baseline accuracy from a collapsed 11.36% to 90.57% on CIFAR-10 and restores ISIC2019 AUROC to 90.57%. Additionally, this framework facilitates exact modular unlearning through the deterministic deletion of specific generative modules.

</details>


### [114] [DISCOVER: A Solver for Distributional Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16436)
*Yikai Gu,Lele Cao,Bo Zhao,Lei Lei,Lei You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISCOVER：一种模型无关的分布反事实解释求解器，通过稀疏提议-选择搜索范式替代梯度下降，支持不可微模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布反事实解释方法依赖梯度优化，但现实世界表格数据处理流程中许多模型不可微，限制了其应用范围

Method: 提出DISCOVER方法，保留原始DCE目标和统计认证，用稀疏提议-选择搜索替代梯度下降；利用运输目标的样本级分解计算每行影响分数，实施top-k干预预算；引入基于输入侧运输几何的OT引导锥形采样原语

Result: 在多个表格数据集上的实验表明，DISCOVER能实现输入和输出分布的强联合对齐，将分布反事实推理扩展到现代黑盒学习流程

Conclusion: DISCOVER成功解决了不可微模型的分布反事实解释问题，通过模型无关的搜索方法扩展了分布反事实推理的应用范围

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CE) explain model decisions by identifying input modifications that lead to different predictions. Most existing methods operate at the instance level. Distributional Counterfactual Explanations (DCE) extend this setting by optimizing an optimal transport objective that balances proximity to a factual input distribution and alignment to a target output distribution, with statistical certification via chance constrained bounds. However, DCE relies on gradient based optimization, while many real-world tabular pipelines are dominated by non-differentiable models. We propose DISCOVER, a model-agnostic solver for distributional counterfactual explanations. DISCOVER preserves the original DCE objective and certification while replacing gradient descent with a sparse propose-and-select search paradigm. It exploits a sample-wise decomposition of the transport objective to compute per-row impact scores and enforce a top-$k$ intervention budget, focusing edits on the most influential samples. To guide candidate generation without predictor gradients, DISCOVER introduces an OT-guided cone sampling primitive driven by input-side transport geometry. Experiments on multiple tabular datasets demonstrate strong joint alignment of input and output distributions, extending distributional counterfactual reasoning to modern black box learning pipelines. A code repository is available at https://github.com/understanding-ml/DCE.

</details>


### [115] [Capability-Guided Compression: Toward Interpretability-Aware Budget Allocation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16440)
*Rishaank Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了能力引导压缩(CGC)框架，通过稀疏自编码器(SAE)生成的能力密度图来分配不同压缩预算，解决现有压缩方法忽视模型组件功能编码的"能力盲压缩"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型压缩方法存在根本限制：压缩预算分配不考虑各个模型组件的功能编码内容，这导致了两个已知问题——基于困惑度的评估对推理能力损失不敏感，以及模型性能的突然相变。作者将这一问题称为"能力盲压缩"问题。

Method: 提出能力引导压缩(CGC)框架，使用稀疏自编码器(SAE)推导的能力密度图在Transformer组件间分配差异化的压缩预算。能力密度是一个形式化定义的标量度量，结合了组件SAE特征激活分布的特征广度、激活熵和跨输入一致性。

Result: 理论证明：能力密度更高的组件具有更低的结构冗余，并在更低的压缩比下达到各自的相变点。实验表明能力密度与Wanda重要性评分统计独立(Spearman rho = -0.054)，证明其是与现有重要性度量正交的新压缩信号。在GPT-2 Medium上的实验提供了关于PPL比较的负面结果，并诊断该模型不足以完全验证CGC假设。

Conclusion: 该研究为能力感知压缩研究建立了理论基础，包括理论框架、密度形式化和正交性发现。CGC框架为解决能力盲压缩问题提供了首个组件级相变预测的预压缩机制。

Abstract: Large language model compression has made substantial progress through pruning, quantization, and low-rank decomposition, yet a fundamental limitation persists across all existing methods: compression budgets are allocated without any representation of what individual model components functionally encode. We term this the capability-blind compression problem and argue it is a root cause of two well-documented failures -- the insensitivity of perplexity-based evaluation to reasoning capability loss, and the abrupt phase transitions in model performance recently characterized by Ma et al. (2026). We propose Capability-Guided Compression (CGC), a framework that addresses this by using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE)-derived capability density maps to allocate differential compression budgets across transformer components. Capability density is a formally defined scalar measure combining the feature breadth, activation entropy, and cross-input consistency of a component's SAE feature activation distribution. We prove theoretically that components with higher capability density exhibit lower structural redundancy and reach their individual phase transition points at lower compression ratios, providing the first pre-compression mechanism for component-level phase transition prediction. Experiments on GPT-2 Medium confirm that capability density is statistically independent of Wanda importance scores (Spearman rho = -0.054, n = 384 heads), establishing it as a genuinely novel compression signal orthogonal to all existing importance metrics. We report a negative result on PPL-based compression comparison and provide a principled diagnosis identifying GPT-2 Medium as an insufficient test bed for the full CGC hypothesis. The theoretical framework, density formalism, and orthogonality finding constitute a foundation for capability-aware compression research.

</details>


### [116] [From the Inside Out: Progressive Distribution Refinement for Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16500)
*Xizhong Yang,Yinan Xia,Huiming Wang,Mofei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DistriTTRL：一种基于模型置信度分布先验的强化学习方法，通过渐进优化奖励信号和多样性惩罚机制，解决测试-训练内部信息差异和奖励黑客问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型内部信息作为自奖励信号的强化学习方法存在两个主要问题：1) 测试与训练阶段内部信息差异未充分解决；2) 基于投票的测试时间训练策略常导致奖励黑客问题

Method: 提出DistriTTRL方法：1) 利用模型置信度的分布先验，通过渐进优化而非单查询rollouts来优化奖励信号；2) 引入多样性目标惩罚机制，缓解投票式TTS策略导致的持续奖励黑客现象

Result: DistriTTRL在多个模型和基准测试中实现了显著的性能提升，得益于模型能力与自奖励信号的互补训练机制以及奖励黑客问题的缓解

Conclusion: DistriTTRL通过分布先验驱动的渐进奖励优化和多样性惩罚，有效解决了测试-训练内部信息差异和奖励黑客问题，为基于模型内部信息的强化学习提供了更稳健的解决方案

Abstract: Leveraging the model's internal information as the self-reward signal in Reinforcement Learning (RL) has received extensive attention due to its label-free nature. While prior works have made significant progress in applying the Test-Time Scaling (TTS) strategies to RL, the discrepancy in internal information between test and training remains inadequately addressed. Moreover, Test-Time Training based on voting-based TTS strategies often suffers from reward hacking problems. To address these issues, we propose DistriTTRL, which leverages the distribution prior of the model's confidence during RL to progressively optimize the reward signal, rather than relying solely on single-query rollouts. Additionally, we mitigate the phenomenon of consistent reward hacking caused by the voting-based TTS strategies through diversity-targeted penalties. Benefiting from this training mechanism where model capability and self-reward signals complement each other, and the mitigation of reward hacking, DistriTTRL has achieved significant performance improvements across multiple models and benchmarks.

</details>


### [117] [FEAT: A Linear-Complexity Foundation Model for Extremely Large Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16513)
*Zhenghang Song,Tang Qian,Lu Chen,Yushuai Li,Zhengke Hu,Bingbing Fang,Yumeng Song,Junbo Zhao,Sheng Zhang,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FEAT提出了一种线性复杂度的结构化数据基础模型，通过多层双轴架构替代二次注意力，实现跨样本线性建模，在11个真实数据集上表现优异且推理速度快40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型结构化数据模型存在三个主要限制：1) 基于样本自注意力的O(N²)复杂度限制了样本数量；2) 线性序列模型因隐藏状态压缩和人为因果偏置导致表示退化；3) 纯合成预训练难以匹配真实世界分布。需要开发能够处理极大规模结构化数据的高效基础模型。

Method: FEAT采用多层双轴架构：1) 自适应融合双Mamba-2用于局部样本依赖建模；2) 卷积门控线性注意力用于全局记忆。结合混合结构因果模型管道和稳定重构目标，实现线性复杂度的跨样本建模。

Result: 在11个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，FEAT在零样本性能上持续优于基线模型，同时实现线性扩展，推理速度提升高达40倍。

Conclusion: FEAT成功解决了现有结构化数据基础模型的关键限制，通过创新的线性复杂度架构实现了高效的大规模结构化数据处理，为医疗、金融等领域的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Structured data is foundational to healthcare, finance, e-commerce, and scientific data management. Large structured-data models (LDMs) extend the foundation model paradigm to unify heterogeneous datasets for tasks such as classification, regression, and decision support. However, existing LDMs face major limitations. First, most rely on sample-wise self-attention, whose O(N^2) complexity limits the sample count. Second, linear sequence models often degrade representations due to hidden-state compression and artificial causal bias. Third, synthetic-only pre-training often fails to match real-world distributions. We propose FEAT, a linear-complexity foundation model for extremely large structured data. FEAT introduces a multi-layer dual-axis architecture that replaces quadratic attention with hybrid linear encoding. The architecture combines adaptive-fusion bi-Mamba-2 (AFBM) for local sample dependencies and convolutional gated linear attention (Conv-GLA) for global memory. This design enables linear-complexity cross-sample modeling while preserving expressive representations. To improve robustness, FEAT adopts a hybrid structural causal model pipeline and a stable reconstruction objective. Experiments on 11 real-world datasets show that FEAT consistently outperforms baselines in zero-shot performance, while scaling linearly and achieving up to 40x faster inference.

</details>


### [118] [SympFormer: Accelerated attention blocks via Inertial Dynamics on Density Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16535)
*Viktor Stein,Wuchen Li,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于惯性Nesterov动力学在密度空间上的加速注意力块，通过引入速度和特征变量，构建哈密顿动量注意力架构，相比经典注意力块收敛更快且保持查询次数不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将注意力块解释为粒子系统，其平均场极限对应概率密度空间上的梯度流。本文旨在扩展这一视角，通过引入惯性Nesterov型动力学来加速注意力机制，提高收敛速度。

Method: 引入携带空间（特征）和速度变量的token，通过时间离散化和加速密度动力学近似得到哈密顿动量注意力块。对于线性自注意力，证明注意力块近似于使用双线性核的Stein变分梯度流。

Result: 证明椭圆轮廓概率分布在加速注意力块下保持不变。提出的粒子基算法显示，加速注意力块比经典注意力块收敛更快，同时保持查询次数不变。

Conclusion: 成功将惯性Nesterov动力学引入注意力机制，构建了收敛更快的加速注意力架构，为Transformer模型提供了新的理论框架和实现方法。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their empirical success in natural language processing to the self-attention blocks. Recent perspectives interpret attention blocks as interacting particle systems, whose mean-field limits correspond to gradient flows of interaction energy functionals on probability density spaces equipped with Wasserstein-$2$-type metrics. We extend this viewpoint by introducing accelerated attention blocks derived from inertial Nesterov-type dynamics on density spaces. In our proposed architecture, tokens carry both spatial (feature) and velocity variables. The time discretization and the approximation of accelerated density dynamics yield Hamiltonian momentum attention blocks, which constitute the proposed accelerated attention architectures. In particular, for linear self-attention, we show that the attention blocks approximate a Stein variational gradient flow, using a bilinear kernel, of a potential energy. In this setting, we prove that elliptically contoured probability distributions are preserved by the accelerated attention blocks. We present implementable particle-based algorithms and demonstrate that the proposed accelerated attention blocks converge faster than the classical attention blocks while preserving the number of oracle calls.

</details>


### [119] [Manifold-Matching Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16568)
*Laurent Cheret,Vincent Létourneau,Isar Nejadgholi,Chris Drummond,Hussein Al Osman,Maia Fraser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Manifold-Matching Autoencoder (MMAE)的无监督正则化方法，通过最小化潜在空间与输入数据空间之间成对距离的均方误差来对齐流形结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自编码器正则化方法在保持数据流形结构方面存在不足，需要一种能够有效对齐潜在空间与原始数据空间几何结构的方法。

Method: MMAE通过最小化潜在空间与输入数据空间成对距离的均方误差来实现正则化。该方法基于距离对齐而非坐标对齐，因此可扩展到更低维的表示，增加了方法的灵活性。

Result: MMAE在基于最近邻距离保持和基于持久同调的度量上优于类似方法。同时观察到MMAE提供了多维缩放(MDS)的可扩展近似。

Conclusion: MMAE是一种简单有效的自编码器正则化方法，能够更好地保持数据的流形结构，并为MDS提供可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: We study a simple unsupervised regularization scheme for autoencoders called Manifold-Matching (MMAE): we align the pairwise distances in the latent space to those of the input data space by minimizing mean squared error. Because alignment occurs on pairwise distances rather than coordinates, it can also be extended to a lower-dimensional representation of the data, adding flexibility to the method. We find that this regularization outperforms similar methods on metrics based on preservation of nearest-neighbor distances and persistent homology-based measures. We also observe that MMAE provides a scalable approximation of Multi-Dimensional Scaling (MDS).

</details>


### [120] [When and Why Does Unsupervised RL Succeed in Mathematical Reasoning? A Manifold Envelopment Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16578)
*Zelin Zhang,Fei Cheng,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究无监督强化学习在数学推理中的应用，通过设计内在奖励机制促进简洁确定性的生成，探索方法边界，并引入几何诊断工具分析训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习虽然能提升大语言模型的数学推理能力，但依赖计算昂贵的地面真值标注，存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈。无监督强化学习提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，但存在训练动态不透明和灾难性不稳定性（如策略崩溃和奖励黑客）的问题。

Method: 1. 设计并评估一套明确强制简洁和确定性生成的内在奖励机制；2. 测试具有不同内在推理能力的基础模型，探索该方法的边界；3. 引入新颖的几何诊断视角，通过流形分析解释配置的稳定性。

Result: 研究发现强制简洁和确定性响应能成功提升数学推理能力，但揭示了该方法在某些情况下会失效的边界条件，并通过几何分析解释了成功配置被流形包围的现象。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅证明了强制简洁确定性响应能提升数学推理能力，更重要的是揭示了无监督方法在何时会失效，并通过几何诊断解释了其原因，为理解无监督强化学习的训练动态提供了新视角。

Abstract: Although outcome-based reinforcement learning (RL) significantly advances the mathematical reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), its reliance on computationally expensive ground-truth annotations imposes a severe scalability bottleneck. Unsupervised RL guided by intrinsic rewards offers a scalable alternative, yet it suffers from opaque training dynamics and catastrophic instability, such as policy collapse and reward hacking. In this paper, we first design and evaluate a suite of intrinsic rewards that explicitly enforce concise and certain generation. Second, to discover the boundaries of this approach, we test base models across a spectrum of intrinsic reasoning capabilities, revealing how a model's foundational logical prior dictates its success or failure. Finally, to demystify why certain configurations stabilize while others collapse, we introduce a novel geometric diagnostic lens, showing that successful cases are enveloped by manifolds. Ultimately, our work goes beyond merely demonstrating that enforcing concise and certain responses successfully boosts mathematical reasoning; we reveal when this unsupervised approach breaks down and geometrically diagnose why.

</details>


### [121] [Trajectory-Optimized Time Reparameterization for Learning-Compatible Reduced-Order Modeling of Stiff Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16583)
*Joe Standridge,Daniel Livescu,Paul Cizmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种轨迹优化的时间重参数化方法（TOTR），用于缓解机器学习降阶模型在处理刚性动力系统时的不稳定性问题，通过优化弧长坐标中的遍历速度剖面来改善训练动态的平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性动力系统对机器学习降阶模型构成挑战：显式时间积分在刚性区域不稳定，而隐式积分在学习循环中计算成本高且降低训练效率。现有时间重参数化方法对ML-ROM可学习性的影响尚未充分理解。

Method: 提出轨迹优化时间重参数化（TOTR）方法，将时间重参数化转化为弧长坐标中的优化问题，通过选择惩罚拉伸时间中加速度的遍历速度剖面来优化时间映射的平滑性。

Result: 在三个刚性问题上评估：参数化刚性线性系统、van der Pol振荡器和HIRES化学动力学模型。TOTR在所有情况下都产生更平滑的重参数化和改进的物理时间预测，相比基准算法实现1-2个数量级的损失减少。

Conclusion: ML-ROM中有效的刚性缓解关键取决于时间映射本身的规律性和可学习性，基于优化的时间重参数化为多尺度动力系统的显式降阶建模提供了稳健框架。

Abstract: Stiff dynamical systems present a challenge for machine-learning reduced-order models (ML-ROMs), as explicit time integration becomes unstable in stiff regimes while implicit integration within learning loops is computationally expensive and often degrades training efficiency. Time reparameterization (TR) offers an alternative by transforming the independent variable so that rapid physical-time transients are spread over a stretched-time coordinate, enabling stable explicit integration on uniformly sampled grids. Although several TR strategies have been proposed, their effect on learnability in ML-ROMs remains incompletely understood. This work investigates time reparameterization as a stiffness-mitigation mechanism for neural ODE reduced-order modeling and introduces a trajectory-optimized TR (TOTR) formulation. The proposed approach casts time reparameterization as an optimization problem in arc-length coordinates, in which a traversal-speed profile is selected to penalize acceleration in stretched time. By targeting the smoothness of the training dynamics, this formulation produces reparameterized trajectories that are better conditioned and easier to learn than existing TR methods. TOTR is evaluated on three stiff problems: a parameterized stiff linear system, the van der Pol oscillator, and the HIRES chemical kinetics model. Across all cases, the proposed approach yields smoother reparameterizations and improved physical-time predictions under identical training regimens than other TR approaches. Quantitative results demonstrate loss reductions of one to two orders of magnitude compared to benchmark algorithms. These results highlight that effective stiffness mitigation in ML-ROMs depends critically on the regularity and learnability of the time map itself, and that optimization-based TR provides a robust framework for explicit reduced-order modeling of multiscale dynamical systems.

</details>


### [122] [Simplex-to-Euclidean Bijection for Conjugate and Calibrated Multiclass Gaussian Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16621)
*Bernardo Williams,Harsha Vardhan Tetali,Arto Klami,Marcelo Hartmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于概率单纯形几何的共轭校准高斯过程多类分类模型，通过Aitchison几何将单纯形值类概率映射到无约束欧几里得表示，将分类转化为GP回归问题，实现共轭推断和可靠预测概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多类高斯过程分类器通常需要近似推断，缺乏共轭性且预测概率可能不可靠。本文旨在利用概率单纯形的几何结构，开发一种具有共轭推断和良好校准性能的多类分类方法。

Method: 使用Aitchison几何将K类概率单纯形映射到(K-1)维欧几里得空间，将多类分类转化为高斯过程回归问题。该方法保持了模型的共轭性，无需在模型构建中使用分布近似，且兼容标准稀疏GP回归技术以实现可扩展性。

Result: 经验结果表明，该方法在合成和真实数据集上表现出良好校准和竞争性性能。模型能够产生可靠的预测概率，并在保持共轭推断的同时实现可扩展的推理。

Conclusion: 通过利用概率单纯形的几何结构，成功开发了一种具有共轭推断、良好校准和可扩展性的多类高斯过程分类器，为多类分类问题提供了可靠的贝叶斯解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a conjugate and calibrated Gaussian process (GP) model for multi-class classification by exploiting the geometry of the probability simplex. Our approach uses Aitchison geometry to map simplex-valued class probabilities to an unconstrained Euclidean representation, turning classification into a GP regression problem with fewer latent dimensions than standard multi-class GP classifiers. This yields conjugate inference and reliable predictive probabilities without relying on distributional approximations in the model construction. The method is compatible with standard sparse GP regression techniques, enabling scalable inference on larger datasets. Empirical results show well-calibrated and competitive performance across synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [123] [Self-Aware Markov Models for Discrete Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16661)
*Gregor Kornhardt,Jannis Chemseddine,Christian Wald,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习马尔可夫转移核的离散扩散模型改进方法，通过允许重新掩码来纠正错误，并使用训练停止准则自适应调整推理步骤数，在数独和倒计时任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码离散扩散模型在推理任务中存在两个主要限制：1) 无法在掩码路径上纠正自身错误；2) 使用固定去噪步骤数，无法根据问题复杂度调整计算量。

Method: 提出基于学习马尔可夫转移核的方法，该核在自身输出上进行训练。设计允许令牌重新掩码以纠正先前错误，不使用固定时间表而是训练停止准则，仅添加两个轻量级预测头即可重用和微调现有预训练模型。

Result: 在Sudoku-Extreme数据集上明显优于其他基于流的方法，达到95%的有效性。在Countdown-4任务中，平均仅需10步即可解决近96%的问题，许多问题仅需2步即可解决。

Conclusion: 该方法通过允许重新掩码和自适应停止准则，有效解决了标准离散扩散模型在推理任务中的局限性，实现了更好的错误纠正能力和计算效率。

Abstract: Standard masked discrete diffusion models face limitations in reasoning tasks due to their inability to correct their own mistakes on the masking path. Since they rely on a fixed number of denoising steps, they are unable to adjust their computation to the complexity of a given problem. To address these limitations, we introduce a method based on learning a Markov transition kernel that is trained on its own outputs. This design enables tokens to be remasked, allowing the model to correct its previous mistakes. Furthermore, we do not need a fixed time schedule but use a trained stopping criterion. This allows for adaptation of the number of function evaluations to the difficulty of the reasoning problem. Our adaptation adds two lightweight prediction heads, enabling reuse and fine-tuning of existing pretrained models. On the Sudoku-Extreme dataset we clearly outperform other flow based methods with a validity of 95%. For the Countdown-4 we only need in average of 10 steps to solve almost 96% of them correctly, while many problems can be solved already in 2 steps.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Lineage-guided Geodesics with Finsler Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16708)
*Aaron Zweig,Mingxuan Zhang,David A. Knowles,Elham Azizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合几何和分类先验的Finsler度量方法，用于轨迹推断中的插值任务，在合成和真实数据上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断旨在从动态系统的观测时间点插值路径，以推断未观测时间的轨迹并理解系统动态。现有方法主要关注连续几何先验，利用数据相关的空间特征定义黎曼度量，但许多应用存在离散、有向的先验知识（如发育生物学中的谱系树）。

Method: 引入了一种Finsler度量，将几何与分类相结合，同时纳入连续几何先验和离散有向先验知识，用于轨迹推断中的插值任务。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据的插值任务中，该方法相比先前方法表现出改进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过结合几何和分类先验的Finsler度量方法，能够更有效地整合不同类型的先验知识，提升轨迹推断的准确性，特别是在存在离散有向先验知识的应用中。

Abstract: Trajectory inference investigates how to interpolate paths between observed timepoints of dynamical systems, such as temporally resolved population distributions, with the goal of inferring trajectories at unseen times and better understanding system dynamics. Previous work has focused on continuous geometric priors, utilizing data-dependent spatial features to define a Riemannian metric. In many applications, there exists discrete, directed prior knowledge over admissible transitions (e.g. lineage trees in developmental biology). We introduce a Finsler metric that combines geometry with classification and incorporate both types of priors in trajectory inference, yielding improved performance on interpolation tasks in synthetic and real-world data.

</details>


### [125] [Federated Learning with Multi-Partner OneFlorida+ Consortium Data for Predicting Major Postoperative Complications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16723)
*Yuanfang Ren,Varun Sai Vemuri,Zhenhong Hu,Benjamin Shickel,Ziyuan Guan,Tyler J. Loftus,Parisa Rashidi,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Azra Bihorac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发并验证了基于多中心数据的联邦学习模型，用于预测重大术后并发症和死亡率，模型性能优于或相当于单中心模型，同时保护数据隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够在保护数据隐私和安全的前提下，利用多中心数据构建具有良好泛化能力的术后并发症和死亡率预测模型，验证联邦学习在临床决策支持系统中的可行性。

Method: 回顾性、纵向、多中心队列研究，纳入2012-2023年间5家医疗机构的358,644名成年患者（494,163次住院大手术）。开发并内部和外部验证联邦学习模型，预测ICU入住、机械通气治疗、急性肾损伤和住院死亡率。与单中心本地模型和所有中心数据池化的集中模型进行比较，主要使用AUROC和AUPRC评估性能。

Result: 联邦学习模型在所有结局指标和所有中心均表现出强大的预测性能，AUROC和AUPRC值一致优于或相当于最佳本地模型，展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过利用多中心数据，开发了稳健、可泛化且保护隐私的术后并发症和死亡率预测模型，这些发现支持联邦学习在临床决策支持系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Background: This study aims to develop and validate federated learning models for predicting major postoperative complications and mortality using a large multicenter dataset from the OneFlorida Data Trust. We hypothesize that federated learning models will offer robust generalizability while preserving data privacy and security. Methods: This retrospective, longitudinal, multicenter cohort study included 358,644 adult patients admitted to five healthcare institutions, who underwent 494,163 inpatient major surgical procedures from 2012-2023. We developed and internally and externally validated federated learning models to predict the postoperative risk of intensive care unit (ICU) admission, mechanical ventilation (MV) therapy, acute kidney injury (AKI), and in-hospital mortality. These models were compared with local models trained on data from a single center and central models trained on a pooled dataset from all centers. Performance was primarily evaluated using area under the receiver operating characteristics curve (AUROC) and the area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) values. Results: Our federated learning models demonstrated strong predictive performance, with AUROC scores consistently comparable or superior performance in terms of AUROC and AUPRC across all outcomes and sites. Our federated learning models also demonstrated strong generalizability, with comparable or superior performance in terms of both AUROC and AUPRC compared to the best local learning model at each site. Conclusions: By leveraging multicenter data, we developed robust, generalizable, and privacy-preserving predictive models for major postoperative complications and mortality. These findings support the feasibility of federated learning in clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [126] [The Cost of Reasoning: Chain-of-Thought Induces Overconfidence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16728)
*Robert Welch,Emir Konuk,Kevin Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现视觉语言模型中的推理过程会降低大多数不确定性估计的质量，即使推理提高了任务准确性，主要原因是隐式答案条件化导致模型过度自信


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越多地部署在高风险环境中，可靠的不确定性量化与预测准确性同样重要。虽然扩展推理（如思维链提示或推理训练模型）已成为现代VLM流程的普遍做法，但其对UQ可靠性的影响尚不清楚

Method: 通过分析推理过程中的隐式答案条件化机制，研究推理如何影响不确定性估计质量。比较了不同不确定性估计方法在推理条件下的表现

Result: 推理一致性地降低了大多数不确定性估计的质量，即使它提高了任务准确性。基于一致性的方法在推理下保持稳健且通常有所改善

Conclusion: 对于启用推理的视觉语言模型，基于一致性的不确定性估计是实用选择，因为它在推理条件下保持稳健，而其他方法会因隐式答案条件化导致过度自信

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings where reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) is as important as predictive accuracy. Extended reasoning via chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting or reasoning-trained models has become ubiquitous in modern VLM pipelines, yet its effect on UQ reliability remains poorly understood. We show that reasoning consistently degrades the quality of most uncertainty estimates, even when it improves task accuracy. We identify implicit answer conditioning as the primary mechanism: as reasoning traces converge on a conclusion before the final answer is generated, token probabilities increasingly reflect consistency with the model's own reasoning trace rather than uncertainty about correctness. In effect, the model becomes overconfident in its answer. In contrast, agreement-based consistency remains robust and often improves under reasoning, making it a practical choice for uncertainty estimation in reasoning-enabled VLMs.

</details>


### [127] [GeMA: Learning Latent Manifold Frontiers for Benchmarking Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16729)
*Jia Ming Li,Anupriya,Daniel J. Graham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeMA（几何流形分析）框架，使用变分自编码器学习生产集的低维流形边界，解决传统前沿方法对凸性、同质性等限制性假设的依赖问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统前沿方法（如DEA和SFA）依赖于生产集的限制性假设，只能间接处理异质性和规模效应，需要更灵活的方法来建模复杂系统的效率评估

Method: 提出GeMA框架，通过生产力-流形变分自编码器（ProMan-VAE）实现。将生产集表示为联合输入-输出空间中低维流形的边界，使用分头编码器学习捕获技术结构和运营效率的潜变量

Result: 在合成数据（具有非凸前沿、异质技术和规模偏差）和四个真实案例（全球城市铁路系统、英国铁路运营商、国民经济、高频风电场数据）上验证。当经典假设成立时表现与传统方法相当，在异质性、非凸性或规模偏差显著时提供额外洞察

Conclusion: GeMA提供了一个灵活的流形前沿框架，能够处理传统方法难以建模的复杂生产结构，为具有异质性、非凸性或规模相关偏差的系统提供更丰富的效率评估工具

Abstract: Benchmarking the performance of complex systems such as rail networks, renewable generation assets and national economies is central to transport planning, regulation and macroeconomic analysis. Classical frontier methods, notably Data Envelopment Analysis (DEA) and Stochastic Frontier Analysis (SFA), estimate an efficient frontier in the observed input-output space and define efficiency as distance to this frontier, but rely on restrictive assumptions on the production set and only indirectly address heterogeneity and scale effects.
  We propose Geometric Manifold Analysis (GeMA), a latent manifold frontier framework implemented via a productivity-manifold variational autoencoder (ProMan-VAE). Instead of specifying a frontier function in the observed space, GeMA represents the production set as the boundary of a low-dimensional manifold embedded in the joint input-output space. A split-head encoder learns latent variables that capture technological structure and operational inefficiency. Efficiency is evaluated with respect to the learned manifold, endogenous peer groups arise as clusters in latent technology space, a quotient construction supports scale-invariant benchmarking, and a local certification radius, derived from the decoder Jacobian and a Lipschitz bound, quantifies the geometric robustness of efficiency scores.
  We validate GeMA on synthetic data with non-convex frontiers, heterogeneous technologies and scale bias, and on four real-world case studies: global urban rail systems (COMET), British rail operators (ORR), national economies (Penn World Table) and a high-frequency wind-farm dataset. Across these domains GeMA behaves comparably to established methods when classical assumptions hold, and provides additional insight in settings with pronounced heterogeneity, non-convexity or size-related bias.

</details>


### [128] [Understanding Quantization of Optimizer States in LLM Pre-training: Dynamics of State Staleness and Effectiveness of State Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16731)
*Kristi Topollai,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了低精度指数移动平均（EMA）优化器状态的量化效应，发现量化会导致状态停滞，并提出了基于理论指导的重置调度方法来恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化优化器状态已成为内存高效大规模预训练的重要技术，但量化后的优化器动态特性尚未被充分理解。需要深入研究低精度EMA状态如何影响优化过程，以及如何缓解量化带来的负面影响。

Method: 1. 分析低精度EMA状态，揭示量化导致状态停滞的机制；2. 开发简单的预测模型估计单步停滞概率，并描述停滞随时间累积的过程；3. 基于理论推导，提出选择有用重置周期的简单方法；4. 在受控模拟和LLM预训练中进行实验验证。

Result: 研究发现量化会导致EMA状态停滞，使状态更新无效，从而减慢适应速度。提出的理论指导的重置调度方法能够有效恢复因低精度状态存储而损失的性能，同时显著减少优化器状态的内存占用。

Conclusion: 对于低精度优化器状态，关键问题不仅是是否需要重置，而是何时应用重置。合适的重置调度能够缓解量化引起的状态停滞问题，在保持内存效率的同时恢复优化性能，为大规模预训练提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantizing optimizer states is becoming an important ingredient of memory-efficient large-scale pre-training, but the resulting optimizer dynamics remain only partially understood. We study low-precision exponential moving average (EMA) optimizer states and show how quantization can cause many nominal updates to round back to the same stored value, making the state effectively stale and slowing adaptation beyond what the nominal decay would suggest. We then develop a simple predictive model of stalling that estimates one-step stalling probabilities and characterizes how stalling builds up over time after the initialization. This perspective provides a mechanistic explanation for why optimizer-state resets help in low precision: once a quantized EMA becomes effectively stale, resetting it can temporarily restore responsiveness. Motivated by this picture, we derive a simple theory-guided method for choosing useful reset periods, showing that in low precision the key question is not only whether resets help, but when they should be applied. Experiments in controlled simulations and LLM pre-training show that suitable reset schedules recover the performance lost to low-precision state storage while substantially reducing optimizer-state memory.

</details>


### [129] [Bayesian Inference of Psychometric Variables From Brain and Behavior in Implicit Association Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16741)
*Christian A. Kothe,Sean Mullen,Michael V. Bronstein,Grant Hanada,Marcelo Cicconet,Aaron N. McInnes,Tim Mullen,Marc Aafjes,Scott R. Sponheim,Alik S. Widge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出稀疏分层贝叶斯模型，利用多模态数据从内隐联想测验（IAT）中预测心理健康相关症状体验，相比传统D分数方法（AUC<0.7）性能有所提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于反应时的D分数方法在预测心理健康相关心理测量变量时性能有限（通常AUC低于0.7），需要更有效的方法来克服IAT数据中个体间变异大、会话内效应小的问题

Method: 提出稀疏分层贝叶斯模型，作为D分数的多元泛化，具有可训练参数，针对IAT研究典型的小样本队列进行参数效率优化。分析两种IAT变体数据：自杀相关E-IAT（n=39）和精神病相关PSY-IAT（n=34）

Result: 最佳模态配置下达到AUC 0.73（E-IAT）和0.76（PSY-IAT），但校正后95%置信区间较宽（±0.18），FDR校正后结果边缘显著（q=0.10）。限制E-IAT为MDD参与者时AUC提升至0.79[0.62,0.97]（q=0.05显著）。性能与最佳参考方法（收缩LDA和EEGNet）相当，且明显优于接近随机的D分数（AUC 0.50-0.53）

Conclusion: 该框架在增强基于IAT的困境和精神病相关体验评估方面显示出潜力，但需要在更大独立队列上进行进一步验证以确立临床实用性

Abstract: Objective. We establish a principled method for inferring mental health related psychometric variables from neural and behavioral data using the Implicit Association Test (IAT) as the data generation engine, aiming to overcome the limited predictive performance (typically under 0.7 AUC) of the gold-standard D-score method, which relies solely on reaction times.
  Approach. We propose a sparse hierarchical Bayesian model that leverages multi-modal data to predict experiences related to mental illness symptoms in new participants. The model is a multivariate generalization of the D-score with trainable parameters, engineered for parameter efficiency in the small-cohort regime typical of IAT studies. Data from two IAT variants were analyzed: a suicidality-related E-IAT ($n=39$) and a psychosis-related PSY-IAT ($n=34$).
  Main Results. Our approach overcomes a high inter-individual variability and low within-session effect size in the dataset, reaching AUCs of 0.73 (E-IAT) and 0.76 (PSY-IAT) in the best modality configurations, though corrected 95% confidence intervals are wide ($\pm 0.18$) and results are marginally significant after FDR correction ($q=0.10$). Restricting the E-IAT to MDD participants improves AUC to 0.79 $[0.62, 0.97]$ (significant at $q=0.05$). Performance is on par with the best reference methods (shrinkage LDA and EEGNet) for each task, even when the latter were adapted to the task, while the proposed method was not. Accuracy was substantially above near-chance D-scores (0.50-0.53 AUC) in both tasks, with more consistent cross-task performance than any single reference method.
  Significance. Our framework shows promise for enhancing IAT-based assessment of experiences related to entrapment and psychosis, and potentially other mental health conditions, though further validation on larger and independent cohorts will be needed to establish clinical utility.

</details>


### [130] [A Practical Algorithm for Feature-Rich, Non-Stationary Bandit Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16755)
*Wei Min Loh,Sajib Kumer Sinha,Ankur Agarwal,Pascal Poupart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出C3 Thompson采样算法，用于解决具有密集臂特征、非线性奖励函数和相关臂随时间变化的上下文赌博机问题，在多个数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文赌博机研究大多假设线性奖励函数或独立臂，而实际应用中常面临密集臂特征、非线性奖励函数以及臂间相关性随时间保持的问题，特别是在推荐系统等场景中。

Method: 提出条件耦合上下文C3 Thompson采样算法，结合嵌入空间上的改进Nadaraya-Watson估计器和Thompson采样，实现无需重新训练的在线学习。

Result: 在四个OpenML表格数据集上，C3相比次优算法平均累积遗憾降低5.7%；在Microsoft News Dataset上点击率提升12.4%。

Conclusion: C3算法有效解决了具有密集臂特征、非线性奖励函数和相关臂的上下文赌博机问题，在推荐系统等实际应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Contextual bandits are incredibly useful in many practical problems. We go one step further by devising a more realistic problem that combines: (1) contextual bandits with dense arm features, (2) non-linear reward functions, and (3) a generalization of correlated bandits where reward distributions change over time but the degree of correlation maintains. This formulation lends itself to a wider set of applications such as recommendation tasks. To solve this problem, we introduce conditionally coupled contextual C3 Thompson sampling for Bernoulli bandits. It combines an improved Nadaraya-Watson estimator on an embedding space with Thompson sampling that allows online learning without retraining. Empirical results show that C3 outperforms the next best algorithm by 5.7% lower average cumulative regret on four OpenML tabular datasets as well as demonstrating a 12.4% click lift on Microsoft News Dataset (MIND) compared to other algorithms.

</details>


### [131] [pADAM: A Plug-and-Play All-in-One Diffusion Architecture for Multi-Physics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16757)
*Amirhossein Mollaali,Bongseok Kim,Christian Moya,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pADAM是一个统一的生成式框架，通过学习跨异构偏微分方程家族的共享概率先验，支持前向预测和逆向推理，无需重新训练即可实现多物理定律的统一推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习求解器主要局限于单一方程设置，限制了跨物理机制和推理任务的迁移能力。需要开发能够泛化到不同物理定律的统一人工智能框架。

Method: pADAM通过学习系统状态和物理参数的联合概率分布，构建共享的概率先验。该框架结合了保形预测进行不确定性量化，并支持从稀疏观测中进行概率模型选择。

Result: 在从标量扩散到非线性Navier-Stokes方程的基准测试中，pADAM即使在稀疏观测下也能实现准确推理。通过保形预测提供可靠的不确定性量化，仅需两个稀疏快照即可进行概率模型选择，识别控制定律。

Conclusion: pADAM展示了生成式多物理建模在统一和不确定性感知的科学推理方面的潜力，为跨不同物理定律的泛化提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Generalizing across disparate physical laws remains a fundamental challenge for artificial intelligence in science. Existing deep-learning solvers are largely confined to single-equation settings, limiting transfer across physical regimes and inference tasks. Here we introduce pADAM, a unified generative framework that learns a shared probabilistic prior across heterogeneous partial differential equation families. Through a learned joint distribution of system states and, where applicable, physical parameters, pADAM supports forward prediction and inverse inference within a single architecture without retraining. Across benchmarks ranging from scalar diffusion to nonlinear Navier--Stokes equations, pADAM achieves accurate inference even under sparse observations. Combined with conformal prediction, it also provides reliable uncertainty quantification with coverage guarantees. In addition, pADAM performs probabilistic model selection from only two sparse snapshots, identifying governing laws through its learned generative representation. These results highlight the potential of generative multi-physics modeling for unified and uncertainty-aware scientific inference.

</details>


### [132] [SOMP: Scalable Gradient Inversion for Large Language Models via Subspace-Guided Orthogonal Matching Pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16761)
*Yibo Li,Qiongxiu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOMP是一种可扩展的梯度反演框架，通过将聚合梯度中的文本恢复建模为稀疏信号恢复问题，解决了大语言模型中梯度反演攻击在批量较大时的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度反演攻击揭示了从共享梯度中可以重建私有训练文本，这对大语言模型构成隐私风险。现有方法在小批量设置下表现良好，但在扩展到更大批量和更长序列时面临严重信号混合、高计算成本和保真度下降的挑战。

Method: SOMP（子空间引导正交匹配追踪）将聚合梯度中的文本恢复建模为稀疏信号恢复问题。该方法利用聚合transformer梯度中保留的可利用的头部几何结构和样本级稀疏性，逐步缩小搜索空间并解耦混合信号，而无需穷举搜索。

Result: 在多个LLM家族、模型规模和五种语言的实验中，SOMP在聚合梯度机制下始终优于先前方法。在批量大小B=16的长序列设置中，SOMP实现了比强基线显著更高的重建保真度，同时保持计算竞争力。即使在极端聚合（高达B=128）下，SOMP仍能恢复有意义的文本。

Conclusion: SOMP表明，在先前攻击效果显著降低的情况下，隐私泄露仍可能持续存在。该方法为大规模梯度反演攻击提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks reveal that private training text can be reconstructed from shared gradients, posing a privacy risk to large language models (LLMs). While prior methods perform well in small-batch settings, scaling to larger batch sizes and longer sequences remains challenging due to severe signal mixing, high computational cost, and degraded fidelity. We present SOMP (Subspace-Guided Orthogonal Matching Pursuit), a scalable gradient inversion framework that casts text recovery from aggregated gradients as a sparse signal recovery problem. Our key insight is that aggregated transformer gradients retain exploitable head-wise geometric structure together with sample-level sparsity. SOMP leverages these properties to progressively narrow the search space and disentangle mixed signals without exhaustive search. Experiments across multiple LLM families, model scales, and five languages show that SOMP consistently outperforms prior methods in the aggregated-gradient regime.For long sequences at batch size B=16, SOMP achieves substantially higher reconstruction fidelity than strong baselines, while remaining computationally competitive. Even under extreme aggregation (up to B=128), SOMP still recovers meaningful text, suggesting that privacy leakage can persist in regimes where prior attacks become much less effective.

</details>


### [133] [Conservative Continuous-Time Treatment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16789)
*Nora Schneider,Georg Manten,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种保守的连续时间随机控制框架，用于从不规则采样的患者轨迹中优化治疗方案，通过路径空间的正则化限制外推，提高鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗治疗优化中，患者轨迹通常是不规则采样的，基于模型的优化可能因模型误差而提出超出观测范围的控制方案，导致对真实动态的优化失败

Method: 将未知患者动态建模为受控随机微分方程，治疗作为连续时间控制；添加基于一致签名的MMD正则化器，在路径空间上惩罚诱导轨迹分布偏离观测轨迹的治疗方案

Result: 实验在基准数据集上显示，相比非保守基线方法，该方法具有更好的鲁棒性和性能表现

Conclusion: 提出的保守连续时间随机控制框架通过路径空间正则化有效限制外推，为不规则采样患者轨迹的治疗优化提供了更可靠的方法

Abstract: We develop a conservative continuous-time stochastic control framework for treatment optimization from irregularly sampled patient trajectories. The unknown patient dynamics are modeled as a controlled stochastic differential equation with treatment as a continuous-time control. Naive model-based optimization can exploit model errors and propose out-of-support controls, so optimizing the estimated dynamics may not optimize the true dynamics. To limit extrapolation, we add a consistent signature-based MMD regularizer on path space that penalizes treatment plans whose induced trajectory distribution deviates from observed trajectories. The resulting objective minimizes a computable upper bound on the true cost. Experiments on benchmark datasets show improved robustness and performance compared to non-conservative baselines.

</details>


### [134] [Adaptive Moments are Surprisingly Effective for Plug-and-Play Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16797)
*Christian Belardi,Justin Lovelace,Kilian Q. Weinberger,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出使用自适应矩估计来稳定扩散采样中的噪声似然分数，在图像恢复和类条件生成任务上达到SOTA效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导扩散采样依赖于近似通常难以处理的似然分数，这会在采样动态中引入显著噪声，需要稳定这些噪声似然分数

Method: 使用自适应矩估计（adaptive moment estimation）来稳定采样过程中的噪声似然分数，通过自适应矩来缓解梯度噪声

Result: 在图像恢复和类条件生成任务上取得了最先进的结果，超越了更复杂且计算成本更高的方法，在合成和真实数据上都进行了实证分析

Conclusion: 通过自适应矩缓解梯度噪声是提高对齐性的有效方法，尽管方法简单但效果显著

Abstract: Guided diffusion sampling relies on approximating often intractable likelihood scores, which introduces significant noise into the sampling dynamics. We propose using adaptive moment estimation to stabilize these noisy likelihood scores during sampling. Despite its simplicity, our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on image restoration and class-conditional generation tasks, outperforming more complicated methods, which are often computationally more expensive. We provide empirical analysis of our method on both synthetic and real data, demonstrating that mitigating gradient noise through adaptive moments offers an effective way to improve alignment.

</details>


### [135] [High-Dimensional Gaussian Mean Estimation under Realizable Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16798)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Thanasis Pittas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高斯分布均值估计在可实现ε污染缺失数据模型下的信息-计算间隙，证明了在统计查询模型中，高效算法需要显著更多样本或指数时间，并提供了匹配下界的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高斯分布均值估计在可实现ε污染缺失数据模型下的计算复杂性。该模型介于完全随机缺失(MCAR)和非随机缺失(MNAR)之间，前人工作建立了信息理论上界和下界，但提出的估计器需要指数时间，因此需要探索高维情况下是否存在高效算法。

Method: 1. 在统计查询(SQ)模型中建立下界，证明算法要么需要显著多于信息理论必要样本量，要么需要指数运行时间；2. 将结果推广到低阶多项式和PTF测试；3. 设计算法，其样本-时间权衡几乎匹配下界。

Result: 证明了可实现ε污染模型下高斯均值估计存在信息-计算间隙：在SQ模型中，任何多项式时间算法都需要比信息理论最优样本量多Ω(√d/ε²)倍样本，否则需要指数时间。提出的算法在样本-时间权衡上几乎匹配该下界。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在可实现ε污染缺失数据模型下建立了高斯均值估计的信息-计算间隙，从计算复杂性角度刻画了该问题的难度，表明高效算法必须付出样本量代价，填补了前人工作中计算效率方面的空白。

Abstract: We study mean estimation for a Gaussian distribution with identity covariance in $\mathbb{R}^d$ under a missing data scheme termed realizable $ε$-contamination model. In this model an adversary can choose a function $r(x)$ between 0 and $ε$ and each sample $x$ goes missing with probability $r(x)$. Recent work Ma et al., 2024 proposed this model as an intermediate-strength setting between Missing Completely At Random (MCAR) -- where missingness is independent of the data -- and Missing Not At Random (MNAR) -- where missingness may depend arbitrarily on the sample values and can lead to non-identifiability issues. That work established information-theoretic upper and lower bounds for mean estimation in the realizable contamination model. Their proposed estimators incur runtime exponential in the dimension, leaving open the possibility of computationally efficient algorithms in high dimensions. In this work, we establish an information-computation gap in the Statistical Query model (and, as a corollary, for Low-Degree Polynomials and PTF tests), showing that algorithms must either use substantially more samples than information-theoretically necessary or incur exponential runtime. We complement our SQ lower bound with an algorithm whose sample-time tradeoff nearly matches our lower bound. Together, these results qualitatively characterize the complexity of Gaussian mean estimation under $ε$-realizable contamination.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamic Meta-Layer Aggregation for Byzantine-Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16846)
*Reek Das,Biplab Kanti Sen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedAOT是一种基于元学习启发的自适应聚合框架，用于防御联邦学习中的多标签翻转和无目标投毒攻击，通过动态加权客户端更新来提高模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在医疗、金融、物联网等领域的应用日益广泛，但容易受到拜占庭攻击者的恶意更新注入，严重影响全局模型性能。现有防御方法通常针对特定攻击类型，无法有效应对多标签翻转或噪声与后门模式组合等无目标策略攻击。

Method: 提出FedAOT防御机制，采用元学习启发的自适应聚合框架，动态加权客户端更新基于其可靠性，抑制对抗性影响，不依赖预定义阈值或限制性攻击假设。

Result: 实验结果表明，FedAOT显著提高了模型准确性和鲁棒性，同时保持计算效率，在不同数据集和多种攻击类型上都能有效泛化，即使在未见过的攻击场景中也能保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: FedAOT为安全联邦学习提供了一个可扩展且实用的解决方案，能够有效防御多标签翻转和无目标投毒攻击，提高联邦学习系统的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is increasingly applied in sectors like healthcare, finance, and IoT, enabling collaborative model training while safeguarding user privacy. However, FL systems are susceptible to Byzantine adversaries that inject malicious updates, which can severely compromise global model performance. Existing defenses tend to focus on specific attack types and fail against untargeted strategies, such as multi-label flipping or combinations of noise and backdoor patterns. To overcome these limitations, we propose FedAOT-a novel defense mechanism that counters multi-label flipping and untargeted poisoning attacks using a metalearning-inspired adaptive aggregation framework. FedAOT dynamically weights client updates based on their reliability, suppressing adversarial influence without relying on predefined thresholds or restrictive attack assumptions. Notably, FedAOT generalizes effectively across diverse datasets and a wide range of attack types, maintaining robust performance even in previously unseen scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that FedAOT substantially improves model accuracy and resilience while maintaining computational efficiency, offering a scalable and practical solution for secure federated learning.

</details>


### [137] [GIST: Gauge-Invariant Spectral Transformers for Scalable Graph Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16849)
*Mattia Rigotti,Nicholas Thumiger,Thomas Frick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GIST：一种通过随机投影实现O(N)复杂度并保持规范不变性的图Transformer架构，解决了图结构数据中位置编码的计算挑战和规范不变性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决图结构数据和网格数据中Transformer位置编码的计算挑战：精确谱方法需要立方复杂度特征分解且可能破坏规范不变性，而近似方法则牺牲了规范对称性，这两种失败模式都会导致归纳学习中的灾难性泛化问题

Method: 提出GIST架构，通过随机投影实现端到端O(N)复杂度，同时通过基于内积的注意力机制在投影嵌入上算法性地保持规范不变性

Result: 在标准图基准测试中达到最先进水平（PPI上99.50% micro-F1），在神经算子基准测试中可扩展到75万节点，在DrivAerNet和DrivAerNet++数据集上实现最先进的气动预测性能

Conclusion: GIST通过结合计算效率和规范不变性，解决了图Transformer中的关键挑战，实现了跨任意网格分辨率的参数传递，为神经算子应用提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Adapting transformer positional encoding to meshes and graph-structured data presents significant computational challenges: exact spectral methods require cubic-complexity eigendecomposition and can inadvertently break gauge invariance through numerical solver artifacts, while efficient approximate methods sacrifice gauge symmetry by design. Both failure modes cause catastrophic generalization in inductive learning, where models trained with one set of numerical choices fail when encountering different spectral decompositions of similar graphs or discretizations of the same mesh. We propose GIST (Gauge-Invariant Spectral Transformers), a new graph transformer architecture that resolves this challenge by achieving end-to-end $\mathcal{O}(N)$ complexity through random projections while algorithmically preserving gauge invariance via inner-product-based attention on the projected embeddings. We prove GIST achieves discretization-invariant learning with bounded mismatch error, enabling parameter transfer across arbitrary mesh resolutions for neural operator applications. Empirically, GIST matches state-of-the-art on standard graph benchmarks (e.g., achieving 99.50% micro-F1 on PPI) while uniquely scaling to mesh-based Neural Operator benchmarks with up to 750K nodes, achieving state-of-the-art aerodynamic prediction on the challenging DrivAerNet and DrivAerNet++ datasets.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Reasoning on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16867)
*Yelysei Bondarenko,Thomas Hehn,Rob Hesselink,Romain Lepert,Fabio Valerio Massoli,Evgeny Mironov,Leyla Mirvakhabova,Tribhuvanesh Orekondy,Spyridon Stasis,Andrey Kuzmin,Anna Kuzina,Markus Nagel,Ankita Nayak,Corrado Rainone,Ork de Rooij,Paul N Whatmough,Arash Behboodi,Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种轻量级方法，使小型LLM能够在资源受限的边缘设备上进行推理，通过LoRA适配器、预算强制、并行测试时缩放和动态适配器切换等技术，在保持准确性的同时显著减少响应长度和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然通过思维链推理在复杂问题解决任务中表现出色，但其冗长的推理轨迹和大上下文需求使其难以在边缘设备上部署。现有方法通常将大型模型的推理轨迹蒸馏到小型模型中，但这些轨迹冗长且风格冗余，不适合设备端推理。

Method: 1. 使用LoRA适配器结合监督微调实现小型LLM的推理能力；2. 通过强化学习引入预算强制，显著减少响应长度；3. 利用并行测试时缩放改善内存受限的解码；4. 提出动态适配器切换机制，仅在需要时激活推理；5. 在提示编码期间采用KV-cache共享策略，减少首次令牌生成时间。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上的实验表明，该方法在严格资源约束下实现了高效、准确的推理，使LLM推理在移动场景中变得实用。视频演示显示解决方案可在移动设备上运行。

Conclusion: 该方法通过创新的轻量级技术组合，成功解决了边缘设备上LLM推理的挑战，包括高令牌生成成本、大KV-cache占用和推理能力蒸馏效率低等问题，为移动场景下的LLM推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with chain-of-thought reasoning achieve state-of-the-art performance across complex problem-solving tasks, but their verbose reasoning traces and large context requirements make them impractical for edge deployment. These challenges include high token generation costs, large KV-cache footprints, and inefficiencies when distilling reasoning capabilities into smaller models for mobile devices. Existing approaches often rely on distilling reasoning traces from larger models into smaller models, which are verbose and stylistically redundant, undesirable for on-device inference. In this work, we propose a lightweight approach to enable reasoning in small LLMs using LoRA adapters combined with supervised fine-tuning. We further introduce budget forcing via reinforcement learning on these adapters, significantly reducing response length with minimal accuracy loss. To address memory-bound decoding, we exploit parallel test-time scaling, improving accuracy at minor latency increase. Finally, we present a dynamic adapter-switching mechanism that activates reasoning only when needed and a KV-cache sharing strategy during prompt encoding, reducing time-to-first-token for on-device inference. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B demonstrate that our method achieves efficient, accurate reasoning under strict resource constraints, making LLM reasoning practical for mobile scenarios. Videos demonstrating our solution running on mobile devices are available on our project page.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [IdentityGuard: Context-Aware Restriction and Provenance for Personalized Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15679)
*Lingyun Zhang,Yu Xie,Ping Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出IDENTITYGUARD方法，通过条件限制和概念特定水印，在个性化文本到图像模型中实现上下文感知的安全防护，防止滥用同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文本到图像模型面临独特的安全挑战，现有通用上下文无关方法存在缺陷。全局过滤器为防止滥用会完全删除概念，导致不可接受的附带损害，损害模型的整体效用。

Method: 提出IDENTITYGUARD方法，基于安全应与威胁本身一样具有上下文感知性的原则，实现条件限制（仅在个性化身份与有害内容结合时阻止）和概念特定水印（用于精确溯源）。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效防止滥用，同时保持模型的效用，并实现强大的溯源能力。

Conclusion: 通过超越粗放的全局过滤器，该方法展示了更有效和负责任的AI安全路径，将安全与个性化概念内在绑定。

Abstract: The nature of personalized text-to-image models poses a unique safety challenge that generic context-blind methods are ill-equipped to handle. Such global filters create a dilemma: to prevent misuse, they are forced to damage the model's broader utility by erasing concepts entirely, causing unacceptable collateral damage.Our work presents a more precisely targeted approach, built on the principle that security should be as context-aware as the threat itself, intrinsically bound to the personalized concept. We present IDENTITYGUARD, which realizes this principle through a conditional restriction that blocks harmful content only when combined with the personalized identity, and a concept-specific watermark for precise traceability. Experiments show our approach prevents misuse while preserving the model's utility and enabling robust traceability. By moving beyond blunt, global filters, our work demonstrates a more effective and responsible path toward AI safety.

</details>


### [140] [State-Dependent Safety Failures in Multi-Turn Language Model Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15684)
*Pengcheng Li,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu,Zhang kejun,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu,Wenbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出STAR框架，从状态空间视角研究多轮对话中的安全失效问题，发现即使静态评估稳健的模型在结构化多轮交互中也会出现快速、可复现的安全崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的安全对齐评估主要基于孤立查询，但实际使用本质上是多轮对话。尽管多轮越狱攻击在经验上有效，但对话安全失效的结构仍未被充分理解。需要从状态空间视角研究多轮安全失效的机制。

Method: 引入STAR（状态导向诊断框架），将对话历史视为状态转移算子，实现对交互轨迹上安全行为的受控分析。该框架不优化攻击强度，而是提供原则性探针，研究对齐模型在自回归条件下的安全边界穿越行为。

Result: 在多个前沿语言模型上发现，静态评估下看似稳健的系统在结构化多轮交互中会经历快速且可复现的安全崩溃。机制分析显示存在远离拒绝相关表征的单调漂移，以及角色条件上下文诱导的突然相变。

Conclusion: 应将语言模型安全视为定义在对话轨迹上的动态、状态依赖过程。多轮安全失效源于结构化的上下文状态演化而非孤立的提示漏洞，这为安全评估和加固提供了新视角。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models is typically evaluated under isolated queries, yet real-world use is inherently multi-turn. Although multi-turn jailbreaks are empirically effective, the structure of conversational safety failure remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we study safety failures from a state-space perspective and show that many multi-turn failures arise from structured contextual state evolution rather than isolated prompt vulnerabilities. We introduce STAR, a state-oriented diagnostic framework that treats dialogue history as a state transition operator and enables controlled analysis of safety behavior along interaction trajectories. Rather than optimizing attack strength, STAR provides a principled probe of how aligned models traverse the safety boundary under autoregressive conditioning. Across multiple frontier language models, we find that systems that appear robust under static evaluation can undergo rapid and reproducible safety collapse under structured multi-turn interaction. Mechanistic analysis reveals monotonic drift away from refusal-related representations and abrupt phase transitions induced by role-conditioned context. Together, these findings motivate viewing language model safety as a dynamic, state-dependent process defined over conversational trajectories.

</details>


### [141] [BadLLM-TG: A Backdoor Defender powered by LLM Trigger Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15692)
*Ruyi Zhang,Heng Gao,Songlei Jian,Yusong Tan,Haifang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadLLM-TG：利用大语言模型进行文本后门防御，通过提示驱动的强化学习生成触发器，并通过对抗训练减轻后门影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于噪声的触发器生成方法无法应用于文本领域，因为文本的离散特性限制了传统方法的适用性。需要开发专门针对自然语言处理的后门防御方法。

Method: 利用大语言模型的丰富知识，提出BadLLM-TG方法，通过提示驱动的强化学习优化触发器生成，使用受害者模型的反馈损失作为奖励信号，然后通过对抗训练减轻后门影响。

Result: 实验表明该方法平均降低攻击成功率76.2%，比第二好的防御方法高出13.7个百分点。

Conclusion: BadLLM-TG成功克服了文本离散性带来的限制，利用LLM知识有效生成触发器并进行后门防御，在NLP后门防御方面表现出色。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks compromise model reliability by using triggers to manipulate outputs. Trigger inversion can accurately locate these triggers via a generator and is therefore critical for backdoor defense. However, the discrete nature of text prevents existing noise-based trigger generator from being applied to nature language processing (NLP). To overcome the limitations, we employ the rich knowledge embedded in large language models (LLMs) and propose a Backdoor defender powered by LLM Trigger Generator, termed BadLLM-TG. It is optimized through prompt-driven reinforcement learning, using the victim model's feedback loss as the reward signal. The generated triggers are then employed to mitigate the backdoor via adversarial training. Experiments show that our method reduces the attack success rate by 76.2\% on average, outperforming the second-best defender by 13.7.

</details>


### [142] [Remarks on the Relevance of Privacy Expectations for Default Opt-out Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15705)
*Sebastian Zimmeck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 美国各州隐私法要求支持通用退出机制，但禁止默认启用，特别是预装软件。然而，这种禁止可能导致隐私保护软件违反FTC法案。作者认为应根据用户隐私期望决定是否默认启用退出机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前美国各州隐私法对通用退出机制的默认设置存在矛盾：一方面要求支持UOOMs，另一方面禁止默认启用（特别是预装软件）。这种限制可能导致隐私保护软件供应商面临不公平或欺骗性行为的法律风险，需要解决这一法律冲突。

Method: 通过法律文本分析，特别是科罗拉多隐私法案等州隐私法的规定，结合FTC法案对不公平或欺骗性行为的规定，提出基于消费者隐私期望的解释框架。论证预装隐私保护软件的使用本身应被视为启用UOOMs的有效选择。

Result: 提出了新的法律解释：对于能合理建立隐私保护期望的预装软件，其使用本身应被视为启用UOOMs的有效选择。这种解释既符合CPA等法律对"肯定、自由、明确选择"的要求，又能避免隐私保护软件供应商违反FTC法案。

Conclusion: 应根据消费者隐私期望决定UOOMs是否默认启用。对于预装隐私保护软件，使用该软件应被视为启用UOOMs的有效选择。这种解释更好地平衡了隐私保护与法律合规，使公司能在隐私保护方面竞争，同时尊重消费者选择。

Abstract: Over the past few years an increasing number of states in the US have adopted new privacy laws. The majority of these laws require compliance with universal opt-out mechanisms (UOOMs), which allow consumers to send legally binding opt-out signals. However, a number of laws generally do not allow UOOMs to be enabled by default. While some laws exempt privacy-protective software from this prohibition, the exemption does not apply to pre-installed software, e.g., a privacy-protective web browser bundled with an operating system. The reason for not allowing default opt-out settings for pre-installed software is to ensure that settings reflect consumers' "affirmative, freely given, and unambiguous choice," as, for example, the Colorado Privacy Act (CPA) is putting it. However, prohibiting vendors of privacy-protective software from turning on UOOMs by default can force them into committing unfair or deceptive acts or practices under the FTC Act and equivalent state laws. Thus, whether UOOMs can be turned on by default on pre-installed software should depend on consumers' privacy expectations. For pre-installed software that is creating a reasonable expectation for consumers that their privacy will be protected, the simple use of such software should be considered a valid choice for enabling UOOMs. In such software a turned-on UOOM is not a "default setting" but rather the software's inherent behavior that a consumer expects and chooses through its use. This interpretation of consumer choice is preferable under the CPA and similar laws as it grounds the notice and choice principle in the privacy expectations of consumers and enables companies to compete on better privacy for consumers.

</details>


### [143] [Grant, Verify, Revoke: A User-Centric Pattern for Blockchain Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15721)
*Supriya Khadka,Sanchari Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ZK-Compliance框架，通过零知识证明实现去中心化应用中用户隐私与合规性的平衡，允许用户选择性披露属性而不暴露身份信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前去中心化应用中用户面临公开可验证性与个人隐私之间的冲突。为了参与受监管的链上服务，用户必须向中心化中介披露敏感身份文件，导致真实身份与公开交易历史的永久链接。这种二元选择剥夺了用户自主权并使其面临持续监控。

Method: 提出选择性披露框架，通过浏览器端零知识证明（ZK-Compliance原型）改变交互模式。用户可以在本地证明特定属性（如"我年满18岁"）而不泄露底层数据。实现用户主导的"授权、验证、撤销"生命周期，将合规性从永久数据移交转变为动态可撤销的授权会话。

Result: 评估显示客户端证明生成时间低于200毫秒，在普通硬件上提供无缝交互体验。该工作提供了早期证据表明监管合规性不必以牺牲用户隐私或自主权为代价。

Conclusion: 通过将资格验证与身份揭示解耦，该框架恢复了用户主权，为去中心化应用中的隐私保护合规提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In decentralized web applications, users face an inherent conflict between public verifiability and personal privacy. To participate in regulated on-chain services, users must currently disclose sensitive identity documents to centralized intermediaries, permanently linking real-world identities to public transaction histories. This binary choice between total privacy loss or total exclusion strips users of agency and exposes them to persistent surveillance. In this work, we introduce a Selective Disclosure Framework designed to restore user sovereignty by decoupling eligibility verification from identity revelation. We present ZK-Compliance, a prototype that leverages browser-based zero-knowledge proofs to shift the interaction model, enabling users to prove specific attributes (e.g., "I am over 18") locally without revealing the underlying data. We implement a user-governed Grant, Verify, Revoke lifecycle that transforms the user's mental model of compliance from a permanent data handover into a dynamic, revocable authorization session. Our evaluation shows that client-side proof generation takes under 200ms, enabling a seamless interactive experience on commodity hardware. This work provides early evidence that regulatory compliance need not come at the cost of user privacy or autonomy.

</details>


### [144] [Do Not Leave a Gap: Hallucination-Free Object Concealment in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15940)
*Amira Guesmi,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出背景一致的对象隐藏攻击方法，通过将目标对象视觉表征重新编码为与背景区域统计和语义一致，避免传统注意力抑制攻击引发的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力抑制的对象隐藏攻击会在视觉表征中产生语义断层，导致模型产生幻觉（虚构不存在但看似合理的对象）。研究发现幻觉并非源于对象缺失本身，而是由抑制攻击引入的语义不连续性所引发。

Method: 提出背景一致的对象隐藏攻击方法，通过像素级优化框架将目标对象的视觉表征重新编码，使其在统计和语义上与周围背景区域保持一致。该方法保留令牌结构和注意力流，避免产生表征空白，同时在多个transformer层中强制执行背景一致的重编码，保持全局场景语义。

Result: 在先进的视觉语言模型上进行广泛实验，结果表明该方法能有效隐藏目标对象，同时保留高达86%的非目标对象，与基于注意力抑制的攻击相比，将接地幻觉减少了3倍。

Conclusion: 通过背景一致的重编码方法，可以避免传统对象隐藏攻击引发的幻觉问题，实现更有效的对象隐藏同时保持场景语义完整性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently shown remarkable capabilities in visual understanding and generation, but remain vulnerable to adversarial manipulations of visual content. Prior object-hiding attacks primarily rely on suppressing or blocking region-specific representations, often creating semantic gaps that inadvertently induce hallucination, where models invent plausible but incorrect objects. In this work, we demonstrate that hallucination arises not from object absence per se, but from semantic discontinuity introduced by such suppression-based attacks. We propose a new class of \emph{background-consistent object concealment} attacks, which hide target objects by re-encoding their visual representations to be statistically and semantically consistent with surrounding background regions. Crucially, our approach preserves token structure and attention flow, avoiding representational voids that trigger hallucination. We present a pixel-level optimization framework that enforces background-consistent re-encoding across multiple transformer layers while preserving global scene semantics. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art vision-language models show that our method effectively conceals target objects while preserving up to $86\%$ of non-target objects and reducing grounded hallucination by up to $3\times$ compared to attention-suppression-based attacks.

</details>


### [145] [Cross-Scale Persistence Analysis of EM Side-Channels for Reference-Free Detection of Always-On Hardware Trojans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16058)
*Mahsa Tahghigh,Hassan Salmani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于电磁侧信道跨尺度持续性分析的参考无关检测框架，用于检测常开硬件木马，通过分析多分辨率时间频率下的统计结构演化来区分正常电路与木马行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常开硬件木马在正常操作期间保持活动状态，在没有可信黄金参考的后部署环境中难以检测，对集成电路安全构成严重威胁，需要参考无关的检测方法。

Method: 提出基于电磁侧信道跨尺度持续性分析的参考无关检测框架：1) 分析多个时间频率分辨率下的电磁辐射；2) 构建稳定性图捕捉重复执行中频谱特征的一致性；3) 使用基于BIC的GMM表征每个尺度的统计结构；4) 引入跨尺度饱和、变异性和中位数混合复杂度指标量化统计结构演化。

Result: 在AES实现上的实验结果显示：无木马设计表现出与瞬态开关行为一致的尺度依赖性变异性，而常开木马产生抑制跨尺度演化的持续性统计特征。不同木马类型（如工作负载相关泄漏信息木马和独立环形振荡器木马）表现出不同的持续性模式。

Conclusion: 跨尺度持续性分析为常开硬件木马的无监督、参考无关检测提供了物理可解释且鲁棒的保证信号，能够有效区分正常电路行为与木马活动。

Abstract: Always-on hardware Trojans pose a serious challenge to integrated circuit trust, as they remain active during normal operation and are difficult to detect in post-deployment settings without trusted golden references. This paper presents a reference-free detection framework based on cross-scale persistence analysis of electromagnetic (EM) side-channels, targeting always-on parasitic hardware behavior. The proposed method analyzes EM emissions across multiple time-frequency resolutions and constructs stability maps that capture the consistency of spectral features over repeated executions. Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) with Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) based model selection are used to characterize statistical structure at each scale. We introduce cross-scale saturation, variability, and median mixture complexity metrics that quantify whether statistical structure evolves naturally or remains persistently anchored across resolutions. Experimental results on AES implementations show that Trojan-free designs exhibit scale-dependent variability consistent with transient switching behavior, while always-on Trojans produce persistent statistical signatures that suppress cross-scale evolution. Furthermore, different Trojan classes, such as workload-correlated leakage-information Trojans and independent ring-oscillator Trojans, exhibit distinct persistence patterns. These findings demonstrate that cross-scale persistence provides a physically interpretable and robust assurance signal for unsupervised, reference-free detection of always-on hardware Trojans.

</details>


### [146] [Resource Consumption Threats in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16068)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Xinyue Wang,Zhican Chen,Weiliu Wang,Zilu Zhang,Zhengshuo Gong,Zhenhong Zhou,Li Sun,Yang Liu,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该调查论文系统回顾了大语言模型中的资源消耗威胁，建立了从威胁诱导到机制理解和缓解的统一框架，旨在为这一新兴领域提供清晰的问题界定和缓解基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算基础设施有限且成本高昂的背景下，大语言模型的资源效率是关键要求。高效的LLM能提高服务提供商的容量，降低用户的延迟和API成本。然而，最近的资源消耗威胁导致过度生成，降低了模型效率，损害了服务可用性和经济可持续性。

Method: 采用系统调查方法，建立统一视角，通过澄清研究范围，沿着从威胁诱导到机制理解和缓解的完整流程来审视问题。目标是明确这一新兴领域的问题格局。

Result: 提出了一个系统性的资源消耗威胁分析框架，涵盖了威胁诱导、机制理解和缓解策略的完整流程，为LLM资源效率研究提供了清晰的基础。

Conclusion: 该调查为LLM资源消耗威胁这一新兴领域建立了统一视图，澄清了问题范围，为后续的特征描述和缓解措施提供了更清晰的基础，有助于提高LLM的资源效率和服务可持续性。

Abstract: Given limited and costly computational infrastructure, resource efficiency is a key requirement for large language models (LLMs). Efficient LLMs increase service capacity for providers and reduce latency and API costs for users. Recent resource consumption threats induce excessive generation, degrading model efficiency and harming both service availability and economic sustainability. This survey presents a systematic review of threats to resource consumption in LLMs. We further establish a unified view of this emerging area by clarifying its scope and examining the problem along the full pipeline from threat induction to mechanism understanding and mitigation. Our goal is to clarify the problem landscape for this emerging area, thereby providing a clearer foundation for characterization and mitigation.

</details>


### [147] [Ciphertext-Policy ABE for $\mathsf{NC}^1$ Circuits with Constant-Size Ciphertexts from Succinct LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16117)
*Jiaqi Liu,Yuanyi Zhang,Fang-Wei Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于格的CP-ABE方案，支持NC¹访问策略，具有恒定大小的密文，公钥和密文大小与电路深度无关，私钥大小与输入长度线性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于格的属性基加密方案通常具有较大的密文尺寸，与访问策略的复杂性相关。本文旨在构建一个密文大小恒定的CP-ABE方案，支持NC¹电路，以解决密文膨胀问题并提高效率。

Method: 基于Wee在CRYPTO 2024提出的poly(λ)-succinct LWE假设，在标准模型下构造选择性安全的CP-ABE方案。方案使用格基密码学技术，确保公钥和密文大小O(1)（独立于电路深度d），私钥大小O(ℓ)。

Result: 成功构建了支持NC¹访问策略的CP-ABE方案，公钥和密文大小恒定（独立于电路深度），私钥大小与输入长度ℓ线性相关。作为应用，获得了广播加密方案，密文大小poly(λ)独立于log N，密钥大小poly(λ, log N)。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了基于格的恒定密文大小CP-ABE方案，支持NC¹访问策略，在标准模型下基于poly(λ)-succinct LWE假设选择性安全。该方案为构建高效的属性基加密和广播加密系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: We construct a lattice-based ciphertext-policy attribute-based encryption (CP-ABE) scheme for $\mathsf{NC}^1$ access policies with constant-size ciphertexts. Let $λ$ be the security parameter. For an $\mathsf{NC}^1$ circuit of depth $d$ and size $s$ on $\ell$-bit inputs, our scheme has the public-key and ciphertext sizes $O(1)$ (independent of $d$), and secret-key size $O(\ell)$, where the $O(\cdot)$ hides $\operatorname{poly}(λ)$ factors. As an application, we obtain a broadcast encryption scheme for $N$ users with ciphertext size $\operatorname{poly}(λ)$ independent of $\log N$ and key sizes $\operatorname{poly}(λ,\log N)$. Our construction is selectively secure in the standard model under the $\operatorname{poly}(λ)$-succinct LWE assumption introduced by Wee (CRYPTO~2024).

</details>


### [148] [Novel CRT-based Asymptotically Ideal Disjunctive Hierarchical Secret Sharing Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16267)
*Hongju Li,Jian Ding,Fuyou Miao,Cheng Wang,Cheng Shu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于中国剩余定理(CRT)的析取层次秘密共享(DHSS)方案，指出现有方案存在安全漏洞或信息率不足的问题，并提出了一种新的渐进完美CRT-DHSS方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRT-DHSS方案存在安全缺陷：Yang等人的理想方案和Tiplea等人的渐进理想方案都不安全，其他方案要么有安全漏洞，要么信息率低于1/2。需要设计既安全又高效的DHSS方案。

Method: 提出基于中国剩余定理(CRT)的渐进完美DHSS方案，支持灵活的份额大小。当所有份额大小相等时，方案是渐进理想的。方案具有计算安全性。

Result: 新方案的信息率达到1（最优），解决了现有方案的安全漏洞和低信息率问题。方案在份额大小相等时是渐进理想的。

Conclusion: 成功设计了一种安全、高效的CRT-DHSS方案，填补了现有方案在安全性和效率方面的不足，为析取层次秘密共享提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Disjunctive Hierarchical Secret Sharing (DHSS)} scheme is a type of secret sharing scheme in which the set of all participants is partitioned into disjoint subsets, and each subset is said to be a level with different degrees of trust and different thresholds. In this work, we focus on the Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT)-based DHSS schemes due to their ability to accommodate flexible share sizes. We point out that the ideal DHSS scheme of Yang et al. (ISIT, 2024) and the asymptotically ideal DHSS scheme of Tiplea et al. (IET Information Security, 2021) are insecure. Consequently, existing CRT-based DHSS schemes either exhibit security flaws or have an information rate less than $\frac{1}{2}$. To address these limitations, we propose a CRT-based asymptotically perfect DHSS scheme that supports flexible share sizes. Notably, our scheme is asymptotically ideal when all shares are equal in size. Its information rate achieves one and it has computational security.

</details>


### [149] [Systematization of Knowledge: The Design Space of Digital Payment Systems with Potential for CBDC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16320)
*Judith Senn,Aljosha Judmayer,Nicholas Stifter,Rainer Böhme*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对36个完整支付系统设计方案进行系统化知识梳理，识别CBDC设计中的模式、权衡与挑战，强调离线支付和后量子安全等研究空白


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBDC作为对私有数字支付的公共回应，提供了重新思考货币未来的机会，但现有隐私增强技术研究往往孤立且缺乏系统性分析

Method: 采用结构化、自上而下的技术分析方法，系统梳理36个完整支付系统设计方案，识别重复设计模式、技术权衡和实施挑战

Result: 识别出CBDC设计中的常见模式、技术权衡和实现挑战，为CBDC设计提供系统性技术分析框架

Conclusion: 强调离线支付和后量子安全等关键研究空白，为未来CBDC系统设计提供方向性指导

Abstract: Central Bank Digital Currencies (CBDCs) are proposed as a public response to the uptake of privately run digital payments, with the digital euro, under development by the European Central Bank (ECB), serving as a prominent example. This momentum provides a unique opportunity to fundamentally rethink the future of money, and, assuming wide adoption, to establish payment systems that offer strong cryptographic security and privacy guarantees from the start. While the central banks in charge are investigating privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs), they often conclude that PETs are immature or insufficiently scalable. Moreover, these efforts tend to examine primitives in isolation, offering little insight into how a system using these PETs would scale. This systematisation of knowledge, therefore, provides a structured, top-down technical analysis of 36 payment system designs of complete system proposals that can inform CBDC designs or were explicitly proposed for this application. We identify recurring design patterns, technical trade-offs, and implementation challenges. Concluding, we highlight research gaps, including offline payments and post-quantum security.

</details>


### [150] [SseRex: Practical Symbolic Execution of Solana Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16349)
*Tobias Cloosters,Pascal Winkler,Jens-Rene Giesen,Ghassan Karame,Lucas Davi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SseRex是首个针对Solana智能合约的符号执行漏洞检测方法，专门发现Solana特有的安全漏洞，如缺失所有者检查、签名者检查等，在8,714个合约中识别出467个潜在漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana的快速普及伴随着一系列重大安全事件，现有分析方法未能解决Solana独特且复杂的账户模型，需要专门的分析工具来检测Solana特有的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出SseRex方法，这是首个针对Solana智能合约的符号执行漏洞检测方法，能够检测Solana特有的漏洞类型，包括缺失所有者检查、缺失签名者检查、缺失密钥检查以及任意跨程序调用。

Result: 对8,714个仅字节码合约的评估显示，SseRex优于现有方法，在467个不同合约中识别出潜在漏洞。对120个开源Solana项目的分析显示，细微且容易被忽视的问题往往是严重漏洞的根本原因。

Conclusion: Solana智能合约存在独特的安全挑战，需要专门的自动化分析工具。SseRex作为首个符号执行漏洞检测方法，能够有效识别Solana特有的安全漏洞，其评估结果突显了这类工具的必要性。

Abstract: Solana is rapidly gaining traction among smart contract developers and users. However, its growing adoption has been accompanied by a series of major security incidents, which have spurred research into automated analysis techniques for Solana smart contracts. Unfortunately, existing approaches do not address the unique and complex account model of Solana. In this paper, we propose SseRex, the first symbolic execution vulnerability detection approach for finding Solana-specific bugs such as missing owner checks, missing signer checks, and missing key checks, as well as arbitrary cross-program invocations. Our evaluation of 8,714 bytecode-only contracts shows that our approach outperforms existing approaches and identifies potential bugs in 467 different contracts. Additionally, we analyzed 120 open-source Solana projects and conducted in-depth case studies on four of them. Our findings reveal that subtle, easily overlooked issues often serve as the root cause of severe exploits, further highlighting the need for specialized analysis tools like SseRex.

</details>


### [151] [Impact of File-Open Hook Points on Backup Ratio in ROFBS on XFS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16364)
*Kosuke Higuchi,Ryotaro Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究比较了Linux文件打开路径上不同钩子点对ROFBS防御勒索软件效果的影响，发现钩子点选择显著影响文件恢复率和加密规模，其中xfs_file_open在XFS文件系统上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件在攻击开始到检测之间的延迟期间持续加密文件，ROFBS通过在文件打开事件时实时备份修改前文件来缓解此问题。但Linux文件打开路径涉及多个内核函数，不清楚钩子点选择如何影响防御效果。

Method: 保持ROFBS机制不变，仅改变Linux文件打开路径上的钩子点：may_open、inode_permission、do_dentry_open、security_file_open和xfs_file_open。在AlmaLinux+XFS系统上测试三种勒索软件家族：AvosLocker、Conti和IceFire。主要使用备份比率作为指标，同时比较有备份的加密文件数量和总加密文件数量。

Result: 钩子点选择显著影响恢复能力和损害规模。对于AvosLocker，security_file_open获得最高备份比率（82.5%）。对于Conti和IceFire，xfs_file_open获得最高值（分别为100.0%和63.2%）。此外，xfs_file_open对所有三种勒索软件家族都最小化了总加密文件数量。

Conclusion: 在ROFBS中，观察文件打开事件的层级是关键设计因素。特别是在XFS文件系统上，当目标是减少总体损害时，钩住文件系统特定回调xfs_file_open可能是有利的。

Abstract: Ransomware continues encrypting files during the delay between attack onset and detection. ROFBS mitigates this problem by backing up pre-modification files in real time upon file-open events. However, because the Linux file-open path traverses multiple kernel functions, it remains unclear how the choice of hook point affects defense effectiveness.
  In this study, we kept the ROFBS mechanism fixed and changed only the hook points on the Linux file-open path. We compared may_open, inode_permission, do_dentry_open, security_file_open, and xfs_file_open on AlmaLinux with XFS using three ransomware families: AvosLocker, Conti, and IceFire. We used Backup Ratio as the main metric and also compared the number of encrypted files with backups and the total number of encrypted files.
  The results showed that hook-point selection substantially affected both recoverability and damage scale. For AvosLocker, security_file_open achieved the highest Backup Ratio (82.5%). For Conti and IceFire, xfs_file_open achieved the highest values (100.0% and 63.2%, respectively). Moreover, xfs_file_open minimized the total number of encrypted files for all three ransomware families.
  These results indicate that, in ROFBS, the layer at which file-open events are observed is a key design factor. In particular, on XFS, hooking the filesystem-specific callback xfs_file_open may be advantageous when the goal is to reduce overall damage.

</details>


### [152] [Rotated Robustness: A Training-Free Defense against Bit-Flip Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16382)
*Deng Liu,Song Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RoR是一种无需训练的防御方法，通过正交旋转激活空间来平滑极端异常值，打破异常值与易受攻击权重之间的对齐，从而保护量化LLM免受硬件位翻转故障的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化权重中的硬件位翻转故障对大型语言模型构成严重可靠性威胁，常导致灾难性模型崩溃。这种脆弱性源于敏感权重位与极端激活异常值之间的空间对齐，使得单个硬件故障被大规模放大。

Method: 提出Rotated Robustness (RoR)，一种基于正交Householder变换的训练免费防御方法。通过对激活空间应用正交旋转，RoR在几何上平滑所有特征维度上的极端异常值，打破异常值与易受攻击权重之间的对齐，同时数学保证原始模型精度。

Result: 在Llama-2/3、OPT和Qwen系列模型上的广泛实验表明：随机位翻转攻击下，RoR将Qwen2.5-7B的随机崩溃率从3.15%降至0.00%；在50个渐进位搜索翻转的严重定向攻击下，RoR在Llama-2-7B上保持43.9%的MMLU准确率（接近未受攻击的45.2%），而竞争防御方法崩溃至随机猜测；针对最激进的单点故障攻击，RoR将攻击复杂度从几个位翻转指数级增加至超过17,000个精确位翻转。

Conclusion: RoR以0.31%的存储开销和9.1%的推理延迟增加，实现了真正的无损鲁棒性，为LLM部署提供了实用且高度可靠的防御方案。

Abstract: Hardware faults, specifically bit-flips in quantized weights, pose a severe reliability threat to Large Language Models (LLMs), often triggering catastrophic model collapses. We demonstrate that this vulnerability fundamentally stems from the spatial alignment between sensitive weight bits and extreme activation outliers, which causes a single hardware fault to be massively amplified. To address this, we propose Rotated Robustness (RoR), a training-free defense utilizing orthogonal Householder transformations. By applying an orthogonal rotation to the activation space, RoR geometrically smooths extreme outliers across all feature dimensions. This mechanism effectively breaks the alignment between outliers and vulnerable weights, mathematically guaranteeing original model accuracy. Extensive empirical evaluations across Llama-2/3, OPT, and Qwen families demonstrate the superior reliability of our approach. Under random bit-flip attacks, RoR reduces the stochastic collapse rate from 3.15\% to 0.00\% on Qwen2.5-7B. Furthermore, under severe targeted attacks with 50 Progressive Bit Search flips, RoR sustains robust reasoning on Llama-2-7B, maintaining a 43.9\% MMLU accuracy that nearly matches its 45.2\% unattacked accuracy, while competing defenses collapse to random guessing. Most notably, against the Single-Point Fault Attack (SPFA) -- the most aggressive targeted threat -- RoR exponentially inflates the attack complexity from a few bits to over 17,000 precise bit-flips. With a negligible storage overhead of 0.31\% and a minimal inference latency increase of 9.1\% on Llama-2-7B, RoR achieves true lossless robustness, providing a practical and highly reliable defense for LLM deployment.

</details>


### [153] [SAMSEM -- A Generic and Scalable Approach for IC Metal Line Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16548)
*Christian Gehrmann,Jonas Ricker,Simon Damm,Deruo Cheng,Julian Speith,Yiqiong Shi,Asja Fischer,Christof Paar*

Main category: cs.CR
